This paper solves the problem by automatically detecting and or predicting mounds on Mars using Neural Network based Semantic Segmentation methodologies. This is done by using supervised semantic segmentation model and generative adversarial approach. A comparison of the approaches shows that adding extra artificially generated data did not improve the result.

</details>


### [288] [Different Layers, Different Manifolds: Module-Wise Weight-Space Geometry in Transformer Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13276)
*Kirato Yoshihara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer中不同模块对权重空间几何约束的偏好差异，发现对注意力层使用Stiefel流形、对MLP层使用DGram流形效果最佳，反之则不稳定，原因是DGram约束下注意力权重的奇异值增长会导致softmax饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往神经网络优化中对权重矩阵的流形约束通常是统一的，但不同Transformer模块（注意力层与MLP层）可能具有不同的几何偏好，本文旨在探究这一差异。

Method: 在GPT-2预训练中使用Manifold Muon优化器，对比Stiefel流形和DGram流形在注意力层和MLP层上的不同分配组合（共四种配置）。

Result: 注意力层用Stiefel、MLP层用DGram组合性能最佳；反向分配以及全DGram配置在共享超参数下不稳定，原因是DGram约束的注意力权重奇异值增长导致注意力logits放大和softmax饱和。

Conclusion: Transformer的对称感知和几何感知优化应当根据模块类型差异化设计，而非统一施加相同的流形约束。

Abstract: Weight-space geometry plays a central role in neural network optimization, yet manifold constraints are often applied uniformly across all weight matrices. In this work, we ask whether different transformer modules prefer different manifold geometries. We study Manifold Muon for GPT-2 pretraining and compare layer-wise assignments of Stiefel and DGram constraints across attention and MLP blocks. Our results show a clear asymmetry: constraining attention layers with Stiefel geometry while assigning DGram geometry to MLP layers gives the best performance among the tested configurations, whereas the inverted assignment and all-DGram configuration become unstable under the shared hyperparameter setting. We trace this failure to singular value growth in DGram-constrained attention weights, which can amplify attention logits and induce softmax saturation. These findings suggest that symmetry-aware and geometry-aware optimization for transformers should be module-specific rather than uniform.

</details>


### [289] [Once-for-All: Scalable Simultaneous Forecasting via Equilibrium State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13285)
*Beinan Xu,Andy Song,Jiti Gao,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为均衡状态估计（ESE）的新范式，用于多系统同步预测，通过估计系统间的均衡状态并基于当前状态与均衡状态的差异生成整体预测，在保持与SOTA方法相当精度的同时实现10-70倍加速，具有线性时间复杂度，且具备良好的泛化性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预测方法通常一次只预测一个系统，而在经济学和医疗建模等现实场景中，多个相互作用的系统需要同时进行协调预测。现有方法无法高效处理这类多系统同步预测任务。

Method: ESE首先估计多个系统之间的均衡状态，然后基于当前状态与所估计均衡状态之间的差异，一次性生成所有系统的整体预测。该方法具有线性时间复杂度，并可无缝集成常规预测器。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集（包括汇率和COVID-19传播建模）上的实验表明，ESE在精度上与SOTA方法相当，但速度显著更快，实现了10-70倍的加速。随着系统数量增加，ESE的线性时间复杂度使其扩展性优于SOTA方法，且在多种扰动下保持准确性。

Conclusion: ESE是一种快速、可泛化、鲁棒且可扩展的多系统同步预测方法，为多系统协同预测提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: We introduce Equilibrium State Estimation (ESE), a novel paradigm for simultaneous prediction, where multiple interacting systems require separate yet coordinated forecasts. Such scenarios often arise in real-world settings such as economics and healthcare modeling. Unlike existing approaches that predict one system at a time, ESE forecasts all systems in a single pass. It first estimates the equilibrium state across systems, then generates holistic forecasts based on the difference between the current state and the estimated equilibrium. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets, including currency exchange and COVID-19 spread modeling, demonstrate that ESE is at least as accurate as state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods while being significantly faster. In addition, ESE integrates seamlessly with conventional predictors, combining their accuracy with its exceptional efficiency and delivering a 10-70x speedup. With linear-time complexity, ESE scales far better than SOTA methods as the number of systems increases. Moreover, it remains accurate under diverse perturbations, establishing ESE as a fast, generalizable, robust, and scalable multi-prediction method.

</details>


### [290] [Clipping Makes Distributed and Federated Asynchronous SGD Robust to Stragglers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13287)
*Samuel Erickson,Mikael Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明了梯度裁剪（gradient clipping）能够消除异步随机梯度下降（ASGD）中最大延迟对Oracle复杂度的影响，并在次Weibull梯度噪声模型下证明了期望收敛和高概率收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步训练中，慢节点导致更新延迟较大，影响收敛；而实践中发现梯度裁剪能"稳定"训练，但缺乏理论解释。本文旨在为这一现象提供理论依据。

Method: 采用次Weibull（sub-Weibull）模型刻画梯度噪声，该模型涵盖次高斯、次指数到更重尾的分布；在此模型下分析带梯度裁剪的ASGD的收敛性，包括期望收敛和高概率收敛。

Result: 证明梯度裁剪可以消除最大延迟在Oracle复杂度中的依赖关系；首次在异步优化中同时得到期望收敛和高概率收敛结果。

Conclusion: 梯度裁剪在异步训练中不仅能稳定训练，而且理论上能消除延迟对收敛速度的负面依赖，为异步训练中梯度裁剪的有效性提供了严格的理论支持。

Abstract: In modern machine learning, parallelization of training is an important strategy for increasing scale. Asynchronous stochastic gradient descent (ASGD), which maximizes the utilization of available hardware by avoiding waiting for slow workers. However, with constant step sizes, the convergence of ASGD is nonetheless affected negatively by slow workers due to large delays in updates. At the same time, it has been empirically observed in asynchronous training of deep learning models that gradient clipping "stabilizes" training. In this work, we provide a theoretical justification for this behavior, as we show that clipping removes the dependence of the maximum delay in the oracle complexity. We employ a sub-Weibull model of gradient noise which generalizes sub-Gaussian and sub-exponential distributions to more heavy-tailed distributions, motivated by empirical observations in deep learning. We show convergence in expectation, and the first time in asynchronous optimization, convergence with high probability.

</details>


### [291] [Quantizing Time-Series Models As Dynamical Systems: Trajectory-Based Quantization Sensitivity Score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13300)
*Mariya Pavlova,Harrison Bo Hua Zhu,Elizsveta Semenova,Yingzhen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于轨迹的量化敏感度分数（TQS），从动力系统稳定性视角重构后训练量化（PTQ），实现与量化过程解耦的先验敏感度估计，并构建无需标定数据的混合精度框架TQS-PTQ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PTQ方法的敏感度分析与量化过程耦合，无法在量化器选择和位宽分配之前进行预算规划，且依赖标定数据或高阶近似，限制了其在黑箱或编译网络中的应用。

Method: 将网络 rollout 建模为离散时间动力系统，定义TQS指标刻画量化误差在 rollout 时域上的传播与放大；基于TQS构建无需标定数据和二阶近似的混合精度量化框架TQS-PTQ。

Result: 实验表明动力系统视角为低精度部署提供了鲁棒且高性能的路径。

Conclusion: TQS实现了与量化过程解耦的先验敏感度估计，TQS-PTQ框架在资源受限场景下无需标定数据即可高效完成混合精度量化。

Abstract: We introduce the Trajectory-based Quantization Sensitivity Score (TQS), a metric that reframes post-training quantization (PTQ) through the lens of dynamical-systems stability. By modeling the network's rollout as a discrete-time dynamical system, TQS characterizes how quantization-induced errors propagate and amplify over the rollout horizon. Unlike conventional PTQ methods, where sensitivity analysis is often coupled to the quantization procedure, TQS enables a priori sensitivity estimation decoupled from quantizer selection and bit-width assignment. This separation allows for quantization budget planning even for black-box or compiled networks with fused operators. Building on this, we present TQS-PTQ, a flexible mixed-precision framework that requires no calibration data or costly second-order approximations. Our experiments show that a dynamical-systems perspective provides a robust, high-performing pathway for low-precision deployment in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [292] [Rarity-Gated Context Conditioning for Offline Imitation Learning-Based Maritime Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13311)
*Yongmin Kim,ByeongHoon Jeon,Sungil Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RGFiLM模块，通过上下文稀有性感知的门控机制调节特征调制强度，在高度不平衡的上下文分布下减少异常检测中的误报，并在船只轨迹异常检测任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中上下文分布高度不平衡，稀有语境下上下文条件化模型容易产生不稳定决策和过多误报，现有方法未显式考虑上下文稀有性对异常检测的影响。

Method: 提出Rarity-Gated Feature-wise Linear Modulation (RGFiLM)，结合逐特征线性调制（上下文条件化的缩放和平移）与基于数据驱动稀有性得分的门控机制，稀有性得分从上下文变量的经验分布估计，门控在稀有语境下更果断、在常见语境下更保守。

Result: 在海上船只轨迹异常检测任务（AIS运动序列+ERA5环境上下文）中，RGFiLM在平均F1与假正率（FPR）权衡上优于所有对比的上下文无关和上下文条件化方法。

Conclusion: 显式考虑上下文稀有性是减少上下文敏感异常检测中误报的有效方法。

Abstract: Contextual anomaly detection aims to identify abnormal behavior conditional on context variables, but practical deployments often face highly imbalanced context distributions where rare regimes can be critical information. Under such frequency bias, context-conditioned models can produce unstable decisions and excessive false alarms in rare contexts. We propose Rarity-Gated Feature-wise Linear Modulation (RGFiLM), a rarity-aware conditioning module that combines feature-wise modulation (i.e., context-conditioned scaling and shifting of hidden features) with a gate controlled by a data-driven rarity score. The rarity score is estimated from the empirical distribution of context variables and regulates how strongly context modulates intermediate representations: the gate becomes more decisive under rare contexts while remaining conservative under frequent contexts. We evaluate RGFiLM on maritime trajectory anomaly detection using AIS motion sequences with ERA5 environmental context in an environment-sensitive detour scenario. When instantiated in a sequential anomaly scoring pipeline, RGFiLM achieves the best mean F1--False Positive Rate (FPR) trade-off among the compared context-agnostic and context-conditioned methods. These results suggest that explicitly accounting for context rarity is an effective approach for reducing false alarms in context-sensitive anomaly detection.

</details>


### [293] [Navigating the Safety-Fidelity Trade-off: Massive-Variate Time Series Forecasting for Power Systems via Probabilistic Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13338)
*Kaijie Xu,Anqi Wang,Xilin Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PowerPhase（大规模概率预测基准，涵盖2,000-36,964通道的电力系统）和PowerForge（场景式分位数预测器），发现分布精度与约束满足之间存在安全-保真度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率预测基准在通道规模和约束评估方面存在不足：典型多变量基准最多2000通道，电力系统基准缺乏时序结构或概率评估。

Method: 基于六个交流潮流求解的输电网构建PowerPhase基准，引入约束感知指标（Safety_mBrier、NECV、CVaR-α）；提出PowerForge模型，包含类型特定解码头和变量组间因果桥。

Result: 在8个基线和3个种子下，分布精度与约束满足排序不同（安全-保真度权衡）；PowerForge在每个网格上均取得最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: 大规模电力系统概率预测中需同时关注分布精度和约束满足，PowerForge在两者间实现了最优平衡。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting models are increasingly deployed on multivariate systems with distinct channel physics and operational constraints, but existing benchmarks evaluate neither property at scale. Public canonical multivariate benchmarks cap out at 2,000 channels, while power-system benchmarks either lack temporal structure or probabilistic evaluation. We introduce PowerPhase, a probabilistic forecasting benchmark built on six transmission grids ranging from 2,000 to 36,964 jointly forecasted channels, more than an order of magnitude beyond popular canonical multivariate benchmarks. Each target trajectory is the output of an AC power-flow solve, and PowerPhase ships with constraint-aware metrics, including Safety_mBrier, NECV, and CVaR-alpha, that complement CRPS and Distortion. Across eight baselines and three seeds, distributional accuracy and constraint satisfaction rank models differently, a trade-off we term safety-fidelity. We further propose PowerForge, a scenario-based quantile forecaster with type-specific decoding heads and a causal bridge between variable groups, which achieves the best average rank on every grid.

</details>


### [294] [Enhanced Low-Density Region Exploration in Classifier-Guided Diffusion Models Through Modified Reverse Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13347)
*Jagriti Singh,Shekhar Verma,Muneendra Ojha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种纯采样阶段的密度感知条件扩散模型扩展方法，通过在逆向过程中使用修改后的分类器梯度引导轨迹走向低置信区域，同时引导采样过程接近预测的真实图像，在不需额外训练的情况下改善长尾分布样本的覆盖率和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分类器引导会集中概率质量于高密度类别均值附近，导致对类别条件分布尾部中的稀有样本覆盖不足。现有方法要么需要额外训练低密度分类器，要么虽能加速或优化扩散采样但不显式处理长尾覆盖问题。

Method: 1) 基于预训练的条件扩散模型和分类器，修改引导逆向动力学，通过分类器梯度将轨迹导向低置信区域（低密度探索）；2) 在每个时间步引导采样过程朝向预测的真实图像，使生成样本接近真实数据流形；3) 直接从含噪图像上施加引导，而非像多数扩散模型那样对预测噪声施加引导。

Result: 所提采样器在64x64分辨率下持续提升ADM模型的召回率，同时保持可比的FID；在256x256分辨率下，展示了不同引导组合的图像质量效果；标准ADM分类器引导结合预测真实图像引导可生成高感知质量的样本。

Conclusion: 纯采样阶段的方法无需额外训练即可改善长尾覆盖，双引导策略（低置信区域引导+预测真实图像引导）在保持生成质量的同时有效提升了稀有样本的探索能力。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as state-of-the-art generative models for high-fidelity image synthesis, particularly in their classifier-free guided and classifier-guided forms. However, standard classifier guidance concentrates probability mass around high-density class mean, leading to poor coverage of rare samples in the tails of the class-conditional distributions. Recent work on diffusion-based tail sampling mitigates this by training an additional low-density-seeking classifier with a synthetic-vs-real discriminator, at the cost of additional networks and training. In parallel, a number of samplers and distillation techniques accelerate or refine diffusion sampling, but do not explicitly address long-tail coverage. We propose a purely sampling-time, density-aware extension of classifier-guided conditional diffusion model that targets low-density regions without any additional training. We have applied guidance at noisy images not on predicted noise like most diffusion models. Starting from a pretrained conditional diffusion model and classifier on ImageNet, we modify the guided reverse dynamics by steering trajectories toward low-confidence regions via the modified classifier gradient, and at each time step, we also guide the sampling process toward the predicted real image. 1st guidance helps explore low-probability samples, and 2nd guidance helps to generate samples to be close to the real data manifold. The proposed sampler consistently improves ADM model recall at 64x64 resolution while maintaining a comparable FID, and with a 256x256 ADM model, we showed the results visually with different combinations of both guidance. We also showed that standard ADM classifier guidance, combined with predicted real image guidance, helps generate high perceptual quality samples with a 256x256 ADM model on ImageNet.

</details>


### [295] [Positional Encoding in the Context of Memristor-Based Analog Computation for Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13379)
*Benedikt Hilmes,Nick Rossenbach,Ralf Schlüter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对忆阻器计算中位置编码输出值过大导致模数转换（ADC）性能退化的问题，提出了两种优化策略：调整特定层的权重与ADC精度比特比例（退化减少约50%，能耗稳定），以及在ADC不可修改时移除编码相关的线性变换（退化减少约30%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 忆阻器能效高，但计算中存在权重编程和执行失真；位置编码的大输出值在ADC环节造成严重退化，需针对性优化。

Method: 1）调整特定忆阻器层的权重与ADC精度比特比例；2）在ADC不可修改时，移除编码相关的线性变换。

Result: 方法1：执行退化减少约50%，能耗基本稳定；方法2：退化减少约30%。

Conclusion: 通过调整ADC配置或移除特定线性变换，可有效缓解忆阻器计算中位置编码带来的精度退化问题。

Abstract: Memristors provide a new chance for resource-efficient computation of neural models for natural language processing by enabling analog execution of vector-matrix-multiplication. Yet, computations on these devices are currently subject to larger distortion, both in weight programming and execution. In this work, we identify large output values of transformed positional encodings to cause major degradation within analog-to-digital conversion (ADC) as part of memristor-based computation. By adjusting the proportion of weight and precision bits of the ADC of specific memristor layers, we reduce the degradation of the execution by ~50% relative, while keeping the estimated energy consumption stable. Additionally, we investigate scenarios where the ADC cannot be modified. In that case the degradation can be reduced by ~30% relative after removing encoding-related linear transformations.

</details>


### [296] [Hölder++: Improving the Quality-Coherence Trade-off in Multimodal VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13381)
*Huyen Vo,María Martínez-García,Isabel Valera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Hölder++，一种改进的多模态变分自编码器，通过无近似的Hölder池化、共享与私有表示分离以及层次化推理，有效提升了生成质量与跨模态一致性的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMVAE方法在生成质量与跨模态语义一致性之间存在权衡，难以同时生成真实多样且语义一致的样本。近期工作发现近似Hölder池化可提升一致性但牺牲了多样性，受此启发，旨在进一步优化这一权衡。

Method: (i) 首次在多模态VAE中实现无近似的Hölder池化；(ii) 扩展架构建模独立的共享表示和私有（模态特有）表示（Hölder+）；(iii) 引入层次化推理进一步解耦共享与私有表示（Hölder++）。

Result: Hölder++在生成质量-一致性权衡上持续提升，获得更具结构化的潜在空间，且学习到的共享表示对下游任务有信息价值。

Conclusion: Hölder++通过无近似Hölder池化、共享/私有表示分离及层次化推理，有效改善了多模态VAE的生成质量与一致性权衡，并产出更优的潜在表示。

Abstract: Existing approaches for multimodal variational autoencoders (VAEs) face a trade-off between generative quality and coherence-i.e., they struggle to generate realistic and diverse samples that, at the same time, are semantically consistent across modalities. A recent work shows that using a simple approximation to Hölder pooling as an aggregation method improves coherence over the SOTA MMVAE+, despite assuming a single shared representation across all modalities. Yet, it slightly compromises sample diversity. Inspired by this insight, we propose Hölder++, a novel multimodal VAE that improves the generative quality-coherence trade-off through: (i) the first implementation of Hölder pooling without any approximation for multimodal VAEs; (ii) an extended architecture that models distinct shared and private (i.e., modality-specific) representations (Hölder+); and (iii) hierarchical inference that further enhances the disentanglement between the shared and private representations (Hölder++). Our experiments corroborate that Hölder++ consistently improves the generative quality-coherence trade-off, yields more structured latent spaces, and learns shared representations that are informative for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [297] [Accelerating Speculative Diffusions via Block Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13426)
*Alexander Soen,Hisham Husain,Valentin De Bortoli,Arnaud Doucet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于扩散模型的高效投机采样机制，通过引入块验证方法，在不增加训练成本的情况下，实现了比现有方法最高6.3%的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投机解码方法主要针对离散空间的LLM设计，难以直接适配连续空间的扩散模型。已有的扩散模型适配方案要么计算效率低，要么采用了替代方案而非原生的投机采样机制。

Method: 提出了一种能够高效实现扩散模型原始投机采样机制的新方案，该方案最关键的优势在于能够将LLM中的块验证（block verification）适配到扩散模型上，并从理论上证明了块验证能提高草稿的接受率。此外，还形式化分析了无需训练的Free Drafter启发式自投机草稿生成器。

Result: Free Drafter结合块验证方法，在无需额外训练且仅增加可忽略的额外开销的情况下，相比现有投机方法实现了最高6.3%的加速。

Conclusion: 通过将块验证从LLM扩展到扩散模型，本文实现了更高效的投机采样，为扩散模型的推理加速提供了新思路。

Abstract: Speculative decoding speeds up LLM inference by using a draft model to generate tokens, with an acceptance-rejection scheme that ensures that the output matches the target distribution. Adapting this to continuous diffusions is difficult because speculative sampling requires drawing from a residual distribution. While straightforward in discrete spaces, efficiently sampling this residual in continuous space is non-trivial. Consequently, existing diffusion adaptations either use computationally inefficient sampling techniques or rely on an alternative scheme. In this work, we introduce a novel scheme that efficiently implements the original speculative sampling mechanism for diffusion models. Our approach offers a critical advantage over current methods: it enables us to adapt block verification from LLMs to diffusions -- which provably improves the acceptance rate of drafts. Furthermore, we formalize and analyze the Free Drafter, a heuristic self-speculative drafter for diffusions that requires no training. By enabling block verification, our Free Drafter yields up to a 6.3% speedup over existing speculative methods with no additional training and negligible overhead beyond the existing parallel verification pass.

</details>


### [298] [How Much Memory Do We Need? Adaptive Memory Gate for Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13443)
*Jihyeon Hur,Yongseok Kwon,Min-Gi Jo,Jeongwhan Choi,Noseong Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AMGFNO方法，通过可学习门控机制动态调整记忆权重，解决传统记忆增强型神经算子中固定记忆权重无法适应不同观测条件的问题，在低分辨率下实现55-79%的nRMSE降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强型神经算子使用固定记忆权重，无法适应不同分辨率或物理参数（如粘度）的变化，导致性能受限。初步实验表明最优记忆权重随分辨率和粘度变化，固定权重无法同时优化多种场景。

Method: 提出AMGFNO（Adaptive Memory Gated FNO），通过可学习门控机制动态调整记忆权重，使模型能够根据观测条件自适应地调节历史信息的利用程度。

Result: 在Kuramoto-Sivashinsky和Burgers方程上，低分辨率下nRMSE降低55-79%；学习到的门控值随分辨率增加从约0.7自动降至接近零。

Conclusion: 自适应记忆门控机制有效提升了神经算子在多分辨率场景下的适应性和性能，验证了动态调节记忆权重的必要性。

Abstract: Neural operators have emerged as a powerful data-driven approach for solving time-dependent PDEs. Among recent advances, memory-augmented neural operators explicitly incorporate past states and have achieved remarkable performance under low-resolution observation settings. However, existing approaches apply a fixed memory weight regardless of observation conditions, such as resolution or physical parameters, limiting their adaptability. Our preliminary experiments reveal that optimal memory weight varies with resolution and viscosity, implying that a fixed memory weight cannot simultaneously optimize performance across diverse settings. We propose AMGFNO, which dynamically modulates memory weight through a learnable gate. On the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky and Burgers' equations, AMGFNO achieves 55-79% nRMSE reduction over at low resolution, with the learned gate value automatically decreasing from $\bar{g} \approx 0.7$ to near-zero as resolution increases.

</details>


### [299] [Clustering Node Attributed Networks with Graph Neural Networks and Self Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13444)
*Rodrigo de Sapienza Luna,Daniel Ratton Figueiredo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的无监督图聚类框架，通过多轮自学习迭代，利用原始图构建的上下文图生成节点表示并进行聚类，在合成数据和真实数据集上均取得了具有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图聚类仅利用网络边信息，而现实数据中节点属性也包含信息。现有方法虽将两者结合，但如何有效融合边信息与节点属性、并在完全无监督场景下提升聚类性能仍具挑战。

Method: 提出多轮自学习框架：每轮使用GNN生成节点表示并进行聚类，聚类结果影响下一轮用于生成节点表示的图结构；同时每轮基于原始图构建上下文图来辅助节点表示学习。整个过程在完全无监督设置下进行。

Result: 合成数据上，该方法在边信息或属性信息均不充分时优于仅利用单一信息源的方法；多轮学习始终优于单轮长训练；真实数据集上，当簇大小平衡时，该方法与现有最优方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效提取网络边和节点属性中的信息，多轮迭代自学习机制优于传统单轮GNN聚类，在簇平衡的真实场景中达到state-of-the-art水平。

Abstract: Graph clustering - partitioning the node set of a graph into disjoint subsets that reflect some latent information - is a fundamental problem as it finds applications in a myriad of different scenarios. While this classic problem has been tackled for decades by different communities, a recent variation of the problem driven by real data considers the scenario where nodes have attributes that are also informative. This has triggered novel methods that simultaneously leverage network information (edges) and node information (attributed) in the design of novel clustering algorithms. This work proposes a novel framework that builds on prior works that have applied graph neural networks (GNN) to graph clustering. The proposed framework operates in rounds of self learning in a fully unsupervised setting. In each round, a GNN generates representations for nodes that are used to cluster the nodes. This clustering influences the graph used to generate the node representation in the next round. Moreover, a context graph built in each round using the original graph is used to generate the node representations. Empirical results show that the proposed methodology extracts information from both network edges and node attributes in synthetic data, outperforming algorithms focused solely on the network or attributes when neither are very informative. Multiple rounds of learning also improve the performance and always outperforms a long single round of training (i.e., classic GNN graph clustering). When considering real datasets, empirical results indicate that the proposed methodology is competitive to state-of-the-art methods when cluster sizes are balanced.

</details>


### [300] [Uncertainty Estimation for Molecular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13451)
*Paul Seij,Christian A. Naesseth,Stephan Mandt,Metod Jazbec*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于拉普拉斯近似的后验不确定性估计方法，用于预训练分子扩散模型生成的3D分子质量评估，通过噪声预测变异性实现样本过滤和测试时性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在3D分子生成中被广泛采用，但缺乏生成分子质量低劣时的原则性信号，需要一种能估计生成样本不确定性的方法。

Method: 基于去噪网络的拉普拉斯近似，在生成轨迹上测量噪声预测的变异性，构建每个样本的不确定性分数。

Result: 不确定性分数与样本级质量指标呈负相关，能有效指示样本质量；通过过滤低质量样本可提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 所提后验不确定性估计方法为预训练分子扩散模型提供了样本质量判别的原则性信号，并可通过测试时缩放策略改进生成结果。

Abstract: Diffusion models have seen wide adoption for 3D molecular generation, yet they offer no principled signal of when a generated molecule is likely to be of low quality. We propose a post-hoc method for estimating per-sample uncertainty in pretrained molecular diffusion models. Building on a Laplace approximation of the denoising network, we measure the variability of the noise prediction across the generation trajectory. Empirically, we show that the resulting uncertainty score is informative of sample quality, exhibiting a negative correlation with established sample-level quality metrics. We further study how the proposed uncertainty score can be used to filter generated samples, improving model performance via test-time scaling.

</details>


### [301] [MaxProof: Scaling Mathematical Proof with Generative-Verifier RL and Population-Level Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13473)
*Jiacheng Chen,Xinyu Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Yanmohan Wang,Lin Li,Tiancheng Qin,Qin Wang,Zhengmao Zhu,Tianle Li,Jingyang Li,Zehan Li,Binyang Jiang,Jin Zhu,Han Ding,Fei Yu,Chenyu Du,Zijian Song,Jiayuan Song,Zhi Zhang,Yunan Huang,Weiyu Cheng,Pengyu Zhao,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MaxProof 是一个面向竞赛级数学证明的群体级测试时扩展框架，通过将 M3 模型作为生成器、验证器、精炼器和排序器，利用群体搜索和锦标赛选择来提升证明性能，在 IMO 2025 和 USAMO 2026 上均超过人类金牌线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在竞赛级数学证明任务中表现不足，缺乏一种能够在测试时通过扩展计算资源来系统提升证明质量的有效框架。

Method: M3 模型首先训练三种面向证明的能力（证明生成、证明验证、基于批评的证明修复），采用低假阳性率的纵深防御生成式验证器。测试时，MaxProof 将模型作为生成器、验证器、精炼器和排序器，对候选证明群体进行搜索，并通过锦标赛选择返回最终证明。

Result: 在 IMO 2025 上达到 35/42，在 USAMO 2026 上达到 36/42，均超过人类金牌线。

Conclusion: MaxProof 证明测试时扩展能够显著提升竞赛级数学证明性能，达到甚至超越人类顶尖水平。

Abstract: We present MaxProof, a population-level test-time scaling framework for competition-level mathematical proof in the MiniMax-M3 series. M3 first trains three proof-oriented capabilities -- proof generation, proof verification, and critique-conditioned proof repair -- using a defense-in-depth generative verifier engineered for low false-positive rate. These capabilities are merged into a single released M3 model. At test time, MaxProof treats the model as a generator, verifier, refiner, and ranker, searches over a population of candidate proofs, and returns one final proof through tournament selection. With MaxProof test-time scaling, the M3 model reaches 35/42 on IMO 2025 and 36/42 on USAMO 2026, exceeding the human gold-medal threshold on both.

</details>


### [302] [SupraBench: A Benchmark for Supramolecular Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13477)
*Tianyi Ma,Yijun Ma,Zehong Wang,Weixiang Sun,Ziming Li,Connor R. Schmidt,Chuxu Zhang,Matthew J. Webber,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次发布了用于评估大语言模型在超分子化学推理能力的基准测试SupraBench，包含四个核心任务（结合亲和力预测、最佳结合剂选择、溶剂识别、主客体描述）和一个辅助视觉任务，并配套发布了包含1600万token的领域语料库SupraPMC。实验表明现有LLMs在所有任务上仍有较大提升空间，领域自适应预训练在分布内回归任务上表现良好但与严格的格式输出存在权衡，不同任务族展现出不同的错误模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前设计主客体系统耗时且需要大量干实验验证，虽然LLMs在分子结合任务上展现出快速替代潜力，但缺乏系统评估LLMs在超分子化学基础任务（如结合亲和力预测）上推理能力的基准测试。

Method: 与领域专家合作设计SupraBench基准测试，包含四个核心任务（结合亲和力预测、最佳结合剂选择、溶剂识别、主客体描述）和一个基于视觉的辅助分子识别任务；同时从Europe PMC中筛选超分子化学文章构建1600万token的SupraPMC语料库；对多种开源和闭源LLM进行基准测试。

Result: 现有LLMs在所有任务上表现不足，存在较大改进空间；领域自适应预训练在分布内回归任务上有效但会与严格的格式输出要求产生权衡；不同任务族展现出不同的难度分布和失败模式，揭示了当前超分子化学推理中的特定缺陷。

Conclusion: 该工作首次建立了超分子化学领域的LLM评估基准，系统揭示了当前模型在该领域推理能力的局限性，为后续研究方向提供了明确指引。

Abstract: Supramolecular chemistry, which includes the study of non-covalent host-guest assemblies, has advanced various applications. However, designing host-guest systems remains time-consuming, requiring days of dry-lab verification per candidate pair. Although LLMs have emerged as a fast alternative with strong performance on molecular binding tasks, no benchmark currently systematically evaluates LLMs for host-guest reasoning across fundamental supramolecular chemistry tasks, e.g., binding affinity prediction. To this end, we collaborate with domain experts to release the first Supramolecular Benchmark, called SupraBench, to evaluate LLMs in chemistry reasoning. Specifically, we design four fundamental tasks, i.e., binding affinity prediction, top-binder selection, solvent identification, and host-guest description, plus an auxiliary vision-based task for molecular identification. We also release SupraPMC, a curated 16M-token corpus of Supramolecular chemistry articles distilled from Europe PMC, to support the adaptation to the supramolecular domain. We benchmark a broad range of open and proprietary LLMs and find that LLMs leave substantial headroom across all tasks. Domain adaptation pretraining over SupraPMC transfers cleanly to in-distribution regression but trades off against strict letter-format output. Moreover, the difficulty profile differs sharply across task families, revealing distinct failure modes that indicate specific gaps in current supramolecular chemistry reasoning. Our source codes and benchmark datasets are available at https://github.com/Tianyi-Billy-Ma/SupraBench.

</details>


### [303] [CRAFTIIF: Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13486)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFTIIF 是一种全无监督的多元时间序列异常检测框架，通过跨分辨率解析小波特征和五结构孤立森林，同时检测点、分布、时间和集体四类异常，在 mTSBench 19个数据集上取得 SOTA 结果（VUS-PR 0.463，较之前最佳提升 40.7%），并提供了内置的异常类型归因能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督时间序列异常检测方法大多只针对一种或两种异常类型（点、分布偏移、节奏变化、传感器间相关性崩溃），缺乏对全部四种结构异质异常的统一处理能力，且可解释性有限。

Method: 生成 K=500 个跨四种小波族（Morlet、DOG、Haar、Coiflet）的随机解析小波特征，每种小波族针对一种异常类型；构建五个孤立森林（每个异常类型一个 + 一个用于复合异常的元 IF）；采用自适应 Otsu/MAD 阈值自动校准检测；每个 IF 仅基于类型特定特征训练，分支触发直接提供异常类型归因。

Result: 在 mTSBench 全部19个数据集上，平均 F1=0.228（全部19个）/0.322（13个可检测数据集）；VUS-PR 达 0.463，较之前最佳 0.329 提升 40.7%，在25种方法中排名第一；消融实验证实自适应阈值（+38%）、四分支结构（+20%）、元 IF（+23%）均至关重要。

Conclusion: CRAFTIIF 首次在无监督框架下统一处理四类异常，具有内置可解释性且无需数据集特定调参；诊断框架识别出19个数据集中6个对任何无监督方法均不可检测，为领域提供了新的评估基准和归因能力。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is challenged by four structurally distinct anomaly types -- point (isolated spikes), distributional (level shifts), temporal (rhythm changes), and collective (inter-sensor correlation breakdowns) -- each requiring different feature representations. Most unsupervised methods target only one or two types and provide limited interpretability. We present CRAFTIIF (Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest), a fully unsupervised framework targeting all four types without dataset-specific tuning. CRAFTIIF generates K=500 random analytic wavelet feature draws across four families (Morlet, DOG, Haar, Coiflet), each targeting a specific anomaly type, feeding five structured Isolation Forests -- one per type plus a meta-IF for compound anomalies. An adaptive Otsu/MAD threshold calibrates detection automatically across anomaly rates from 0.1% to 69.2%. Because each IF is trained exclusively on type-specific features, branch firing provides direct anomaly-type attribution by construction, without post-hoc explanation. Evaluated on all 19 datasets of the mTSBench benchmark (Zhou et al., TMLR 2026), CRAFTIIF achieves mean F1=0.228 (all 19 datasets) and F1=0.322 (13 detectable datasets), ranking first among all 25 evaluated methods on VUS-PR (0.463 vs. previous best 0.329, +40.7%). A diagnostic framework -- oracle F1, detectability limits, and branch separation ratios -- identifies 6 of 19 datasets as fundamentally undetectable by any unsupervised method. Ablation over 11 conditions confirms adaptive thresholding (+38% F1), four-branch structure (+20%), and meta-IF (+23%) are each essential. Code: https://github.com/smitswil/craftiif

</details>


### [304] [A2D2: Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13565)
*Sophia Tang,Yuchen Zhu,Molei Tao,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出A2D2框架，首次实现了对任意长度离散扩散模型基于奖励引导的微调，通过联合优化插入和去掩码策略以及基于质量的推理调度，实现了理论上有保证的奖励倾斜分布生成，在奖励优化和生成质量上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在序列生成中表现出色，已扩展到任意长度设置（通过token插入），但原则性的奖励引导微调方法在任意长度离散扩散中尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 1) 推导联合插入-去掩码路径测度的Radon-Nikodym导数，实现无需目标样本即可收敛到奖励倾斜分布的理论保证；2) 建立去掩码和插入质量作为最小化解码误差的可处理方法；3) 提出自适应联合解码(AJD)损失，可证明地生成奖励倾斜分布的最优路径测度。

Result: A2D2在奖励优化方面取得改进，同时在生成灵活性和准确性上优于先前的固定长度微调和推理时间引导方法。

Conclusion: A2D2为任意长度离散扩散模型提供了统一且理论上严谨的奖励引导微调框架，有效结合了插入与去掩码策略的联合优化。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models offer a simple and stable likelihood-based framework for sequence generation, recently extended to any-length settings via token insertion. Principled reward-guided fine-tuning for any-length discrete diffusion, however, remains largely unexplored. We introduce Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding (A2D2), a unified framework for reward-guided fine-tuning of any-length discrete diffusion models via joint optimization of the insertion and unmasking policies together with a quality-based inference schedule. We derive the Radon-Nikodym derivative for the joint insertion-unmasking path measures, enabling theoretically guaranteed convergence to the intractable reward-tilted sequence distribution without requiring target samples. Building on this, we establish unmasking and insertion quality as tractable approaches for minimizing decoding error and introduce the Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) loss, which provably yields the optimal path measure that generates the reward-tilted distribution. Empirically, A2D2 improves reward optimization while enhancing generation flexibility and accuracy over prior fixed-length fine-tuning and inference-time guidance methods.

</details>


### [305] [Adjusted Cup-Product Neural Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13568)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种调整杯积神经层（adjusted cup product neural layer），该层通过硬编码杯积与高规范理论中的调整项，实现了设计上的规范不变性。理论结果表明，在闭合循环上输出完全依赖于调整系数，该系数是规范不变信号的唯一来源，且该可观测量为非零二次型，在单次和两次规范变换下严格不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理与几何中许多重要的可观测量是上链的杯积，现有方法无法保证规范不变性，因此需要一种能够天然保持规范不变性的神经层设计。

Method: 提出了调整杯积神经层，将杯积与高规范理论中的调整项硬编码为神经原语，使得读出结果在设计上即满足规范不变性。

Result: 理论上证明了在闭合循环上，输出完全由调整系数决定；调整系数为零时输出完全消失；该可观测量为非零二次型，且在单次和两次规范变换下严格不变。

Conclusion: 调整项是唯一能产生规范不变信号的来源，为物理与几何可观测量的神经网络建模提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Many important observables in physics and geometry are cup products of cochains. The adjusted cup product neural layer has been introduced in this paper. It is a neural primitive that hard wires the cup product with an adjustment term from higher gauge theory. This creates a readout that is gauge invariant by design. Their main theoretical result shows that on a closed cycle the output relies entirely on the adjustment coefficient. Setting this coefficient to zero removes the output completely regardless of other parameters. Thus the adjustment is the only source of gauge invariant signal. They prove this observable is a nonzero quadratic form and is exactly invariant under one and two gauge transformations.

</details>


### [306] [Existence Precedes Value: Joint Modeling of Observational Existence and Evolving States in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13571)
*Yifan Hu,Hongzhou Chen,Peiyuan Liu,Yiding Liu,Zewei Dong,Jiang-Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Timeflies是一个统一框架，将时间序列预测重构为未来可观测性推断与值估计的联合问题，通过双流架构（观测流和值流）及三个专用模块显式建模观测动态与状态演化的交互，并在新构建的Shadow基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测方法（如时序插补后预测、Neural ODE、连续时间图网络）虽能处理历史不完整性，但均隐式假设推理时未来有效观测的时间戳已知，这限制了实际应用价值，因为真实系统中不仅需要预测未来值，还需判断未来是否会产生有效观测。

Method: 提出Timeflies框架，采用双流架构（观测流+值流），通过三个耦合模块实现：(1) 可靠性感知嵌入模块，(2) 观测引导的依赖建模模块，(3) 联合预测模块。同时构建Shadow基准数据集并引入观测-值联合熵(OVJE)评估指标。

Result: 大量实验表明Timeflies在各类缺失值场景下一致优于现有方法，证明了显式建模未来可观测性对时间序列预测的重要性。

Conclusion: 未来可观测性推断与值估计应作为联合问题处理，显式建模观测动态与状态演化之间的交互能显著提升不完整时间序列的预测性能。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often highly incomplete and irregular due to sensor dormancy, transmission delays, and event-driven sampling, making reliable forecasting fundamentally challenging. Existing methods have evolved from impute-then-forecast pipelines to continuous-time models such as Neural ODEs and continuous-time graph networks. While these approaches improve the modeling of historical irregularity, they still rely on an implicit oracle assumption at inference time: the timestamps of future valid observations are presumed to be known in advance. This assumption limits practical relevance, since in many real systems the more fundamental question is not only what the future value will be, but also whether a valid observation will occur at all. In this paper, we propose Timeflies, a unified framework that reformulates forecasting as a joint problem of future observability inference and value estimation. To explicitly model the interaction between observation dynamics and state evolution, Timeflies adopts an observation stream and a value stream, coupled through three dedicated modules for reliability-aware embedding, observation-guided dependency modeling, and joint prediction. We further construct Shadow, a benchmark that combines natural missingness from public datasets with real-world industrial data, and introduce the Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE) metric to comprehensively evaluate this coupled predictability. Extensive experiments show that Timeflies consistently outperforms existing methods, highlighting the importance of explicitly modeling future observability in time series forecasting with missing values. Code and dataset are available in https://github.com/ant-intl/Timeflies.

</details>


### [307] [Learning with Simulators: No Regret in a Computationally Bounded World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13576)
*Sasha Voitovych,Abhishek Shetty,Noah Golowich,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可模拟过程"框架，证明在拥有数据生成过程的模拟器（即使数据高度依赖）时，可恢复与独立数据相同的学习保证（基于VC维的误差界），并展示条件采样的统计与计算优势。特别地，提出一个统一算法，能在所有有界多项式时间可采样的过程中同时学习任意VC类，其遗憾由过程的时间有界Kolmogorov复杂度控制，显著扩展了经典PAC模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典学习理论严重依赖数据独立性假设，而对强依赖数据的学习结果非常有限。本文旨在填补这一空白，探讨在最弱假设下——数据生成过程可任意复杂和依赖——能否恢复与独立数据相同的学习保证。

Method: 引入"可模拟过程"框架，假设学习者可访问一个近似真实数据分布的模拟器。基于此框架，推导出VC维依赖的误差界；进一步利用条件采样来展示统计和计算优势。核心成果是提出一个统一算法，该算法同时学习所有在有界多项式时间内可采样的过程所对应的任意VC类。

Result: 1）在可模拟过程框架下，即使数据高度依赖，也能获得与经典独立数据设定相同的基于VC维的误差界；2）条件采样在该设定下具有统计和计算上的严格优势；3）提出单一算法，能在所有有界多项式时间可采样过程上同时学习任意VC类，遗憾由过程的时间有界Kolmogorov复杂度控制。

Conclusion: 可模拟过程框架显著拓宽了经典PAC学习模型的适用范围，揭示了在强依赖数据下仍可获得独立数据级别的学习保证，为处理复杂依赖数据的学习问题提供了全新理论路径。

Abstract: Understanding the minimal assumptions necessary for generalization is the fundamental question in learning theory. Unfortunately, most results rely heavily on independence (or some proxy thereof) of the data-generating process, while results for strongly dependent data are far more limited. Towards addressing this gap, we introduce the framework of simulatable processes, where the learner has access to a simulator that approximates the distribution generating the data (which may be an arbitrarily complex and dependent process). Surprisingly, given access to such a simulator, we show that we can recover the same learning guarantees as in the classical setting with independent data, namely, error bounds that depend on the VC dimension. Further, we use this framework to study the power of conditional sampling and show strict statistical and computational advantages in this setting. As a highlight of our framework, we exhibit a single algorithm that simultaneously learns any given VC class under all processes samplable in bounded polynomial time, with regret controlled by the time-bounded Kolmogorov complexity of the process. This provides a significant conceptual broadening of the classical PAC model.

</details>


### [308] [Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging: Transitioning from Uniform Priors to Sparse Posteriors in Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13589)
*Meher Sai Preetam,Meher Bhaskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCSB框架，通过在概率单纯形上联合优化集成剪枝与概率校准，引入凹二次罚项解决L1-单纯形悖论，实现高达96%的集成压缩，同时提升校准质量和泛化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Bagging集成（如随机森林、Bagged SVM等）对所有基估计器赋予均匀投票权重，忽略了各基估计器在不同局部区域的差异性能力，导致模型过度自信且校准不良。

Method: 在概率单纯形上最小化袋外（OOB）损失，将集成剪枝与校准建模为联合优化问题；为克服单纯形上L1范数为常数无法实现稀疏性的"L1-单纯形悖论"，引入凹二次罚项诱导稀疏解。

Result: 模型无关的SCSB框架实现高达96%的集成压缩，推理速度线性提升，期望校准误差（ECE）显著降低，泛化精度保持或提升。

Conclusion: SCSB提供了一种数学严谨的后训练压缩与校准框架，有效解决了Bagging集成中均匀权重假设的缺陷，兼具稀疏性、高效性与良好的概率校准性能。

Abstract: We present Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB), a mathematically rigorous framework for post-training compression and probability calibration of bootstrap-based bagging ensembles. Standard bagging ensembles (such as Random Forests, Bagged SVMs, and Bagged Neural Networks) assign uniform voting power to all constituent estimators. However, this naive uniform prior ignores the varying local competence of base estimators and contributes to model overconfidence. We formulate ensemble pruning and calibration as a joint optimization problem over the probability simplex by minimizing the Out-Of-Bag (OOB) loss. To induce sparsity, we address the theoretical "L1-simplex paradox" -- the mathematical reality that the L1 norm is constant on the simplex and fails to prune -- by introducing a concave quadratic penalty. SCSB is model-agnostic and achieves up to 96% ensemble compression, yielding linear inference speedups and superior probability calibration (lowered Expected Calibration Error) while preserving or enhancing generalization accuracy.

</details>


### [309] [Beyond the Commitment Boundary: Probing Epiphenomenal Chain-of-Thought in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13603)
*Daniel Scalena,Sara Candussio,Luca Bortolussi,Elisabetta Fersini,Malvina Nissim,Gabriele Sarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文发现链式思维推理中存在一个"承诺边界"——模型从临时猜测突然转变为稳定高置信答案的临界点，该转变通常发生在推理块结束前的单个步骤中，后续步骤对最终答案概率无实质影响。基于此，作者利用注意力探针从中间步骤中线性解码答案形成阶段，并实现早停推理，平均缩短55%的推理链长度且性能损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维推理是测试时扩展的主流范式，但单个推理步骤对最终答案的因果影响仍不明确。理解这一机制有助于优化推理效率并揭示模型内部的推理过程。

Method: 通过早期退出估计每个推理步骤的因果重要性，识别承诺边界；使用注意力探针从中间推理步骤中线性解码答案形成阶段，并利用该信号在承诺边界处提前退出推理块。

Result: 推理过程存在承诺边界，该边界通常在推理块结束前的单个步骤中发生；承诺边界后的步骤对最终答案概率无影响（即"副现象"步骤）；注意力探针能以高准确率从中间步骤解码答案形成阶段，并泛化到未见过的推理任务；基于该信号的早停方法平均缩短55%的推理链长度，性能几乎不受影响。

Conclusion: 链式思维推理中的大量中间步骤是因果冗余的，答案在早期就已基本确定。通过识别承诺边界可以大幅提升推理效率，为高效推理提供了新的理论依据和实践方法。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is the dominant paradigm for inference-time scaling in language models, yet the causal influence of individual steps on the final answer poorly understood. We estimate each step's causal importance via early exit and use this measure to study how answers form across the reasoning traces of several model families. Across diverse tasks, we find that reasoning typically crosses a \emph{commitment boundary} -- a sharp transition from transient intermediate guesses to a stable, high-confidence answer. This transition often happens in a single step, well before the model's reasoning block ends, and is followed by \emph{epiphenomenal} CoT steps that leave the final answer probability unaltered. Using attention probes, we show that answer-formation stages can be linearly decoded from intermediate reasoning steps with high accuracy and generalize robustly to unseen reasoning tasks. We exploit this signal to early-exit reasoning blocks at the commitment boundary, reducing the length of CoTs up to 55\% on average with negligible impact on model performance.

</details>


### [310] [The Stable Recovery Manifold: Geometric Principles Governing Recoverability in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13637)
*Ayushman Trivedi,Bhavika Melwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分析恢复子空间维度（k_t）发现，在持续学习过程中，尽管表征发生显著漂移，但遗忘知识的恢复流形保持稳定（平均k_t=8.0），主角漂移与可恢复性强相关（r=-0.862），几何模型解释82.2%的方差。结论支持稳定恢复流形假说，表明灾难性遗忘主要是可访问性和流形对齐问题，而非信息破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Accessibility Collapse框架，探究持续学习中遗忘知识在几何结构上的可恢复性，验证遗忘是信息破坏还是可访问性问题的本质。

Method: 在Split CIFAR-100上使用顺序训练的ResNet-18，跨10个任务分析可恢复性、表征漂移和恢复复杂度；引入恢复子空间维度（k_t）量化保持90%完整探测性能所需的最小奇异方向数。

Result: 恢复维度稳定（k_t均值8.0），与可恢复性扩散假说相悖；主角漂移与可恢复性高度负相关（r=-0.862）；简单几何模型解释82.2%的可恢复性方差。

Conclusion: 支持稳定恢复流形假说，灾难性遗忘本质上是可访问性和流形对齐问题，而非先前学习知识的破坏。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is often viewed as the destruction of previously learned knowledge during sequential learning. Building on the Accessibility Collapse framework, we investigate the geometric structure of recoverability in continual learning. Using Split CIFAR-100 and a sequentially trained ResNet-18, we analyze recoverability, representational drift, and recovery complexity across ten tasks. We introduce Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), a measure of the minimum number of singular directions required to preserve 90 percent of full probe performance. Contrary to our Recoverability Diffusion hypothesis, recovery dimensionality remains stable throughout training (mean k_t = 8.0) despite substantial representational drift. Principal-angle drift strongly predicts recoverability (r = -0.862), and a simple geometric model explains 82.2 percent of recoverability variance. These findings support the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis, suggesting that forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable despite representational reorganization. The results indicate that catastrophic forgetting is primarily an accessibility and manifold-alignment problem rather than information destruction.

</details>


### [311] [Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13657)
*Guo Yu,Wenlin Liu,Yulan Hu,Hao-Xuan Ma,Jun-Peng Jiang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了on-policy distillation (OPD)的参数更新特性，发现其更新具有稀疏性和频谱集中性：更新量小而坐标稀疏、分布在各层且以FFN为主；尽管数值上满秩但频谱集中，主要落于源权重接近零的坐标上，偏离源权重的主奇异子空间。这表明密集教师监督并未使OPD成为普通的密集参数重写，而是保留了on-policy后训练的重要几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管OPD已成为流行的后训练方法，但其对模型参数的具体影响机制尚不清晰。作者旨在从稀疏性和几何结构两个维度揭示OPD更新如何改变模型参数。

Method: 跨多个语言和视觉-语言模型对及使用场景，分析OPD更新的稀疏性（更新大小、层分布、FFN侧重）和几何特性（数值秩、频谱集中性、与源权重子空间的关系），并进行优化器消融实验（SGD vs AdamW）及子网络训练验证。

Result: (1) OPD更新稀疏、小而坐标稀疏，分布在各层且以FFN为主，仅训练发现的子网络可恢复几乎全部性能；(2) 优化器消融中SGD不如AdamW，因密集教师监督保留了异质坐标梯度尺度；(3) 更新数值上满秩但频谱集中，主要落于源权重接近零的坐标，偏离源权重主奇异子空间。

Conclusion: 密集教师监督并未使OPD退化为普通密集参数重写，OPD保留了on-policy后训练的重要几何特征，更新稀疏且频谱集中的特性揭示了其与普通监督微调的本质差异。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe as it combines two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision, yet how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD}-style updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers and are usually FFN-heavy. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork recovers nearly the same performance as full \textsc{OPD}. However, the sparsity-inducing SGD optimizer underperforms AdamW in our optimizer ablation, likely because dense teacher supervision preserves heterogeneous coordinate-wise gradient scales where AdamW's adaptive scaling remains useful. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.

</details>


### [312] [Understanding Truncated Positional Encodings for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13671)
*James Flora,Mitchell Black,Weng-Keen Wong,Amir Nayyeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图神经网络中位置编码（PEs）在截断情况下的表达能力差异，证明理论上等价的谱类与游走类PE在截断后表达力根本不同，且截断的谱PE不再强于1-WL测试，实验表明混合使用多种截断PE优于单一类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中常用的截断位置编码（如前k个特征向量或邻接矩阵幂次）的理论性质尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 理论分析证明截断后不同PE家族表达力的根本差异，并引入k-调和距离族谱PE进行对比研究，辅以实验验证。

Result: 截断后谱PE不再强于1-WL测试；不同截断PE表达力存在本质差异；实验上混合截断PE优于单一类型。

Conclusion: 截断位置编码的理论性质与完整版本完全不同，实践中应混合使用多种截断PE以获得更好性能。

Abstract: Positional encodings (PEs) enhance the power of graph neural networks (GNNs), both theoretically and empirically. Two of the most popular families of PEs - spectral (e.g., Laplacian eigenspaces, effective resistance) and walk-based (polynomials of the adjacency matrix) - are theoretically equivalent in expressive power, with expressivity between the 1-WL and 3-WL tests. However, this equivalence assumes the GNN uses the "complete" version of these PEs, which requires $O(n^3)$ time and space complexity. Instead, practitioners commonly use truncated variants of these encodings, such as the first $k$ eigenspaces or powers of the adjacency matrix. However, the theoretical properties of these truncated PEs are unknown. In this work, we initiate the study of these truncated PEs. Theoretically, we show that, under truncation, several families of PEs are fundamentally different in expressive power. As a corollary, we show that truncated spectral PEs are no longer stronger than the 1-WL test. We also study a family of spectral PEs, the $k$-harmonic distances, to highlight the differences in expressive power of even closely related truncated PEs. Finally, we experimentally show that a mix of truncated PEs is preferable to any single family on real-world datasets.

</details>


### [313] [On Approximating the Dynamic Response of Synchronous Generators via Operator Learning: A Step Towards Building Deep Operator-based Power Grid Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.12538)
*Christian Moya,Amirhossein Mollaali,Guang Lin,Meng Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于算子学习（DeepONet）的框架，用于近似同步发电机的动态响应，包括神经网络模型构建、递归仿真、残差方案及误差估计，并通过DAgger策略进行数据聚合微调，最终验证了其在暂态模型近似中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统同步发电机动态模型在高精度仿真中计算成本高，难以在实时或大规模电网仿真中高效使用，因此需要一种数据驱动的、能够近似无限维解算子且可与电网仿真器交互的轻量化框架。

Method: 采用DeepONet学习发电机解算子；设计递归数值方案基于DeepONet进行多步仿真；提出残差DeepONet方案以融入已有数学模型并给出累积误差估计；利用DAgger策略进行数据聚合微调，使模型适应交互仿真中的分布偏移。

Result: 作为概念验证，所提框架能够有效近似同步发电机的暂态模型，表明其在电网仿真中的可行性和准确性。

Conclusion: 该算子学习框架为构建可交互的神经网络发电机模型提供了有效途径，兼具数据驱动与先验知识融合能力，未来可推广至更复杂的电力系统组件建模。

Abstract: This paper develops an Operator Learning framework for approximating the dynamic response of synchronous generators. The framework can be used to (i) build a neural network-based generator model that interacts with a power grid simulator or (ii) shadow the true generator's transient response. First, we develop a data-driven Deep Operator Network (DeepONet) to approximate the infinite-dimensional solution operator of the generators. Then, we design a numerical scheme based on DeepONet that simulates the generator's response over a given time horizon. The proposed scheme recursively employs the trained DeepONet to simulate the response for a given multi-dimensional input that describes the interaction between the generator and the power grid. In addition, we design a residual DeepONet numerical scheme that can incorporate information from existing mathematical models. We accompany this residual DeepONet scheme with an estimate for the prediction's cumulative error. Finally, we build a data aggregation (DAgger) strategy that allows fine-tuning of DeepONets using aggregated training data that the DeepONets will likely encounter during interactive simulations with other grid components. As a proof of concept, we demonstrate that the proposed frameworks can effectively approximate the transient model of a synchronous generator.

</details>


### [314] [On Pitfalls of $\textit{RemOve-And-Retrain}$: Data Processing Inequality Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.13836)
*Junhwa Song,Keumgang Cha,Junghoon Seo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文信息论视角揭示ROAR基准存在根本性缺陷：后处理（如模糊化）虽不增加信息量（数据加工不等式），却能提升ROAR分数，说明ROAR分数高不意味着归因图包含更多模型决策信息。该偏差源于ROAR偏好空间模糊的掩码，实验（CIFAR-10、SVHN、CUB-200）验证了模糊性与ROAR性能的正相关，该模式在ROAD变体中也存在。文章提出更谨慎的基于移除的基准测试指南，对神经网络内部机制验证有启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ROAR基准在评估特征归因方法时被广泛使用，但其有效性从未从信息论角度被深入验证。作者怀疑ROAR的排序结果可能存在偏差，即不携带更多信息的后处理操作也能提升ROAR分数，因此需要通过信息论分析来揭示这一潜在缺陷。

Method: 从信息论出发，利用数据加工不等式证明模型和数据无关的后处理（如模糊化）不会增加归因图关于决策函数的信息量。通过实验在CIFAR-10、SVHN、CUB-200数据集上验证模糊性与ROAR性能的正相关关系，并扩展到ROAD变体进行检验。

Result: 证实后处理操作（虽不增加信息）能提升ROAR分数，表明ROAR排名不反映归因图的信息含量。模糊性（blurriness）与ROAR性能存在一致的正相关，该偏差模式在ROAD变体中也重复出现。

Conclusion: ROAR基准存在对模糊掩码的偏好偏差，不能作为特征归因方法信息含量的可靠指标。提出更谨慎的基于移除的基准测试指南，对验证神经网络内部机制的理解具有重要启示。

Abstract: The RemOve-And-Retrain (ROAR) benchmark is widely used to evaluate feature attribution methods, yet its validity remains underexplored from an information-theoretic perspective. We show that model- and data-agnostic post-processing of attribution maps (transformations that, by the data processing inequality, \emph{cannot} add information about the decision function) can often improve ROAR scores. This means that an improved ROAR ranking is not, by itself, evidence that an attribution map carries more information about the model. We trace this failure mode to a bias toward spatially blurry masks. Experiments on CIFAR-10, SVHN, and CUB-200 show a consistent association between blurriness and ROAR performance, a pattern that also appears in the ROAD variant. We provide guidelines for more cautious removal-based benchmarking, with implications for validating mechanistic understanding of neural network internals.

</details>


### [315] [Geometry of Lightning Self-Attention: Identifiability and Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.17221)
*Nathan W. Henry,Giovanni Luca Marchetti,Kathlén Kohn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用代数几何工具，从理论上分析了无归一化自注意力网络定义的函数空间几何，研究了深层注意力的可辨识性，描述了参数化的通用纤维，计算了函数空间维度，并表征了单层模型的奇异点和边界点，最后将结果推测性扩展到归一化自注意力网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力网络是Transformer的核心组件，但其函数空间的理论几何性质尚未被充分理解。本文旨在填补这一理论基础空白，从代数几何视角揭示自注意力网络的本质结构。

Method: 采用代数几何作为核心分析工具。针对无归一化的自注意力网络（多项式形式），研究参数化映射的通用纤维以刻画可辨识性，推导函数空间维度；对单层模型分析奇异点和边界点；并通过理论证明和数值验证将结论推广至归一化情况。

Result: 给出了任意层数深度自注意力网络参数化通用纤维的描述；计算了函数空间的维度；完整刻画了单层模型的奇异点和边界点；对于归一化自注意力网络，证明了单层情况下的结论，并在深层情况下进行了数值验证。

Conclusion: 代数几何为理解自注意力网络的理论性质提供了有力工具。无归一化自注意力网络具有可刻画的可辨识性和函数空间结构，且这些理论结果可部分推广至更实用的归一化自注意力网络。

Abstract: We consider function spaces defined by self-attention networks without normalization, and theoretically analyze their geometry. Since these networks are polynomial, we rely on tools from algebraic geometry. In particular, we study the identifiability of deep attention by providing a description of the generic fibers of the parametrization for an arbitrary number of layers and, as a consequence, compute the dimension of the function space. Additionally, for a single-layer model, we characterize the singular and boundary points. Finally, we formulate a conjectural extension of our results to normalized self-attention networks, prove it for a single layer, and numerically verify it in the deep case.

</details>


### [316] [Is Stochastic Gradient Descent Effective? A PDE Perspective on Machine Learning processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08425)
*Davide Barbieri,Matteo Bonforte,Peio Ibarrondo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Fokker-Planck型抛物偏微分方程分析随机梯度下降(SGD)的行为，识别出漂移阶段（权重集中到局部最小值）和扩散阶段（随机波动帮助逃离次优局部最小值），并利用对偶方法和熵方法证明了非凸损失函数和退化扩散矩阵下SGD的平均退出时间上下界及渐近收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGD是监督学习中广泛使用的优化方法，但其在非凸势函数和退化扩散矩阵下的动力学行为尚不明确。现有Fokker-Planck方程理论多针对凸情形或非退化扩散，无法直接应用于实际深度学习场景，因此需要发展新理论来分析SGD的逃逸时间、收敛性和参数演化等基本问题。

Method: 1. 将SGD动力学建模为Fokker-Planck型抛物PDE；2. 区分漂移阶段（损失函数驱动权重集中到最近局部极小值）和扩散阶段（随机波动帮助逃离次优局部极小值）；3. 对平均退出时间(MET)推导上下界；4. 利用对偶方法和熵方法处理非凸损失函数和退化扩散矩阵下的渐近收敛问题。

Result: 1. 定量刻画了漂移阶段权重向最近局部最小值集中的现象；2. 给出了平均退出时间的上下界估计；3. 在非凸成本函数和退化扩散矩阵条件下，证明了SGD的渐近收敛性。

Conclusion: 建立了随机优化与PDE理论之间的深层联系，为机器学习中的基本问题（SGD逃离坏极小值所需时间、参数是否收敛、训练早期参数如何演化）提供了理论答案和洞见。

Abstract: In this paper we analyze the behaviour of the stochastic gradient descent (SGD), a widely used method in supervised learning for optimizing neural network weights via a minimization of non-convex loss functions. Since the pioneering work of E, Li and Tai (2017), the underlying structure of such processes can be understood via parabolic PDEs of Fokker-Planck type, which are at the core of our analysis. Even if Fokker-Planck equations have a long history and a extensive literature, almost nothing is known when the potential is non-convex or when the diffusion matrix is degenerate, and this is the main difficulty that we face in our analysis.
  We identify two different regimes: in the initial phase of SGD, the loss function drives the weights to concentrate around the nearest local minimum. We refer to this phase as the drift regime and we provide quantitative estimates on this concentration phenomenon. Next, we introduce the diffusion regime, where stochastic fluctuations help the learning process to escape suboptimal local minima. We analyze the Mean Exit Time (MET) and prove upper and lower bounds of the MET. Finally, we address the asymptotic convergence of SGD, for a non-convex cost function and a degenerate diffusion matrix, that do not allow to use the standard approaches, and require new techniques. For this purpose, we exploit two different methods: duality and entropy methods.
  We provide new results about the dynamics and effectiveness of SGD, offering a deep connection between stochastic optimization and PDE theory, and some answers and insights to basic questions in the Machine Learning processes: How long does SGD take to escape from a bad minimum? Do neural network parameters converge using SGD? How do parameters evolve in the first stage of training with SGD?

</details>


### [317] [Fourier Multi-Component and Multi-Layer Neural Networks: Unlocking High-Frequency Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18959)
*Shijun Zhang,Hongkai Zhao,Yimin Zhong,Haomin Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出傅里叶多分量多层神经网络（FMMNN），将正弦型激活函数与多分量多层结构相结合，证明了其在函数逼近中的指数级表达能力、更优的优化景观，并提出了加速训练的第一层权重缩放随机初始化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的架构与激活函数的选择对性能至关重要，且两者之间的匹配是实现有效表示和学习的关键。现有研究缺乏对两者协同设计的深入探讨。

Method: 将正弦型激活函数与多分量多层结构（MMNN）结合，每个分量表示为固定随机正弦型基函数的可训练线性组合，通过多层组合生成复杂自适应的高频特征；提出第一层权重的缩放随机初始化方法。

Result: FMMNN在低秩架构下仍能保持指数级函数逼近表达能力；优化景观比标准全连接网络更有利，尤其对高频目标；所提初始化方法在样本充足时加速训练并提升最终性能；数值实验证实了其对振荡函数逼近的强精度和良好收敛行为。

Conclusion: FMMNN通过架构与激活函数的协同设计，在高频函数逼近任务中优于标准网络，兼具理论保证与实践有效性。

Abstract: The architecture of a neural network and the choice of its activation function are both fundamental to its performance. Equally important is ensuring that these two elements are well matched, as their alignment is key to effective representation and learning. In this paper, we introduce the Fourier Multi-Component and Multi-Layer Neural Network (FMMNN), a model that combines sine-type activations with the multi-component and multi-layer structure of MMNNs. In an FMMNN, each component is represented as a trainable linear combination of fixed random sine-type basis functions, while multi-layer composition generates more complex and adaptive high-frequency features. We establish that FMMNNs retain exponential expressive power for function approximation even under a low-rank architectural structure. We also analyze the optimization landscape of FMMNNs and find it to be substantially more favorable than that of standard fully connected neural networks, especially for high-frequency targets. In addition, we propose a scaled random initialization method for the first-layer weights in FMMNNs, which accelerates training and improves final performance when sufficient samples are available. Extensive numerical experiments support our theoretical insights, showing that FMMNNs achieve strong accuracy and favorable convergence behavior on oscillatory function-approximation benchmarks.

</details>


### [318] [ASTER: Latent Pseudo-Anomaly Generation for Unsupervised Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13924)
*Romain Hermary,Samet Hicsonmez,Dan Pineau,Abd El Rahman Shabayek,Djamila Aouada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASTER提出了一种在潜在空间中生成伪异常的时间序列异常检测框架，利用预训练LLM增强表示，无需领域知识即可实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列异常检测面临异常稀少、异质性强以及标签数据稀缺的挑战，现有无监督方法（如重构、预测或基于嵌入的方法）要么难以处理复杂数据，要么需要领域特定的异常合成和固定距离度量。

Method: ASTER在潜在空间中直接生成伪异常，使用潜在空间解码器生成定制的伪异常来训练基于Transformer的异常分类器，并利用预训练LLM增强潜在空间的时间与上下文表示。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能，树立了基于LLM的时间序列异常检测的新标准。

Conclusion: ASTER通过潜在空间伪异常生成与LLM增强表示，有效解决了无监督时间序列异常检测中领域知识依赖和复杂数据处理的难题。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection (TSAD) is critical in domains such as industrial monitoring, healthcare, and cybersecurity, but it remains challenging due to rare and heterogeneous anomalies and the scarcity of labelled data. This scarcity makes unsupervised approaches predominant, yet existing methods often rely on reconstruction or forecasting, which struggle with complex data, or on embedding-based approaches that require domain-specific anomaly synthesis and fixed distance metrics. We propose ASTER, a framework that generates pseudo-anomalies directly in the latent space, avoiding handcrafted anomaly injections and the need for domain expertise. A latent-space decoder produces tailored pseudo-anomalies to train a Transformer-based anomaly classifier, while a pre-trained LLM enriches the temporal and contextual representations of this space. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that ASTER achieves state-of-the-art performance and sets a new standard for LLM-based TSAD.

</details>


### [319] [Learning on a Razor's Edge: Identifiability and Singularity of Polynomial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11846)
*Vahid Shahverdi,Giovanni Luca Marchetti,Kathlén Kohn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用代数几何工具，研究了以神经网络参数化的函数空间（神经流形）。针对深度MLP和CNN（使用足够通用的多项式激活函数），首先解决了可辨识性问题：MLP中几乎所有函数仅对应有限个参数选择，而CNN的参数化是单射的，并据此计算了神经流形的维度。其次，描述了神经流形的奇异点，发现奇异点源于稀疏子网络，且MLP的奇异点常对应于均方误差损失的临界点（CNN则不然），从而从几何角度解释了MLP的稀疏性偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络参数化函数空间的几何结构，特别是可辨识性（参数与函数的对应关系）和奇异点的性质，并借此解释MLP的稀疏性偏好这一经验现象。

Method: 采用代数几何工具，研究使用多项式激活函数的深度MLP和CNN的神经流形。通过分析参数化映射的代数性质，证明可辨识性结论并计算流形维度；通过分析雅可比矩阵的秩刻画奇异点，并建立与稀疏子网络的联系。

Result: 1) MLP的神经流形中几乎所有函数仅有有限个参数表示；CNN的参数化是生成单射的。2) 计算了两种架构的神经流形维度。3) 完全刻画了CNN的奇异点，部分刻画了MLP的奇异点，两者均源于稀疏子网络。4) MLP的奇异点常对应MSE损失的临界点，而CNN不然，这解释了MLP的稀疏性偏好。

Conclusion: 代数几何为理解神经网络的几何结构提供了有力工具，揭示了可辨识性、奇异点与稀疏性之间的深层联系，为解释和设计具有更好泛化性能的网络架构提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study function spaces parametrized by neural networks, referred to as neuromanifolds. Specifically, we focus on deep Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLPs) and Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) with an activation function that is a sufficiently generic polynomial. First, we address the identifiability problem, showing that, for almost all functions in the neuromanifold of an MLP, there exist only finitely many parameter choices yielding that function. For CNNs, the parametrization is generically one-to-one. As a consequence, we compute the dimension of the neuromanifold. Second, we describe singular points of neuromanifolds. We characterize singularities completely for CNNs, and partially for MLPs. In both cases, they arise from sparse subnetworks. For MLPs, we prove that these singularities often correspond to critical points of the mean-squared error loss, which does not hold for CNNs. This provides a geometric explanation of the sparsity bias of MLPs. All of our results leverage tools from algebraic geometry.

</details>


### [320] [BrainDINO: A Brain MRI Foundation Model for Generalizable Clinical Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27277)
*Yizhou Wu,Shansong Wang,Yuheng Li,Mojtaba Safari,Mingzhe Hu,Chih-Wei Chang,Harini Veeraraghavan,Xiaofeng Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BrainDINO是一个基于自蒸馏的预训练基础模型，在约660万张无标注轴向切片上训练，通过冻结编码器搭配轻量任务头，可泛化至脑肿瘤分割、神经退行性疾病分类、脑龄估计、中风后时间预测、分子状态预测、MRI序列分类和生存建模等多种脑MRI下游任务，在标签稀缺场景下优势尤为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的脑MRI分析方法多为任务特定且依赖大量标注数据，缺乏能跨不同任务、人群、疾病和采集设置统一泛化的通用表示模型。

Method: 使用自蒸馏（self-distillation）自监督学习范式，在来自20个数据集的约660万张无标注轴向切片上训练BrainDINO；下游应用时冻结编码器，仅训练轻量任务头（lightweight task heads），无需体素级预训练或全网络微调。

Result: BrainDINO在肿瘤分割、神经退行性与神经发育疾病分类、脑龄估计、中风后时间预测、分子状态预测、MRI序列分类及生存建模等任务上，一致达到或超过了基于自然图像和MRI特定的自监督基线方法，在标签稀缺时优势尤为突出；特征分析显示其具有解剖学组织和病理敏感的表示结构。

Conclusion: 大规模切片级自监督学习能够产生统一的脑MRI表示，支持多样化的神经影像下游任务，无需体素级预训练或全网络微调，为鲁棒且数据高效的脑影像分析提供了可扩展的基础范式。

Abstract: Brain MRI underpins a wide range of neuroscientific and clinical applications, yet most learning-based methods remain task-specific and require substantial labeled data. Here we show that a single self-supervised representation can generalize across heterogeneous brain MRI endpoints. We trained BrainDINO, a self-distilled foundation model, on approximately 6.6 million unlabeled axial slices from 20 datasets encompassing broad variation in population, disease, and acquisition setting. Using a frozen encoder with lightweight task heads, BrainDINO supported transfer across tumor segmentation, neurodegenerative and neurodevelopmental conditions classification, brain age estimation, post-stroke temporal prediction, molecular status prediction, MRI sequence classification, and survival modeling. Across tasks and supervision regimes, BrainDINO consistently equaled or exceeded natural-image and MRI-specific self-supervised baselines, with particularly strong advantages under label scarcity. Representation analyses further showed anatomically organized and pathology-sensitive feature structure in the absence of task-specific supervision. Our findings indicate that large-scale slice-wise self-supervised learning can yield a unified brain MRI representation that supports diverse neuroimaging tasks without volumetric pretraining or full-network fine-tuning, establishing a scalable foundation for robust and data-efficient brain imaging analysis. Code is available at https://github.com/mclwu22/BrainDINO

</details>


### [321] [Lightweight and Interpretable Transformer via Mixed Graph Algorithm Unrolling for Traffic Forecast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13102)
*Ji Qi,Tam Thuc Do,Mingxiao Liu,Zhuoshi Pan,Yuzhe Li,Gene Cheung,H. Vicky Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过展开混合图优化算法，构建了一种轻量级、可解释的类Transformer神经网络用于时空交通预测。模型利用无向图捕获空间相关性、有向图捕获时序关系，设计新的$\ell_2$和$\ell_1$范数变分项促进有向图上的信号平滑性，通过ADMM算法展开为前馈网络，并周期性插入图学习模块替代自注意力机制。实验表明，该方法在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，参数量大幅减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于自注意力的Transformer模型作为"黑盒"缺乏可解释性且参数量庞大，本文旨在构建一种轻量级、可解释的类Transformer网络用于时空交通预测。

Method: 构建无向图$\mathcal{G}^u$（空间相关性）和有向图$\mathcal{G}^d$（时序关系）；设计$\ell_2$和$\ell_1$范数变分项量化/促进有向图信号平滑性；基于ADMM设计迭代算法并展开为前馈网络进行数据驱动参数学习；周期性插入图学习模块替代自注意力机制。

Result: 所提出的展开网络在交通预测任务上达到了与最先进方案竞争的性能，同时参数量大幅减少。

Conclusion: 通过优化算法展开构建可解释的轻量级Transformer替代方案是可行的，图学习模块可有效替代自注意力机制，实现性能与效率的权衡。

Abstract: Unlike conventional "black-box" transformers with classical self-attention mechanism, we build a lightweight and interpretable transformer-like neural net by unrolling a mixed-graph-based optimization algorithm to forecast traffic with spatial and temporal dimensions. We construct two graphs: an undirected graph $\mathcal{G}^u$ capturing spatial correlations across geography, and a directed graph $\mathcal{G}^d$ capturing sequential relationships over time. We predict future samples of signal $\mathbf{x}$, assuming it is "smooth" with respect to both $\mathcal{G}^u$ and $\mathcal{G}^d$, where we design new $\ell_2$ and $\ell_1$-norm variational terms to quantify and promote signal smoothness (low-frequency reconstruction) on a directed graph. We design an iterative algorithm based on alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM), and unroll it into a feed-forward network for data-driven parameter learning. We periodically insert graph learning modules for $\mathcal{G}^u$ and $\mathcal{G}^d$ that play the role of self-attention. Experiments show that our unrolled networks achieve competitive traffic forecast performance as state-of-the-art prediction schemes, while reducing parameter counts drastically.

</details>


### [322] [Possibilistic Predictive Uncertainty for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00600)
*Yao Ni,Jeremie Houssineau,Yew-Soon Ong,Piotr Koniusz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dirichlet-近似可能性后验预测（DAPPr）框架，通过可能性理论实现高效且原则性的认知不确定性建模。该方法将参数的后验可能性投影到预测空间并用可学习的Dirichlet可能性函数近似，在保持计算效率的同时取得了与最先进二阶预测器相当或更优的不确定性量化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性建模方法面临两难困境：贝叶斯方法提供原则性估计但计算成本高昂，而高效的二阶预测器缺乏其特定目标与认知不确定性量化之间的严格联系。为解决这一困境，需要一个既具有理论原则性又保持计算效率的统一框架。

Method: DAPPr首先定义参数上的可能性后验，通过上确界算子将其投影到预测空间，然后使用可学习的Dirichlet可能性函数近似投影后的后验。这种"投影-近似"策略产生了具有闭式解的简单训练目标。

Result: 在多个不同基准上的广泛实验表明，DAPPr在不确定性量化性能上与最先进的二阶预测器相比具有竞争力或更优，同时保持了原则性的推导过程和计算效率。

Conclusion: DAPPr成功弥合了贝叶斯方法的理论严谨性与二阶预测器的计算效率之间的鸿沟，为认知不确定性建模提供了一种既有原则性又实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep neural networks achieve impressive results across diverse applications, yet their overconfidence on unseen inputs necessitates reliable epistemic uncertainty modeling. Existing methods for uncertainty modeling face a fundamental dilemma: Bayesian approaches provide principled estimates but remain computationally prohibitive, while efficient second-order predictors lack rigorous connections between their specific objectives and epistemic uncertainty quantification. To resolve this dilemma, we introduce Dirichlet-approximated possibilistic posterior predictions (DAPPr), a principled framework grounded in possibility theory. We define a possibilistic posterior over parameters, project it to the prediction space via supremum operators, and approximate the projected posterior using learnable Dirichlet possibility functions. This projection-and-approximation strategy yields a simple training objective with closed-form solutions. Despite its simplicity, extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks show that DAPPr achieves competitive or superior uncertainty quantification performance over state-of-the-art second-order predictors while maintaining both principled derivation and computational efficiency. Code is available at https://github.com/MaxwellYaoNi/DAPPr.

</details>


### [323] [ExPLAIND: Unifying Model, Data, and Training Attribution to Study Model Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20076)
*Florian Eichin,Yupei Du,Philipp Mondorf,Maria Matveev,Barbara Plank,Michael A. Hedderich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ExPLAIND是一个统一的理论框架，能够整合模型组件、数据和训练轨迹，支持从局部到全局的多粒度解释。通过将AdamW训练的模型重新表述为核机器，推导出参数级和步骤级的影响分数，并在Transformer的Grokking现象和EuroLLM预训练中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的事后可解释性方法通常孤立地归因模型行为到组件、数据或训练轨迹，且局限于特定的局部-全局粒度，导致缺乏统一视角并可能遗漏关键交互。

Method: 基于梯度路径核的理论推广，将AdamW训练的模型重新表述为核机器；从核特征图中推导参数级和步骤级的影响分数。

Result: 在Transformer Grokking实验中，支持了先前提出的学习阶段划分，并精炼了最终阶段为外层围绕记忆后形成的表征管道对齐；在EuroLLM预训练中，揭示了第一阶段外层MLP学习、第二阶段中间注意力层相对影响增加的两阶段动态。

Conclusion: ExPLAIND作为一种统一的解释模型行为和训练动态的框架得到了实验验证。

Abstract: Post-hoc interpretability methods typically attribute a model's behavior to its components, data, or training trajectory in isolation, and are often tied to a particular level of granularity along the local-to-global spectrum. This leads to explanations that lack a unified view and may miss key interactions. We present ExPLAIND, a theoretically grounded, unified framework that integrates model components, data, and training trajectory while supporting explanations across granularities. We generalize recent work on gradient path kernels, reformulating models trained by AdamW as kernel machines. From the resulting kernel feature maps, we derive novel parameter-wise and step-wise influence scores. We empirically validate the resulting decomposition of model behavior in several settings and apply ExPLAIND to two case studies. Our findings on a Transformer exhibiting Grokking support previously proposed learning phases, while refining the final phase as one in which outer layers align around a representation pipeline learned after memorization. For EuroLLM pretraining, ExPLAIND reveals a two-phase dynamic, with the first characterized by outer-layer MLP learning and the second by increased relative influence of intermediate attention layers. These results establish ExPLAIND as a unified framework for interpreting model behavior and training dynamics.

</details>


### [324] [LLM-ODDR: A Large Language Model Framework for Joint Order Dispatching and Driver Repositioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22695)
*Tengfei Lyu,Siyuan Feng,Hao Liu,Hai Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLM-ODDR框架，利用大语言模型联合优化网约车订单分配与司机重定位，在纽约曼哈顿真实数据集上显著优于传统方法，并首次探索LLM作为决策主体在网约车领域的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网约车订单分配与司机重定位方法（组合优化、规则启发式、强化学习）忽视了司机收入公平性、可解释性及对真实世界动态的适应性。

Method: 提出LLM-ODDR框架，包含三个组件：(1)多目标导向的订单价值细化；(2)公平感知的订单分配；(3)时空需求感知的司机重定位。并开发了领域知识微调的JointDR-GPT模型。

Result: 在纽约曼哈顿出租车真实数据集上，框架在有效性、异常条件适应性和决策可解释性方面显著优于传统方法；通过并行分解和模型蒸馏可将计算延迟降低到生产可用水平。

Conclusion: 首次将LLM作为决策主体应用于网约车订单分配与司机重定位任务，为智能交通系统中集成高级语言模型建立了基础性见解。

Abstract: Ride-hailing platforms face significant challenges in optimizing order dispatching and driver repositioning operations in dynamic urban environments. Traditional approaches based on combinatorial optimization, rule-based heuristics, and reinforcement learning often overlook driver income fairness, interpretability, and adaptability to real-world dynamics. To address these gaps, we propose LLM-ODDR, a novel framework leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) for joint Order Dispatching and Driver Repositioning (ODDR) in ride-hailing services. LLM-ODDR framework comprises three key components: (1) Multi-objective-guided Order Value Refinement, which evaluates orders by considering multiple objectives to determine their overall value; (2) Fairness-aware Order Dispatching, which balances platform revenue with driver income fairness; and (3) Spatiotemporal Demand-Aware Driver Repositioning, which optimizes idle vehicle placement based on historical patterns and projected supply. We also develop JointDR-GPT, a fine-tuned model optimized for ODDR tasks with domain knowledge. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets from Manhattan taxi operations demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms traditional methods in terms of effectiveness, adaptability to anomalous conditions, and decision interpretability. To our knowledge, this is the first exploration of LLMs as decision-making agents in ride-hailing ODDR tasks, establishing foundational insights for integrating advanced language models within intelligent transportation systems. While the current framework incurs higher computational costs than traditional methods, we show that parallel decomposition and model distillation can reduce latency to production-viable levels for deployment.

</details>


### [325] [How Reliable are Fairness Audits with Unreliable Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.23033)
*Yash Vardhan Tomar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了公平性审计中受保护标签缺失对审计结果可靠性的影响，提出了种子校准压力测试方法，发现在部分标签缺失时，所选缓解方法的表现通常未超出完全标签下的种子间基线。当标签完全缺失时，候选方法退化为经验风险最小化基线。阈值优化可能将单一受保护轴上的公平性改进转化为交叉性伤害，且该发现经随机森林验证仍成立。研究建议报告标签缺失时应同时提供种子校准、候选集背景和交叉性后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平性审计是负责任机器学习部署的关键组成部分，但在受保护标签不完整的情况下，审计建议的可靠性尚不明确。现有研究对标签缺失如何影响公平性缓解方法的审计效果缺乏系统理解。

Method: 提出了种子校准压力测试方法，用于分离缺失效应与完全标签下已有的种子间波动。在ACS/Folktables任务上评估不同缺失设置下公平性缓解方法的表现。

Result: 部分标签缺失时缓解方法未显著超出完全标签基线；零标签访问时方法退化为ERM基线；阈值优化可在单一轴上产生公平增益但引发交叉性伤害，该结果经随机森林验证稳健。

Conclusion: 受保护标签缺失应结合种子校准、候选集背景和交叉性后果进行报告，而不应直接将其视为审计脆弱性的证据。

Abstract: Fairness audits are a key component of responsible machine-learning deployment. Yet, audit-recommendation reliability under incomplete protected-label access is still poorly understood. In this work, we focused on protected-label missingness in fairness mitigation audits. We introduced a seed-calibrated stress test to separate missingness effects from seed-to-seed movement already present under complete labels. Across ACS/Folktables tasks, missingness settings that retain some protected labels usually do not move selected mitigation methods beyond a complete-label seed-to-seed baseline. At $0%$ protected-label access, candidates collapse to an empirical-risk-minimization baseline and deterministic tie-breaking rather than revealing a broad missingness effect. We also found that threshold optimization can turn fairness gains on a single protected axis into intersectional harm above a seed baseline, and this threshold-optimizer finding persists under random-forest validation. Overall, our results highlight that protected-label missingness should be reported with seed-null calibration, candidate-set context, and intersectional consequences before it is treated as evidence of audit fragility.

</details>


### [326] [PlaceRep: Geospatial Place Representation Learning from Large-Scale Point-of-Interest Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02921)
*Mohammad Hashemi,Hossein Amiri,Andreas Zufle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PlaceRep 是一种通过聚类空间和语义相关的兴趣点（POI）来构建场所级表示的地理空间表示学习方法，无需模型预训练，可自动识别多尺度空间区域，在人口密度估计和房价预测任务中优于现有方法，并实现高达100倍的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间表示学习方法通常将POI聚合到预定义的行政边界（如普查单元或邮政编码区域）内，为每个区域分配单一的嵌入表示。然而，POI往往形成跨越、包含或超越这些边界的语义上有意义的群体，能更好地反映人类活动和城市功能。现有方法无法捕捉这种跨边界的空间结构。

Method: PlaceRep 通过聚类空间和语义相关的POI来构建场所级表示。该方法从大规模POI图（美国Foursquare数据）中总结生成通用城市区域嵌入，同时自动识别多空间尺度的场所。无需模型预训练，提供可扩展且高效的解决方案。

Result: 在人口密度估计和房价预测两个下游任务中，PlaceRep 优于大多数最先进的基于图的地理空间表示学习方法，并且在处理大规模POI图时生成区域级表示的速度提升了高达100倍。

Conclusion: PlaceRep 通过聚类POI而非依赖固定行政边界，有效捕捉了城市的空间结构，提供了高效、可扩展且性能优越的地理空间表示学习方案。

Abstract: Learning effective representations of urban environments requires capturing spatial structure beyond fixed administrative boundaries. Existing geospatial representation learning approaches typically aggregate Points of Interest (POIs) into pre-defined administrative regions such as census units or ZIP code areas, assigning a single embedding to each region. However, POIs often form semantically meaningful groups that extend across, within, or beyond these boundaries, defining places that better reflect human activity and urban function. To address this limitation, we propose PlaceRep, a geospatial representation learning method that constructs place-level representations by clustering spatially and semantically related POIs. PlaceRep summarizes large-scale POI graphs from U.S. Foursquare data to produce general-purpose urban region embeddings while automatically identifying places across multiple spatial scales. By eliminating model pre-training, PlaceRep provides a scalable and efficient solution for multi-granular geospatial analysis. Experiments using the tasks of population density estimation and housing price prediction as downstream tasks show that PlaceRep outperforms most state-of-the-art graph-based geospatial representation learning methods and achieves up to a x100 speedup in generating region-level representations on large-scale POI graphs. The implementation of PlaceRep is available at https://github.com/mohammadhashemii/PlaceRep.

</details>


### [327] [Single vs. Multiple Branches in DeepONet and S-DeepONet: Network Architecture Follows Coupling in Multiphysics Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03660)
*Jaewan Park,Kazuma Kobayashi,Qibang Liu,Seid Koric,Diab Abueidda,Syed Bahauddin Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了单分支和多分支深度算子网络在强耦合多物理场问题中的性能，发现单分支架构通过共享潜在表征在紧耦合场景下表现更优，而多分支架构更适合解耦或单物理任务。训练后的代理模型比传统物理求解器快达1.8×10⁴倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度算子网络在单物理问题中表现良好，但在捕捉耦合系统（如热-机械、电-热耦合）中的非线性交互作用方面的有效性尚未充分探索。本文旨在回答一个实际问题：神经算子的架构设计是否应反映其所建模的物理耦合强度。

Method: 比较了单分支和多分支两种架构设计，涵盖前馈和序列递归两种形式，在三个代表性系统上进行实验：具有异质源的反应-扩散问题、具有温度相关电导率和焦耳热的非线性热电问题、钢凝固过程的粘塑性热-机械模型。

Result: 单分支网络在紧耦合机制下始终优于多分支变体，通过鼓励共享潜在表征实现；多分支设计在解耦或单物理任务中仍具优势。训练后的代理模型比基于物理的求解器快达1.8×10⁴倍。

Conclusion: 神经算子架构设计应反映物理耦合强度：强耦合场景宜采用单分支架构以利用共享潜在表征，弱耦合或解耦场景可考虑多分支架构。
