Abstract: `Real-time prediction of complex physical systems requires surrogate models that learn from data while representing strong multiphysics coupling. Deep Operator Networks have shown success in single-physics problems, yet their effectiveness in capturing nonlinear interactions in coupled systems (such as thermo-mechanical or electro-thermal coupling) remains underexplored. Here we pose a practical question: should the architecture of a neural operator reflect the strength of physical coupling it aims to model? We compare single-branch and multi-branch designs, in both feedforward and sequential recurrent forms, across three representative systems: a reaction--diffusion problem with heterogeneous sources, a nonlinear thermo-electrical problem with temperature-dependent conductivity and Joule heating, and a viscoplastic thermo-mechanical model of steel solidification. Single-branch networks consistently outperform multi-branch variants in tightly coupled regimes by encouraging shared latent representations, whereas multi-branch designs remain favorable for decoupled or single-physics tasks. Once trained, these surrogates deliver full-field predictions up to $1.8 \times 10^4$ times faster than physics-based solvers.

</details>


### [328] [Meta-Learning Transformers to Improve In-Context Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.05019)
*Lorenzo Braccaioli,Anna Vettoruzzo,Prabhant Singh,Joaquin Vanschoren,Mohamed-Rafik Bouguelia,Nicola Conci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用多个小型、领域特定的数据集集合来训练上下文学习模型，替代传统的大规模非结构化数据集。实验表明，这种策略能提高模型泛化能力，在受控、持续学习和无监督场景下均表现良好，同时具备模块化和可替换性的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练范式依赖大型非结构化数据集，存在存储成本高、质量与平衡性难以评估、以及包含敏感信息引发的隐私和伦理问题。为解决这些局限和风险，提出替代训练策略。

Method: 使用Meta-Album集合（多个小型、领域特定数据集），通过元学习训练上下文学习器。在三种场景下评估：1) 受控环境（测试域完全排除在训练知识外）；2) 持续学习场景（信息可获取时间有限）；3) 无监督场景。

Result: 1) 高质量和多样性的小规模数据集集合能提升上下文学习器在训练域外的泛化能力；2) 性能与在单个大规模数据集上训练的模型相当；3) 该范式在模块化和可替换性方面具有优势。

Conclusion: 使用精心筛选的数据集集合训练Transformer模型仍能保持上下文预测的泛化能力，同时解决了大规模数据集的存储、隐私和伦理问题，提供了一种更可控、可维护的训练范式。

Abstract: In-context learning enables transformer models to generalize to new tasks based solely on input prompts, without any need for weight updates. However, existing training paradigms typically rely on large, unstructured datasets that are costly to store, difficult to evaluate for quality and balance, and pose privacy and ethical concerns due to the inclusion of sensitive information. Motivated by these limitations and risks, we propose an alternative training strategy where we leverage a collection of multiple, small-scale, and domain-specific datasets. We empirically demonstrate that the increased quality and diversity of such data improve the generalization abilities of in-context learners beyond their training domain, while achieving comparable performance with models trained on a single large-scale dataset. We investigate this paradigm by leveraging meta-learning to train an in-context learner on the Meta-Album collection under several settings. Firstly, we show the performance in a controlled environment, where the test domain is completely excluded from the training knowledge. Secondly, we explore the robustness of these models to forgetting in a continual scenario where the information is accessible for a limited time. Finally, we explore the more challenging unsupervised scenario. Our findings demonstrate that transformers still generalize for in-context prediction when trained on a curated dataset collection while offering advantages in modularity and replaceability.

</details>


### [329] [Reconstructing Template-Memorized Images from Natural Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.07947)
*Sol Yarkoni,Mahmood Sharif,Roi Livni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低资源、低数据依赖的对抗性攻击方法，利用从电商网站抓取的数据中模板化布局与文本-图像关联的脆弱性，能够通过看似无害的提示（如“蓝色中性T恤”）重建训练数据中的人物面部等敏感内容，揭示了扩散模型在隐私与版权方面的潜在风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型（如扩散模型）存在隐私泄露、版权侵犯和数据管理问题，而现有的训练数据重建攻击方法通常需要高计算资源、部分训练集访问权限或精心设计的提示，缺乏一种低资源、低假设条件下即可实施的有效攻击手段。

Method: 利用从电商网站抓取的数据中模板化布局与模式化文本提示之间的紧密关联这一领域知识，设计低资源攻击，识别看似良性的提示以重建训练数据中的图像，并结合真实世界提示数据发现记忆的视觉元素。

Result: 例如，在现有模型中，提示“blue Unisex T-Shirt”能够生成真实人物的面部；结合真实提示数据，发现了可重现记忆视觉元素的提示。

Conclusion: 该方法暴露了使用抓取电商数据训练生成模型时存在的基础性脆弱性，即模板化布局与模式化文本提示的关联可能导致隐私泄露，且此类重建可能无意中发生，甚至对非专业用户亦然。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative models, such as diffusion models, have raised concerns related to privacy, copyright infringement, and data stewardship. To better understand and control these risks, prior work has introduced techniques and attacks that reconstruct images, or parts of images, from training data. While these results demonstrate that training data can be recovered, existing methods often rely on high computational resources, partial access to the training set, or carefully engineered prompts.
  In this work, we present a new attack that requires low resources, assumes little to no access to the training data, and identifies seemingly benign prompts that can lead to potentially risky image reconstruction. We further show that such reconstructions may occur unintentionally, even for users without specialized knowledge. For example, we observe that for one existing model, the prompt ``blue Unisex T-Shirt'' generates the face of a real individual. Moreover, by combining the identified vulnerabilities with real-world prompt data, we discover prompts that reproduce memorized visual elements.
  Our approach builds on insights from prior work and leverages domain knowledge to expose a fundamental vulnerability arising from the use of scraped e-commerce data, where templated layouts and images are closely tied to pattern-like textual prompts.
  The code for our attack is publicly available at https://github.com/TheSolY/lr-tmi.

</details>


### [330] [One Token to Fool LLM-as-a-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.08794)
*Yulai Zhao,Haolin Liu,Dian Yu,Sunyuan Kung,Meijia Chen,Haitao Mi,Dong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现即使是基于参考的生成式奖励模型（如RLVR）也容易受到"万能钥匙"攻击——通过添加无意义符号或通用推理开头词即可骗取虚假高奖励分数。作者提出了一种数据增强策略（使用截断模型输出作为对抗负样本）来训练主奖励模型（Master-RMs），在保持标准评估性能的同时实现了最先进的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示基于参考的生成式奖励模型（如RLVR）中存在的系统性奖励破解漏洞，挑战已有假设中"参考范式足够鲁棒"的观点。

Method: 1) 发现"万能钥匙"攻击：无意义符号（如":"、"."）或通用推理开头词（如"Thought process:"）即可骗取高奖励；2) 提出数据增强策略：使用截断模型输出作为对抗负样本；3) 训练Master Reward Models (Master-RMs)。

Result: "万能钥匙"攻击广泛影响多种模型，包括GPT-o1和Claude-4等闭源系统；Master-RMs在标准评估中保持高性能，同时实现最先进的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LLM作为自动评判者的可靠性受到挑战，但通过对抗性数据增强可以显著提升鲁棒性；研究为未来鲁棒LLM评估提供了方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly trusted as automated judges, assisting evaluation and providing reward signals for training other models, particularly in reference-based settings like Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR). However, we uncover a critical vulnerability even in this reference-based paradigm: generative reward models are systematically susceptible to reward hacking. We find that superficial inputs, which we term ''master keys'' such as non-word symbols (e.g., '':'' or ''.'') or generic reasoning openers (e.g., ''Thought process:'' or ''Let's solve this problem step by step.''), can consistently elicit false positive rewards without any substantive reasoning. Our systematic evaluation demonstrates this is a widespread failure affecting a diverse range of models, including leading proprietary systems such as GPT-o1 and Claude-4. These results challenge the assumed robustness of LLM judges and pose a significant threat to their reliability. To address this, we propose a simple yet effective data augmentation strategy using truncated model outputs as adversarial negative examples. The resulting Master Reward Models (Master-RMs) demonstrate state-of-the-art robustness against these ''master key'' attacks while maintaining high performance in standard evaluation settings. We supplement these findings with a comprehensive analysis of the vulnerability across model scales, prompt variations, and common inference-time strategies, offering insights to guide future research on robust LLM evaluation. We release our robust, general-domain reward models and the synthetic training data at https://huggingface.co/sarosavo/Master-RM and https://huggingface.co/datasets/sarosavo/Master-RM.

</details>


### [331] [Decoding the Multimodal Maze: A Systematic Review on the Adoption of Explainability in Multimodal Attention-based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.04427)
*Md Raisul Kibria,Sébastien Lafond,Janan Arslan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了2020年1月至2024年初期间关于多模态模型可解释性的研究，发现现有工作主要集中在视觉-语言和纯语言模型上，注意力机制是最常用的解释方法，但评估方法缺乏系统性、一致性和鲁棒性，未能充分考虑模态间交互和模态特定的认知上下文因素。基于分析，作者提出了促进多模态XAI研究标准化评估和报告实践的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多模态学习（尤其是基于注意力的模型）的显著进展，以及可解释人工智能（XAI）需求的增长，有必要系统梳理多模态模型可解释性研究的现状，识别现有方法的不足，特别是在评估方法上的系统性缺失，以推动更可解释、负责任的多模态AI系统发展。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，分析2020年1月至2024年初发表的多模态模型可解释性研究，从模型架构、所涉及模态、解释算法和评估方法等多个维度进行审视，并整合近期新兴进展进行补充分析。

Result: 发现大多数研究集中在视觉-语言和纯语言模型上；注意力机制是最常用的解释方法，但难以充分捕捉模态间的完整交互；跨领域的架构异质性加剧了解释难度；评估方法普遍缺乏系统性、一致性、鲁棒性，且未充分考虑模态特定的认知和上下文因素。

Conclusion: 提出了一套综合性建议，旨在推动多模态XAI研究中采用更严谨、透明和标准化的评估与报告实践，以支持未来构建以可解释性为核心、更可解释、可问责和负责任的多模态AI系统。

Abstract: Multimodal learning has witnessed remarkable advancements in recent years, particularly with the integration of attention-based models, leading to significant performance gains across a variety of tasks. Parallel to this progress, the demand for explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) has spurred a growing body of research aimed at interpreting the complex decision-making processes of these models. This systematic literature review analyzes research published between January 2020 and early 2024 that focuses on the explainability of multimodal models. Framed within the broader goals of XAI, we examine the literature across multiple dimensions, including model architecture, modalities involved, explanation algorithms and evaluation methodologies. Our analysis reveals that most studies are concentrated on vision-language and language-only models, with attention-based techniques being the most commonly employed for explanation. However, these methods often fall short in capturing the full spectrum of interactions between modalities, a challenge further compounded by the architectural heterogeneity across domains. Importantly, we find that evaluation methods for XAI in multimodal settings are largely non-systematic, lacking consistency, robustness, and consideration for modality-specific cognitive and contextual factors. To address these gaps, we not only synthesize findings from the surveyed works but also incorporate a complementary analysis that integrates recent and emerging advances driving multimodal explainability. Based on these insights, we provide a comprehensive set of recommendations aimed at promoting rigorous, transparent, and standardized evaluation and reporting practices in multimodal XAI research. Our goal is to support future research in more interpretable, accountable, and responsible multimodal AI systems, with explainability at their core.

</details>


### [332] [Retrieval-Augmented Foundation Models for Water Level Prediction in the Everglades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.04888)
*Rahuul Rangaraj,Jimeng Shi,Rajendra Paudel,Giri Narasimhan,Yanzhao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种检索增强机制（WaterRAF），通过从历史观测档案中检索相似水文 episodes 来改进预训练时间序列基础模型在 Everglades 水位预测中的表现，实验表明该方法能持续提升长时程预测精度，尤其在极端事件中提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的时间序列基础模型在 Everglades 水域水位预测这一领域特定任务上表现有限，其领域适用性尚未被充分理解，亟需提升模型在该场景下的预测能力。

Method: 构建了 Everglades 水域特定数据集；采用检索增强机制，从历史观测档案中检索相似多变量水文 episodes 作为预训练模型的输入上下文；研究了基于统计相似性和基于互信息的两种检索策略。

Result: 检索增强能持续提升长时程水位预测精度；在极端事件期间获得不成比例的更大增益；两种检索策略均有效。

Conclusion: 基于类比的历史检索能够有效增强预训练时间序列基础模型在环境科学中的应用效果；该框架具有通用性，可推广至其他水文系统；为模型在水文预测中的优势、局限和失败模式提供了实证见解。

Abstract: Accurate water level forecasting in the Everglades is essential for flood mitigation, drought management, water resource planning, and biodiversity conservation. While recent time-series foundation models have shown strong performance on generic tasks (represented in their pre-training), their effectiveness in domain-specific applications remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we curate a domain-specific dataset for water-level forecasting in the Everglades and observe that the performance of current state-of-the-art models remains limited. To address this gap, we leverage a retrieval-augmented mechanism that retrieves analogous multivariate hydrological episodes from an external archive of historical observations to enrich the input context of those pre-trained models. We study two retrieval strategies, statistical similarity-based retrieval and mutual information-based retrieval, and analyze how incorporating retrieved historical contexts affects predictive performance. Extensive experiments show that retrieval augmentation consistently improves long-horizon water level forecasts and yields disproportionately larger gains during extreme events, which is particularly critical for environmental decision-making. Our study provides empirical evidence that analog-based retrieval can benefit pretrained time-series foundation models in environmental science, offering practical insights into their strengths, limitations, and failure modes when applied to hydrological forecasting in the Everglades. Although evaluated in the Everglades, the proposed framework is general and can be applied to other hydrological systems given time series data. The code and data have been made publicly available at https://github.com/rahuul2992000/WaterRAF.

</details>


### [333] [Equivariant Flow Matching for Symmetry-Breaking Bifurcation Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.03340)
*Fleur Hendriks,Ondřej Rokoš,Martin Doškář,Marc G. D. Geers,Vlado Menkovski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用生成式AI（流匹配）结合等变架构和最优传输耦合机制，对非线性动力系统中的分岔现象导致的多重稳定解进行概率建模，在梁屈曲、Allen-Cahn方程等系统中验证了该方法能准确捕捉多模态分布和对称性破缺分岔，显著优于非概率和变分方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性动力系统中的分岔现象常产生多个共存的稳定解（尤其在对称性破缺时），而确定性机器学习模型无法捕捉这种多重性，会平均化不同解并丢失低对称性结果。

Method: 将流匹配与等变架构结合，并引入基于最优传输的耦合机制；进一步将等变流匹配推广为对称耦合策略，使预测与目标输出在群作用下对齐，从而实现等变设置下的准确学习。

Result: 在从简单概念系统到物理问题（梁屈曲、Allen-Cahn方程）的多个系统中，该方法准确捕捉了多模态分布和对称性破缺分岔，且显著优于非概率方法和变分方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为高维系统中的多重稳定性建模提供了一种有原则且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Bifurcation phenomena in nonlinear dynamical systems often lead to multiple coexisting stable solutions, particularly in the presence of symmetry breaking. Deterministic machine learning models are unable to capture this multiplicity, averaging over solutions and failing to represent lower-symmetry outcomes. In this work, we formalize the use of generative AI, specifically flow matching, as a principled way to model the full probability distribution over bifurcation outcomes. Our approach builds on existing techniques by combining flow matching with equivariant architectures and an optimal-transport-based coupling mechanism. We generalize equivariant flow matching to a symmetric coupling strategy that aligns predicted and target outputs under group actions, allowing accurate learning in equivariant settings. We validate our approach on a range of systems, from simple conceptual systems to physical problems such as buckling beams and the Allen--Cahn equation. The results demonstrate that the approach accurately captures multimodal distributions and symmetry-breaking bifurcations. Moreover, our results demonstrate that flow matching significantly outperforms non-probabilistic and variational methods. This offers a principled and scalable solution for modeling multistability in high-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [334] [PLaID++: A Preference Aligned Language Model for Targeted Inorganic Materials Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.07150)
*Andy Xu,Rohan Desai,Larry Wang,Ethan Ritz,Gabriel Hope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PLaID++ 是一种基于 LLM 后训练（RLVR）的晶体生成模型，通过对称性感知的 Wyckoff 文本表示和温度缩放熵正则化，在材料生成中实现了比先前方法高约 50% 的热力学稳定、独特且新颖的结构生成率，并能按空间群属性条件生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多科学问题（如材料生成）中，目标不是产生单一正确答案，而是生成满足约束条件的多样化候选集合。现有 RLVR 方法主要针对答案正确性优化，缺乏对多样性和约束满足的支持。

Method: 1) 引入对称性感知的 Wyckoff 文本表示（紧凑、对称约束编码）；2) 将温度缩放作为熵正则化项，对抗模式坍缩并鼓励探索；3) 采用 RLVR 后训练，通过奖励函数引导模型输出向理想化学空间偏移。

Result: PLaID++ 生成热力学稳定、独特且新颖的结构的比率比先前方法高约 50%，并能按指定空间群属性条件生成晶体结构。

Conclusion: 将 NLP 领域的后训练技术（RLVR、温度正则化）适配到材料设计是可行的，为定向高效发现新材料铺平了道路。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a promising approach to improve correctness in LLMs, however, in many scientific problems, the objective is not necessarily to produce the correct answer, but instead to produce a diverse array of candidates which satisfy a set of constraints. We study this challenge in the context of materials generation. To this end, we introduce PLaID++, an LLM post-trained for stable and property-guided crystal generation. We find that performance hinges on our crystallographic representation and reward formulation. First, we introduce a compact, symmetry-informed Wyckoff text representation which improves computational efficiency and encourages generalization from physical priors. Second, we demonstrate that temperature scaling acts as an entropy regularizer which counteracts mode collapse and encourages exploration. By encoding symmetry constraints directly into text and guiding model outputs towards desirable chemical space, PLaID++ generates structures that are thermodynamically stable, unique, and novel at a $\sim$50\% greater rate than prior methods and conditionally generates structures with desired space group properties. Our work demonstrates the potential of adapting post-training techniques from natural language processing to materials design, paving the way for targeted and efficient discovery of novel materials.

</details>


### [335] [Structuring The Future: Diffusion LLM Speculative Decoding via Calibrated Draft Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18085)
*Sudhanshu Agrawal,Risheek Garrepalli,Raghavv Goel,Christopher Lott,Fatih Porikli,Mingu Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Spiffy是一种针对扩散LLM的投机解码算法，通过有向草稿图和离线校准实现高达8.6倍推理减少和6.3倍token速率加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散LLM相比自回归LLM具有更高token生成速率的潜力，但缺乏有效的推理加速方法。现有投机解码技术主要针对AR-LLM设计，无法直接应用于dLLM。

Method: Spiffy采用自动投机消除独立草稿模型开销，设计新颖的有向草稿图结构利用dLLM的双向块状生成特性，离线校准最大化接受率，推理时动态剪枝提升计算效率。

Result: 结合KV缓存和基于阈值的动态掩码，在LLaDA、Dream和SDAR模型上实现最多8.6倍模型推理减少和6.3倍token速率加速。

Conclusion: Spiffy是首个将投机解码成功应用于扩散LLM的算法，在保持输出分布不变的前提下显著提升了推理速度。

Abstract: Diffusion LLMs (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a powerful alternative to autoregressive LLMs (AR-LLMs) with the potential to operate at significantly higher token-generation rates. To unlock this potential, we present Spiffy, a speculative decoding algorithm to accelerate dLLM inference while provably preserving the model's output distribution. This work addresses the unique challenges involved in applying ideas from speculative decoding of AR-LLMs to dLLMs. Spiffy performs auto-speculation to eliminate the overheads of an independent draft model, structuring draft states in the form of a novel directed draft graph to take advantage of the bidirectional, blockwise nature of dLLM generation. These draft graphs are calibrated offline to maximize acceptance rates and are dynamically pruned during inference for improved computational efficiency. We present a detailed formulation of Spiffy and demonstrate its ability to accelerate LLaDA, Dream, and SDAR models in combination with KV caching and threshold-based dynamic unmasking leading to up to $8.6\times$ reduction in model inferences and $6.3\times$ acceleration in token rate.

</details>


### [336] [Metriplectic Conditional Flow Matching for Dissipative Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19526)
*Ali Baheri,Lars Lindemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MCFM通过将保守-耗散分裂嵌入向量场和保结构采样器，学习不违反第一原理的耗散动力学，避免神经代理注入能量导致的不稳定性，在受控机械基准上实现了更准确的相图和更少的能量增加事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经代理模型（neural surrogates）在长时间展开时常常会注入能量并导致不稳定，这是因为它们没有内嵌物理系统的保守-耗散结构。MCFM旨在学习耗散动力学的同时不违反热力学第一原理，确保长期rollout的稳定性。

Method: MCFM将保守-耗散分裂同时构建在向量场和保结构采样器中；通过条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）在短轨迹上训练，避免长时间rollout的伴随计算；推理时采用Strang-prox方案，交替进行辛更新（symplectic update）和近端度量步（proximal metric step），确保离散能量衰减；可选择投影机制在已知可信能量时强制执行严格衰减。

Result: 在受控机械基准上，MCFM生成的相图更接近真实值，能量增加和正能量率事件显著少于同等表达能力的无约束神经流，同时匹配了终端分布拟合质量。

Conclusion: MCFM在连续和离散时间上提供了守恒、单调耗散和稳定rollout的理论保证，是一种结合物理归纳偏置与流匹配框架的有效方法。

Abstract: Metriplectic conditional flow matching (MCFM) learns dissipative dynamics without violating first principles. Neural surrogates often inject energy and destabilize long-horizon rollouts; MCFM instead builds the conservative-dissipative split into both the vector field and a structure preserving sampler. MCFM trains via conditional flow matching on short transitions, avoiding long rollout adjoints. In inference, a Strang-prox scheme alternates a symplectic update with a proximal metric step, ensuring discrete energy decay; an optional projection enforces strict decay when a trusted energy is available. We provide continuous and discrete time guarantees linking this parameterization and sampler to conservation, monotonic dissipation, and stable rollouts. On a controlled mechanical benchmark, MCFM yields phase portraits closer to ground truth and markedly fewer energy-increase and positive energy rate events than an equally expressive unconstrained neural flow, while matching terminal distributional fit.

</details>


### [337] [BrainPro: Towards Large-scale Brain State-aware EEG Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22050)
*Yi Ding,Muyun Jiang,Weibang Jiang,Shuailei Zhang,Xinliang Zhou,Chenyu Liu,Shanglin Li,Yong Li,Cuntai Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BrainPro 是一个大型EEG基础模型，通过检索式空间学习实现跨布局空间对齐，并采用脑状态解耦模块并行学习共享和特定状态表征，在9个公共BCI数据集上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型基于自注意力机制，无法保留位置特异性信息，难以对齐不同通道配置的信号；同时脑状态包含共享和状态特异的区域活动，现有模型仅学习共享表征，不足以支撑多样化的下游解码任务。

Method: 提出BrainPro模型：1) 检索式空间学习机制实现跨布局空间对齐；2) 脑状态解耦模块通过并行编码器和区域感知重构学习共享与状态特异性表征；3) 在大规模EEG语料上预训练。

Result: 在9个公共BCI数据集（涵盖情绪、运动、语音、压力、精神疾病和注意力任务）上达到最优性能，空间滤波器、通道缺失鲁棒性和编码器贡献分析验证了空间对齐和状态感知通路有效性。

Conclusion: BrainPro在EEG空间模式的可解释性和表征质量上均有显著提升，能够有效服务于多样化的脑电解码任务。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) reflects underlying brain states, whose activities are distributed across brain regions and manifest as spatial patterns on the scalp. Learning these spatially structured, state-related patterns requires consistent spatial representations across datasets. However, existing EEG foundation models are typically based on self-attention, which does not preserve location-specific information and struggles to align signals recorded with different channel configurations. Moreover, brain states contain both shared and state-specific regional activity, suggesting that learning neurophysiologically plausible, state-aware representations can complement the shared representations targeted by current models and improve downstream decoding. To address these limitations, we propose BrainPro, a large EEG model that combines a retrieval-based spatial learning mechanism for cross-layout spatial alignment with a brain state-decoupling module that learns both shared and state-specific representations through parallel encoders and region-aware reconstruction. Pre-trained on a large EEG corpus, BrainPro achieves state-of-the-art performance across nine public BCI datasets spanning emotion, motor, speech, stress, mental disease, and attention tasks. Analyses of spatial filters, channel-drop robustness, and encoder contributions further validate the effectiveness of its spatial alignment and state-aware pathways. These results show that BrainPro achieves improved interpretability of learned spatial patterns and produces representations that benefit diverse EEG decoding tasks.

</details>


### [338] [Toward General Digraph Contrastive Learning: A Dual Spatial Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16311)
*Zhengyu Wu,Daohan Su,Yang Zhang,Xunkai Li,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: S2-DiGCL是一种面向有向图的新型对比学习框架，通过复域（磁拉普拉斯个性化扰动）和实域（基于路径的子图增强）两个空间视角，构建高质量正负样本，在有向图节点分类和链接预测任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图对比学习（GCL）方法主要关注无向图，忽略了真实网络（如社交网络、推荐系统）中至关重要且有向的边方向信息。

Method: 从复域视角引入磁拉普拉斯的个性化扰动来自适应调制边相位和方向语义；从实域视角采用基于路径的子图增强策略捕获细粒度局部非对称性和拓扑依赖关系；联合两种互补空间视图构建高质量正负样本。

Result: 在7个真实有向图数据集上，节点分类提升4.41%，链接预测提升4.34%，在监督和无监督设置下均达到SOTA。

Conclusion: S2-DiGCL通过联合利用复域和实域的空间视角，有效提升了有向图对比学习的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Graph Contrastive Learning (GCL) has emerged as a powerful tool for extracting consistent representations from graphs, independent of labeled information. However, existing methods predominantly focus on undirected graphs, disregarding the pivotal directional information that is fundamental and indispensable in real-world networks (e.g., social networks and recommendations).In this paper, we introduce S2-DiGCL, a novel framework that emphasizes spatial insights from complex and real domain perspectives for directed graph (digraph) contrastive learning. From the complex-domain perspective, S2-DiGCL introduces personalized perturbations into the magnetic Laplacian to adaptively modulate edge phases and directional semantics. From the real-domain perspective, it employs a path-based subgraph augmentation strategy to capture fine-grained local asymmetries and topological dependencies. By jointly leveraging these two complementary spatial views, S2-DiGCL constructs high-quality positive and negative samples, leading to more general and robust digraph contrastive learning. Extensive experiments on 7 real-world digraph datasets demonstrate the superiority of our approach, achieving SOTA performance with 4.41% improvement in node classification and 4.34% in link prediction under both supervised and unsupervised settings.

</details>


### [339] [From Isolation to Entanglement: When Do Interpretability Methods Identify and Disentangle Known Concepts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15134)
*Aaron Mueller,Andrew Lee,Shruti Joshi,Ekdeep Singh Lubana,Dhanya Sridhar,Patrik Reizinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多概念评估框架，发现稀疏自编码器和探针等特征化方法虽能生成对单一概念敏感的特征，但概念往往分布在多个特征上；在理想化设置中，操控单个特征常影响多个概念，表明相关性指标不足以证明操控选择性，多概念评估对可解释性研究至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性研究中，特征质量通常在孤立条件下评估，并隐含独立假设，但这些假设在实践中未必成立。因此，常见的特征化方法（如SAEs和探针）能否真正解耦不同概念尚不清楚。

Method: 构建包含情感、领域、语态、时态等多个概念的多概念评估设置，评估特征化方法生成解耦表示的能力，并通过特征操控实验测量各概念是否可独立操控以及特征间是否存在交互作用。

Result: 特征通常只对单一概念敏感，但概念分布在多个特征上；在理想化设置中，操控一个特征往往影响多个概念，尽管特征间几乎没有交互效应。

Conclusion: 相关性指标不足以证明操控选择性，两个特征分属不同空间也不足以保证它们对单一概念具有选择性，强调了多概念评估在可解释性研究中的重要性。

Abstract: A goal of interpretability is to recover disentangled representations of latent concepts (features) from the activations of neural networks. The quality of features is typically evaluated in isolation, and under implicit independence assumptions that may not hold in practice. Thus, it is unclear to what extent common featurization methods such as sparse autoencoders (SAEs) and probes disentangle one concept from another. We propose a multi-concept evaluation setting using concepts including sentiment, domain, voice, and tense. We evaluate how well featurizers produce disentangled representations of each concept, observing that features are typically sensitive to only one concept, but also that concepts are distributed across many features. Then, we steer these features, measuring whether each concept is independently manipulable, and whether features interact. Even in idealized settings, steering a feature often affects many concepts, despite a near absence of interaction effects. These results suggest that correlational metrics are insufficient to establish steering selectivity, and that demonstrating that two features operate in separate spaces is insufficient to claim that they will be selective for one concept. These results underscore the importance of multi-concept evaluations in interpretability research.

</details>


### [340] [Cluster Aggregated GAN (CAG): A Cluster-Based Hybrid Model for Appliance Pattern Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22287)
*Zikun Guo,Adeyinka. P. Adedigba,Rammohan Mallipeddi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Cluster Aggregated GAN框架，通过将间歇性和连续性家电分流到专门分支（间歇性设备使用聚类模块分配专用生成器，连续性设备使用LSTM生成器），在UVIC数据集上优于基线方法，显著提升了合成负荷数据的真实性、多样性和训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成家电数据对非侵入式负荷监测（NILM）和隐私保护能源研究至关重要，但标注数据集稀缺。现有GAN方法将所有设备统一建模，忽视间歇性与连续性设备的行为差异，导致训练不稳定、输出 fidelity 有限。

Method: 提出Cluster Aggregated GAN框架：1）间歇性设备分支——聚类模块将相似激活模式分组，为每个集群分配专用生成器；2）连续性设备分支——使用LSTM生成器通过序列压缩捕获渐进时间演化；3）混合生成架构将不同设备路由到对应分支。

Result: 在UVIC智能插头数据集上，该框架在真实性、多样性和训练稳定性指标上持续优于基线方法；聚类作为主动生成组件显著提升了可解释性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: Cluster Aggregated GAN框架是一种有效的合成负荷生成方法，为NILM研究提供了高质量数据支持。

Abstract: Synthetic appliance data are essential for developing non-intrusive load monitoring algorithms and enabling privacy preserving energy research, yet the scarcity of labeled datasets remains a significant barrier. Recent GAN-based methods have demonstrated the feasibility of synthesizing load patterns, but most existing approaches treat all devices uniformly within a single model, neglecting the behavioral differences between intermittent and continuous appliances and resulting in unstable training and limited output fidelity. To address these limitations, we propose the Cluster Aggregated GAN framework, a hybrid generative approach that routes each appliance to a specialized branch based on its behavioral characteristics. For intermittent appliances, a clustering module groups similar activation patterns and allocates dedicated generators for each cluster, ensuring that both common and rare operational modes receive adequate modeling capacity. Continuous appliances follow a separate branch that employs an LSTM-based generator to capture gradual temporal evolution while maintaining training stability through sequence compression. Extensive experiments on the UVIC smart plug dataset demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms baseline methods across metrics measuring realism, diversity, and training stability, and that integrating clustering as an active generative component substantially improves both interpretability and scalability. These findings establish the proposed framework as an effective approach for synthetic load generation in non-intrusive load monitoring research.

</details>


### [341] [Geometric and Quantum Kernel Methods for Predicting Skeletal Muscle Outcomes in chronic obstructive pulmonary disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.00921)
*Azadeh Alavi,Hamidreza Khalili,Stanley H. Chan,Fatemeh Kouchmeshki,Muhammad Usman,Ross Vlahos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于核几何的量子混合方法，用于利用生物标志物预测COPD小鼠的骨骼肌功能。在肌肉重量预测上，该方法相比最佳经典方法RMSE降低了约1.8%，但未达到统计显著性。肌肉质量预测也获得最佳数值结果，而力的预测中经典Ridge回归表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COPD影响全球数亿人，其骨骼肌功能障碍具有重要临床意义。量子机器学习在生物医学预测中的价值，尤其是在小样本生物标志物队列中，需要与强经典基线方法进行基准对比验证。

Method: 提出核几何量子混合方法：将合成的对称正定参考映射到再生核希尔伯特空间，通过仅训练集的随机投影进行压缩、归一化，然后输入低维量子回归电路。使用条件分层重复交叉验证进行评估，对比经典岭回归/核模型、SPD关系表示和量子核回归。

Result: 肌肉重量预测中，所提方法的平均RMSE最低，比最佳经典比较器低约1.8%，但配对折级检验经Holm校正后未达到统计显著性。肌肉质量预测中该方法也获得最低数值RMSE。力预测中，仅使用生物标志物的Ridge回归表现最佳。

Conclusion: 量子混合方法在小样本生物标志物队列中展现出数值优势，但统计显著性不足。不同终点（肌肉重量、质量、力）的最优方法不同，提示终点线性结构差异。该方法在生物学有意义的终点上具有潜在价值，但需进一步验证。

Abstract: Chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (COPD) affects hundreds of millions of people worldwide, and skeletal-muscle dysfunction is clinically important. Quantum machine learning is increasingly explored for biomedical prediction, but its value in small biomarker cohorts requires benchmarking against strong classical baselines. We analysed a cigarette-smoke COPD cohort of 213 animals with blood and bronchoalveolar-lavage biomarkers to predict tibialis anterior muscle weight, muscle quality, and force. We developed a kernel-geometric quantum hybrid method in which synthetic symmetric positive definite (SPD) references are mapped through a reproducing kernel Hilbert space, compressed using train-only random projection, normalised, and supplied to low-dimensional quantum regression circuits. We benchmarked this approach against classical ridge/kernel models, SPD relational representations, and quantum-kernel regression (QKR). All methods were evaluated using condition-stratified repeated cross-validation. The largest numerical improvement was observed for muscle weight, where the proposed method had the numerically lowest mean root mean squared error (RMSE), approximately 1.8% below the best classical comparator; paired fold-level testing did not establish statistically significant superiority after Holm adjustment, but the endpoint is biologically meaningful. The method also had the numerically lowest mean RMSE for muscle quality. For force, biomarker-only Ridge performed best, suggesting a more linear endpoint structure.

</details>


### [342] [FedBiCross: Personalized One-Shot Federated Learning on Medical Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01901)
*Yuexuan Xia,Yinghao Zhang,Yalin Liu,Hong-Ning Dai,Yong Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedBiCross通过客户端聚类、双层跨簇优化和个性化蒸馏，在数据无知识蒸馏的单轮联邦学习中解决了非独立同分布数据下预测冲突导致的软标签信息稀释问题，在四个医学图像数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OSFL方法在非独立同分布数据下聚合所有客户端的预测形成全局教师时，冲突的预测在平均过程中相互稀释，产生信息量较低的软标签，削弱了蒸馏效果。

Method: 提出FedBiCross框架，包含三个阶段：(1) 根据模型输出相似性对客户端聚类形成子集成；(2) 双层跨簇优化，学习自适应权重选择性利用有益的跨簇知识同时抑制负迁移；(3) 个性化蒸馏实现客户端特定适应。

Result: 在四个医学图像数据集上，FedBiCross在不同非独立同分布程度下均一致优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: FedBiCross通过聚类和跨簇知识的选择性利用，有效缓解了非独立同分布数据下的知识冲突问题，提升了单轮联邦学习的蒸馏效果。

Abstract: Data-free knowledge distillation-based one-shot federated learning (OSFL) trains a model in a single communication round without sharing raw data, making OSFL attractive for privacy-sensitive medical applications. However, existing methods aggregate predictions from all clients to form a global teacher. Under non-IID data, conflicting predictions dilute each other during averaging, yielding less informative soft labels that weaken distillation. We propose FedBiCross, a personalized OSFL framework with three stages: (1) clustering clients by model output similarity to form coherent sub-ensembles, (2) bi-level cross-cluster optimization that learns adaptive weights to selectively leverage beneficial cross-cluster knowledge while suppressing negative transfer, and (3) personalized distillation for client-specific adaptation. Experiments on four medical image datasets demonstrate that FedBiCross consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across different non-IID degrees.

</details>


### [343] [Decentralized Autoregressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03184)
*Stepan Maschan,Haoxuan Qu,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文正式建立了去中心化自回归生成与集中式训练之间的理论等价性，通过离散流匹配框架证明全局模型可自然分解为独立专家，并在多模态基准上实验验证了去中心化训练与集中式架构的性能对等性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化自回归生成作为解决扩展瓶颈的方案引起了广泛关注，但该范式缺乏严格的理论依据。本文旨在为去中心化训练提供理论正当性。

Method: 采用离散流匹配（Discrete Flow Matching）框架，利用其固有性质证明全局模型自然分解为独立专家，从而建立去中心化与集中式训练的理论等价性。

Result: 在多模态基准上的大量实验验证了去中心化训练与标准集中式架构在性能上保持竞争性对等。

Conclusion: 去中心化训练从理论上等价于集中式训练，且在实践中能达到与之相当的性能表现，为大规模自回归生成系统的去中心化部署提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The decentralization of autoregressive generation has attracted considerable attention in recent years as a solution to scaling bottlenecks. However, despite promising empirical results, this paradigm currently lacks rigorous theoretical justification. In this work, we formally establish the theoretical equivalence between decentralized and centralized training. To achieve this, we adapt the Discrete Flow Matching framework for autoregressive generation, leveraging its inherent properties to demonstrate that global models naturally decompose into independent experts. Finally, we conduct extensive experiments across diverse multimodal benchmarks, empirically validating that decentralized training maintains competitive parity with standard centralized architectures.

</details>


### [344] [When Smaller Wins: Dual-Stage Distillation and Pareto-Guided Compression of Liquid Neural Networks for Edge Battery Prognostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06227)
*Dhivya Dharshini Kannan,Wei Li,Wei Zhang,Jianbiao Wang,Zhi Wei Seh,Man-Fai Ng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DLNet提出了一种双阶段液态神经网络蒸馏框架，将高容量模型压缩为可在Arduino Nano等边缘设备上部署的紧凑模型，用于电池健康预测。最终部署的学生模型在预测未来100个周期的电池健康时误差仅为0.0066，比教师模型低15.4%，模型大小从616 kB缩减至94 kB（减少84.7%），每次推理仅需21毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电池管理系统需要在严格的设备端约束下实现准确的电池健康预测，现有高容量模型难以直接部署到资源受限的边缘设备上。

Method: 采用欧拉离散化将液态网络动力学子以重构以兼容嵌入式设备；通过双阶段知识蒸馏传递教师模型的时序行为并在进一步压缩后恢复性能；利用帕累托引导的多目标选择保留在精度与效率之间取得平衡的学生模型。

Result: 预测未来100个周期的电池健康误差为0.0066，比教师模型低15.4%；模型大小从616 kB降至94 kB（减少84.7%）；在Arduino Nano 33 BLE Sense上推理时间为21毫秒。

Conclusion: 验证了"小模型优于大模型"的观察：在适当的监督与选择下，紧凑模型在边缘端预测任务上能够达到甚至超越大型教师模型的表现。该框架可推广至其他具有严格硬件约束的工业分析任务。

Abstract: Battery management systems increasingly require accurate battery health prognostics under strict on-device constraints. This paper presents DLNet, a practical framework with dual-stage distillation of liquid neural networks that turns a high-capacity model into compact and edge-deployable models for battery health prediction. DLNet first applies Euler discretization to reformulate liquid dynamics for embedded compatibility. It then performs dual-stage knowledge distillation to transfer the teacher model's temporal behavior and recover it after further compression. Pareto-guided selection under joint error-cost objectives retains student models that balance accuracy and efficiency. We evaluate DLNet on a widely used dataset and validate real-device feasibility on an Arduino Nano 33 BLE Sense using int8 deployment. The final deployed student achieves a low error of 0.0066 when predicting battery health over the next 100 cycles, which is 15.4% lower than the teacher model. It reduces the model size from 616 kB to 94 kB with 84.7% reduction and takes 21 ms per inference on the device. These results support a practical smaller wins observation that a small model can match or exceed a large teacher for edge-based prognostics with proper supervision and selection. Beyond batteries, the DLNet framework can extend to other industrial analytics tasks with strict hardware constraints.

</details>


### [345] [Hellinger Multimodal Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06572)
*Huyen Vo,Isabel Valera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过概率意见池化（Hölder池化）重新审视多模态变分自编码器的推理过程，提出基于Hellinger矩匹配近似的HELVAE模型，在避免子采样的同时实现了更具表达力的潜在表示，并在生成一致性与质量之间取得了更优的平衡，超越了现有最先进的多模态VAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流多模态VAE方法（PoE、MoE及其组合）在聚合各模态推理分布时存在局限性，本文希望从优化视角（概率意见池化）出发，探索更优的多模态后验近似方法。

Method: 从Hölder池化（α=0.5，即α-散度族中唯一的对称成员）出发，推导出Hellinger矩匹配近似，并基于此提出HELVAE模型，该模型无需子采样即可高效训练。

Result: HELVAE模型能够随着更多模态的观察学习到更具表达力的潜在表示，并在生成一致性与生成质量之间实现更优的权衡，在多个基准上超越现有最先进的多模态VAE。

Conclusion: 基于Hellinger矩匹配近似的多模态VAE是一种高效且有效的替代方案，为多模态生成式学习提供了新的优化视角。

Abstract: Multimodal variational autoencoders (VAEs) are widely used for weakly supervised generative learning with multiple modalities. Predominant methods aggregate unimodal inference distributions using either a product of experts (PoE), a mixture of experts (MoE), or their combinations to approximate the joint posterior. In this work, we revisit multimodal inference through the lens of probabilistic opinion pooling, an optimization-based approach. We start from Hölder pooling with $α=0.5$, which corresponds to the unique symmetric member of the $α\text{-divergence}$ family, and derive a moment-matching approximation, termed Hellinger. We then leverage such an approximation to propose HELVAE, a multimodal VAE that avoids sub-sampling, yielding an efficient yet effective model that: (i) learns more expressive latent representations as additional modalities are observed; and (ii) empirically achieves better trade-offs between generative coherence and quality, outperforming state-of-the-art multimodal VAE models.

</details>


### [346] [Contrastive Geometric Learning Unlocks Unified Structure- and Ligand-Based Drug Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.09693)
*Lisa Schneckenreiter,Sohvi Luukkonen,Lukas Friedrich,Daniel Kuhn,Günter Klambauer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ConGLUDe，一个通过对比几何学习统一基于结构和配体的计算药物设计模型。该模型使用几何蛋白质编码器生成全蛋白质表示和隐式结合位点嵌入，结合快速配体编码器，通过对比学习对齐配体与全局蛋白质表示及多个候选结合位点，无需预定义口袋。模型支持虚拟筛选、靶标钓鱼和配体条件口袋预测，在多项基准测试中展现竞争性能，尤其在新颖靶标钓鱼任务上大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于结构和基于配体的计算药物设计依赖不同的数据源和建模假设，限制了它们在规模化场景下的联合使用。现有方法通常需要预定义的结合口袋，且难以在同一框架下统一处理结构和配体信息。

Method: ConGLUDe采用对比几何学习框架，包含一个几何蛋白质编码器（生成全蛋白质表征和隐式预测的结合位点嵌入）和一个快速配体编码器。通过对比学习，将配体同时与全局蛋白质表示和多个候选结合位点对齐，无需预定义口袋。模型在蛋白质-配体复合物和大规模生物活性数据上联合训练。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，ConGLUDe在零样本虚拟筛选上取得有竞争力的性能；在新颖靶标钓鱼任务上大幅超越现有方法；在配体条件口袋选择任务上达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 统一的结构-配体训练具有显著优势，ConGLUDe可作为药物发现领域通用基础模型的重要一步。

Abstract: Structure-based and ligand-based computational drug design have traditionally relied on disjoint data sources and modeling assumptions, limiting their joint use at scale. In this work, we introduce Contrastive Geometric Learning for Unified Computational Drug Design (ConGLUDe), a single contrastive geometric model that unifies structure- and ligand-based training. ConGLUDe couples a geometric protein encoder that produces whole-protein representations and implicit embeddings of predicted binding sites with a fast ligand encoder, removing the need for predefined pockets. By aligning ligands with both global protein representations and multiple candidate binding sites through contrastive learning, ConGLUDe supports ligand-conditioned pocket prediction in addition to virtual screening and target fishing, while being trained jointly on protein-ligand complexes and large-scale bioactivity data. Across diverse benchmarks, ConGLUDe achieves competitive zero-shot virtual screening performance, substantially outperforms existing methods on a challenging target fishing task, and demonstrates state-of-the-art ligand-conditioned pocket selection. These results highlight the advantages of unified structure-ligand training and position ConGLUDe as a step toward general-purpose foundation models for drug discovery.

</details>


### [347] [Data-driven Lake Water Quality Forecasting for Time Series with Missing Data using Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15503)
*Rishit Chatterjee,Tahiya Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对志愿者主导的湖泊监测中产生的不规则、季节性且存在大量缺失值的时间序列数据，研究了Secchi盘深度（SDD）的预测问题。采用MICE处理缺失值，在六个候选模型中岭回归表现最优。通过分析最小样本量和最小特征集，提出联合可行性函数，发现在5%精度容忍度下，仅需每湖约64个近期样本和单一预测因子即可达到目标，为湖泊研究者提供了简单高效的采样策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 志愿者主导的湖泊监测数据存在不规则、季节性、大量缺失等问题（如冰盖、天气限制、人为错误），给有害藻华预警和预测带来困难，需要开发适用于此类数据特点的预测方法并量化最低数据需求。

Method: 采用MICE处理缺失值，用nMAE作为跨湖可比指标；比较六种候选模型（岭回归最优）；通过向后最近历史协议量化最小样本量；识别最小特征集；引入联合可行性函数统一考虑样本量和特征数。

Result: 岭回归在六种模型中测试性能最优；向后最近历史协议下约176个训练样本可达全历史精度5%以内；四个特征子集可匹配十三个特征的基线；联合可行性函数表明约64个近期样本和单一预测因子即可满足5%精度目标。

Conclusion: 提出的联合可行性策略将最近历史长度和特征选择统一在固定精度目标下，为湖泊研究者提供了设置采样频率和测量优先级的简单高效规则，证明了针对性监测的实用性。

Abstract: Volunteer-led lake monitoring yields irregular, seasonal time series with many gaps arising from ice cover, weather-related access constraints, and occasional human errors, complicating forecasting and early warning of harmful algal blooms. We study Secchi Disk Depth (SDD) forecasting on a 30-lake, data-rich subset drawn from three decades of in-situ records collected across Maine lakes. Missingness is handled via Multiple Imputation by Chained Equations (MICE), and we evaluate performance with a normalized Mean Absolute Error (nMAE) metric for cross-lake comparability. Among six candidates, ridge regression provides the best mean test performance. Using ridge regression, we then quantify the minimal sample size, showing that under a backward, recent-history protocol, the model reaches within 5% of full-history accuracy with approximately 176 training samples per lake on average. We also identify a minimal feature set, where a compact four-feature subset matches the thirteen-feature baseline within the same 5% tolerance. Bringing these results together, we introduce a joint feasibility function that identifies the minimal training history and fewest predictors sufficient to achieve the target of staying within 5% of the complete-history, full-feature baseline. In our study, meeting the 5% accuracy target required about 64 recent samples and just one predictor per lake, highlighting the practicality of targeted monitoring. Hence, our joint feasibility strategy unifies recent-history length and feature choice under a fixed accuracy target, yielding a simple, efficient rule for setting sampling effort and measurement priorities for lake researchers.

</details>


### [348] [Kareus: Joint Reduction of Dynamic and Static Energy in Large Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.17654)
*Ruofan Wu,Jae-Won Chung,Mosharaf Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kareus训练系统，通过细粒度内核调度与频率缩放联合优化动态和静态能耗，在相同训练时间下节能高达28.3%，或相同能耗下缩短训练时间27.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI计算需求快速增长，但能源供给跟不上，能源成为昂贵且需显式管理的资源。现有优化工作仅分别关注动态或静态能耗，缺乏对二者联合优化的研究。

Method: 发现细粒度内核调度与频率缩放对动态和静态能耗存在联合且相互依赖的影响；将不可解的联合优化问题分解为局部的、基于分区的子问题；使用多通路多目标优化算法寻找帕累托最优的执行调度方案。

Result: 与现有最优方法相比，Kareus在相同训练时间下减少高达28.3%的能耗，在相同能耗下减少高达27.5%的训练时间。

Conclusion: Kareus通过联合优化动态与静态能耗，有效拓展了时间-能耗权衡边界，实现了显著的能效提升。

Abstract: The computing demand of AI is growing at an unprecedented rate, but energy supply is not keeping pace. As a result, energy has become an expensive and contended resource that requires explicit management and optimization. Although recent works have made significant progress in large model training optimization, they focus on optimizing either dynamic or static energy consumption.
  We find that fine-grained kernel scheduling and frequency scaling jointly and interdependently impact both dynamic and static energy consumption. Based on this finding, we design Kareus, a training system that pushes the time-energy tradeoff frontier by optimizing both aspects. Kareus decomposes the intractable joint optimization problem into local, partition-based subproblems. It then uses a multi-pass multi-objective optimization algorithm to find execution schedules that push the time-energy tradeoff frontier. Compared to the state of the art, Kareus reduces training energy by up to 28.3% at the same training time, or reduces training time by up to 27.5% at the same energy consumption.

</details>


### [349] [Generalized Schrödinger Bridge on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04675)
*Panagiotis Theodoropoulos,Juno Nam,Evangelos Theodorou,Jaemoo Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出广义图上的薛定谔桥框架（GSBoG），一种可扩展的数据驱动方法，用于在任意图上学习可执行的连续时间马尔可夫链策略，在满足端点边际分布的同时优化中间状态成本，解决了现有图传输方法在表达能力、泛化性和可扩展性上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图传输方法依赖严格假设、难以泛化到稀疏拓扑、且随图规模和时间范围扩展性差，缺乏学习可执行策略的表达能力。

Method: 提出GSBoG框架，通过似然优化方法学习轨迹级策略，避免密集全局求解器，在满足端点边际分布的同时优化中间状态依赖的运行成本。

Result: 在多种真实世界图拓扑上的实验表明，GSBoG能够可靠地学习准确且尊重拓扑结构的策略，同时优化特定应用的中间状态成本。

Conclusion: GSBoG具有广泛的适用性，为通用图上的成本感知动态传输开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Transportation on graphs is a fundamental challenge across many domains, where decisions must respect topological and operational constraints. Despite the need for actionable policies, existing graph-transport methods lack this expressivity. They rely on restrictive assumptions, fail to generalize across sparse topologies, and scale poorly with graph size and time horizon. To address these issues, we introduce Generalized Schrödinger Bridge on Graphs (GSBoG), a novel scalable data-driven framework for learning executable controlled continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) policies on arbitrary graphs under state cost augmented dynamics. Notably, GSBoG learns trajectory-level policies, avoiding dense global solvers and thereby enhancing scalability. This is achieved via a likelihood optimization approach, satisfying the endpoint marginals, while simultaneously optimizing intermediate behavior under state-dependent running costs. Extensive experimentation on challenging real-world graph topologies shows that GSBoG reliably learns accurate, topology-respecting policies while optimizing application-specific intermediate state costs, highlighting its broad applicability and paving new avenues for cost-aware dynamical transport on general graphs.

</details>


### [350] [GEMSS: A Variational Bayesian Method for Discovering Multiple Sparse Solutions in Classification and Regression Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08913)
*Kateřina Henclová,Václav Šmídl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GEMSS算法，一种能同时发现多个多样化的稀疏特征组合的变分方法，在高维、欠定且高度相关的系统中优于五种主流特征选择方法，并在代谢组学和物理化学的实际数据中验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维、欠定且高度相关的系统中存在多个等价的稀疏特征子集，传统方法只能找到单一解，无法揭示所有合理的解释，这限制了对潜在机制的深入理解。

Method: GEMSS使用结构化的spike-and-slab先验实现稀疏性，混合高斯分布近似难以处理的多模态后验，并引入Jaccard惩罚项控制解的多样性，通过随机梯度下降优化单一目标函数。

Result: 在128个综合实验中，GEMSS始终优于五种通过ALFESE框架适配的主流特征选择方法；在代谢组学和物理化学的3个真实数据集上成功分离出多个不同但质量较高的解。

Conclusion: GEMSS能够有效发现多个多样的稀疏特征子集，解决了高维欠定系统中特征选择的多解问题，且已作为PyPI包发布，并提供无代码应用GEMSS Explorer。

Abstract: High-dimensional, underdetermined and highly correlated systems are common in data science practice, especially when analyzing physical measurements. In such settings, feature selection poses a fundamental challenge because multiple distinct sparse subsets may explain the response equally well. Their identification is crucial not only for predictive modeling but also for generating domain-specific insights into the underlying mechanisms. Yet, conventional methods typically isolate a single solution, obscuring the full spectrum of plausible explanations. This work introduces GEMSS (Gaussian Ensemble for Multiple Sparse Solutions), a variational algorithm designed to simultaneously discover multiple, diverse sparse feature combinations. The method employs a structured spike-and-slab prior for sparsity, a mixture of Gaussians to approximate the intractable multimodal posterior, and a Jaccard-based penalty to further control solution diversity. A single objective function is optimized via stochastic gradient descent. The method is tested on 128 comprehensive experiments by a novel benchmarking framework designed to generate artificial problems with multiple sparse solutions of equal predictive properties. This allows us to measure the retrieval of ground truth features rather than only evaluating predictive performance -- characteristics more fitting to our practical needs. A comparative analysis shows that GEMSS consistently outperforms five prominent feature selection methods adapted through the ALFESE framework. Finally, we demonstrate practical usability through 3 challenging real-world datasets from metabolomics and physical chemistry: GEMSS successfully isolates multiple distinct yet quality solutions. GEMSS is available as a PyPI package 'gemss'. The corresponding repository github.com/kat-er-ina/gemss/ includes the full codebase and a free, no-code application GEMSS Explorer.

</details>


### [351] [Hierarchical Successor Representation for Robust Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12753)
*Changmin Yu,Máté Lengyel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分层继代表征（HSR），通过时间抽象构建预测表征，克服了经典SR的策略依赖性和谱扩散问题。结合非负矩阵分解（NMF），HSR-NMF生成稀疏低秩状态表征，支持多隔间环境中对新任务的高效迁移学习，并发现可解释的拓扑结构。HSR还能驱动高效探索，扩展到大规模程序化生成环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典继代表征（SR）受限于策略依赖性——策略因持续学习、环境非平稳性和任务需求变化而改变，导致已建立的预测表征失效。此外，在拓扑复杂环境中，SR存在谱扩散问题，产生密集重叠且扩展性差的特征。

Method: 1) 提出分层继代表征（HSR），将时间抽象（temporal abstractions）融入预测表征的构建，学习对策略变化鲁棒的稳定状态特征；2) 对HSR应用非负矩阵分解（NMF），获得稀疏低秩的状态表征。

Result: HSR-NMF在复杂多隔间环境中实现了高样本效率的任务迁移；发现了可解释的拓扑结构；并能驱动高效探索，有效扩展到大型程序化生成环境。

Conclusion: HSR提供了一种策略无关的分层映射，有效桥接了无模型最优性和基于模型的灵活性，为任务迁移和高效探索提供了强大的表征基础。

Abstract: The successor representation (SR) provides a powerful framework for decoupling predictive dynamics from rewards, enabling rapid generalisation across reward configurations. However, the classical SR is limited by its inherent policy dependence: policies change due to ongoing learning, environmental non-stationarities, and changes in task demands, making established predictive representations obsolete. Furthermore, in topologically complex environments, SRs suffer from spectral diffusion, leading to dense and overlapping features that scale poorly. Here we propose the Hierarchical Successor Representation (HSR) for overcoming these limitations. By incorporating temporal abstractions into the construction of predictive representations, HSR learns stable state features which are robust to task-induced policy changes. Applying non-negative matrix factorisation (NMF) to the HSR yields a sparse, low-rank state representation that facilitates highly sample-efficient transfer to novel tasks in multi-compartmental environments. Further analysis reveals that HSR-NMF discovers interpretable topological structures, providing a policy-agnostic hierarchical map that effectively bridges model-free optimality and model-based flexibility. Beyond providing a useful basis for task-transfer, we show that HSR's temporally extended predictive structure can also be leveraged to drive efficient exploration, effectively scaling to large, procedurally generated environments.

</details>


### [352] [Structured vs. Unstructured Pruning: An Exponential Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02234)
*Davide Ferre',Frédéric Giroire,Frederik Mallmann-Trenn,Emanuele Natale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了强彩票假设（SLTH）在结构化剪枝（神经元剪枝）与无结构化剪枝（权重剪枝）之间的根本性差异，证明在近似单个ReLU神经元时，神经元剪枝需要Ω(1/ε)的初始网络规模，而权重剪枝仅需O(log(1/ε))，两者之间存在指数级分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLTH的理论结果几乎全部基于无结构剪枝，而结构化剪枝（如神经元剪枝）虽在实际中能直接带来硬件加速，却缺乏理论分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过近似单个无偏置ReLU神经元这一简单场景，揭示神经元剪枝的固有限制。

Method: 考虑在随机初始化的两层ReLU网络中，通过剪枝隐藏单元来近似单个无偏置ReLU神经元。分别分析神经元剪枝和权重剪枝所需的最小初始网络规模，推导出两种剪枝方式的下界和上界。

Result: 神经元剪枝实现ε-近似需要Ω(1/ε)的初始规模，而权重剪枝仅需O(log(1/ε))的隐藏单元，两种方法之间呈现指数级分离。

Conclusion: 神经元剪枝在理论上比权重剪枝效率低得多，这为实际中设计更高效的剪枝策略提供了理论指导，也揭示了SLTH在结构化剪枝场景下的局限性。

Abstract: The Strong Lottery Ticket Hypothesis (SLTH) states that large, randomly initialized neural networks contain sparse subnetworks capable of approximating a target function at initialization without training, suggesting that pruning alone is sufficient. Pruning methods are typically classified as unstructured, where individual weights can be removed from the network, and structured, where parameters are removed according to specific patterns, as in neuron pruning. Existing theoretical results supporting the SLTH rely almost exclusively on unstructured pruning, showing that logarithmic overparameterization suffices to approximate simple target networks. In contrast, neuron pruning has received limited theoretical attention, despite its practical appeal for direct hardware speedups. In this work, we consider the problem of approximating a single bias-free ReLU neuron by pruning hidden units of a randomly initialized two-layer ReLU network, effectively isolating the intrinsic limitations of neuron pruning. We show that achieving an $\varepsilon$-approximation requires a starting network size of $Ω(1/\varepsilon)$ for neuron pruning, whereas weight pruning succeeds with only $O(\log(1/\varepsilon))$ hidden units, revealing an exponential separation between the two approaches.

</details>


### [353] [Echo2ECG: Enhancing ECG Representations with Cardiac Morphology from Multi-View Echos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08505)
*Michelle Espranita Liman,Özgün Turgut,Alexander Müller,Eimo Martens,Daniel Rueckert,Philip Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Echo2ECG框架，通过多视图超声心动图的自监督学习增强心电图表征，使ECG能够预测心脏形态学表型，在结构表型分类和Echo检索任务上超越现有方法，且模型体积小18倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECG成本低、广泛应用但无法直接测量心脏形态学表型（如LVEF）；现有自监督方法仅将ECG与单视图Echo对齐，存在表征不匹配问题，无法捕捉完整解剖结构。

Method: 提出Echo2ECG多模态自监督学习框架，通过将ECG与多视图Echo对齐，丰富ECG表征中蕴含的心脏形态结构信息。

Result: 在结构心脏表型分类（三个数据集）和Echo形态学检索两项任务上，ECG表征一致优于SOTA单模态和多模态基线方法，且模型体积比最大基线小18倍。

Conclusion: Echo2ECG是鲁棒且强大的ECG特征提取器，能够从ECG中有效提取心脏形态信息，实现早期可及的健康筛查。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG) is a low-cost, widely used modality for diagnosing electrical abnormalities like atrial fibrillation by capturing the heart's electrical activity. However, it cannot directly measure cardiac morphological phenotypes, such as left ventricular ejection fraction (LVEF), which typically require echocardiography (Echo). Predicting these phenotypes from ECG would enable early, accessible health screening. Existing self-supervised methods suffer from a representational mismatch by aligning ECGs to single-view Echos, which only capture local, spatially restricted anatomical snapshots. To address this, we propose Echo2ECG, a multimodal self-supervised learning framework that enriches ECG representations with the heart's morphological structure captured in multi-view Echos. We evaluate Echo2ECG as an ECG feature extractor on two clinically relevant tasks that fundamentally require morphological information: (1) classification of structural cardiac phenotypes across three datasets, and (2) retrieval of Echo studies with similar morphological characteristics using ECG queries. Our extracted ECG representations consistently outperform those of state-of-the-art unimodal and multimodal baselines across both tasks, despite being 18x smaller than the largest baseline. These results demonstrate that Echo2ECG is a robust, powerful ECG feature extractor. Our code is accessible at https://github.com/michelleespranita/Echo2ECG.

</details>


### [354] [Differentiable Thermodynamic Phase-Equilibria for Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11249)
*Karim K. Ben Hicham,Moreno Ascani,Jan G. Rittig,Alexander Mitsos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DISCOMAX，一种可微分的相平衡计算算法，通过离散相态枚举与掩码softmax聚合结合直通梯度估计器，保证热力学一致性，在液液相平衡数据上优于现有替代方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理一致的机器学习方法在活度系数建模中表现良好，但将其推广到基于极值原理的相平衡数据（如液液相平衡）仍然困难。现有方法难以保证热力学一致性。

Method: 提出DISCOMAX算法：在正向传播中枚举离散相态并传播真实平衡态，在反向传播中使用掩码softmax聚合结合直通梯度估计器，实现物理一致的端到端学习。该方法与统计热力学存在类比关系。

Result: 在二元液液相平衡数据上，DISCOMAX优于现有基于替代模型的方法。

Conclusion: DISCOMAX提供了一个通用框架，可以从不同类型的相平衡数据中学习，在保证热力学一致性的同时实现端到端训练。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of phase equilibria remains a central challenge in chemical engineering. Physics-consistent machine learning methods that incorporate thermodynamic structure into neural networks have recently shown strong performance for activity-coefficient modeling. However, extending such approaches to equilibrium data arising from an extremum principle, such as liquid-liquid equilibria, remains difficult. Here we present DISCOMAX, a differentiable algorithm for phase-equilibrium calculation that guarantees thermodynamic consistency at both training and inference, only subject to a user-specified discretization. The method combines discrete enumeration of feasible phase states with masked softmax aggregation in the backward pass, with the propagation of the true equilibrium state in the forward pass, using a straight-through gradient estimator to enable physics-consistent end-to-end learning of neural \gls{gE}-models. We show that this approach bears analogy to statistical thermodynamics, and we evaluate it on binary liquid-liquid equilibrium data where it outperforms existing surrogate-based methods, while offering a general framework for learning from different kinds of equilibrium data.

</details>


### [355] [ARROW: Augmented Replay for RObust World models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11395)
*Abdulaziz Alyahya,Abdallah Al Siyabi,Markus R. Ernst,Luke Yang,Levin Kuhlmann,Gideon Kowadlo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARROW是一种受神经科学启发的基于模型的持续强化学习算法，通过增强DreamerV3的记忆高效分布匹配回放缓冲区，在无共享结构任务上显著减少遗忘，同时保持前向迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续强化学习方法大多依赖基于模型无关的方法和回放缓冲区来缓解灾难性遗忘，但这些方法面临内存需求大的可扩展性挑战。

Method: ARROW扩展DreamerV3，采用双重互补缓冲区：短期缓冲区存储近期经验，长期缓冲区通过智能采样保持任务多样性，实现记忆高效的分布匹配回放。

Result: 在Atari（无共享结构）和Procgen CoinRun（有共享结构）两个持续RL设定下，与同等规模的模型无关和基于模型的基线相比，ARROW在无共享结构任务上遗忘显著减少，同时保持可比的前向迁移性能。

Conclusion: 基于模型的强化学习和生物启发式方法在持续强化学习中具有潜力，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Continual reinforcement learning challenges agents to acquire new skills while retaining previously learned ones with the goal of improving performance in both past and future tasks. Most existing approaches rely on model-free methods with replay buffers to mitigate catastrophic forgetting; however, these solutions often face significant scalability challenges due to large memory demands. Drawing inspiration from neuroscience, where the brain replays experiences to a predictive World Model rather than directly to the policy, we present ARROW (Augmented Replay for RObust World models), a model-based continual RL algorithm that extends DreamerV3 with a memory-efficient, distribution-matching replay buffer. Unlike standard fixed-size FIFO buffers, ARROW maintains two complementary buffers: a short-term buffer for recent experiences and a long-term buffer that preserves task diversity through intelligent sampling. We evaluate ARROW on two challenging continual RL settings: Tasks without shared structure (Atari), and tasks with shared structure, where knowledge transfer is possible (Procgen CoinRun variants). Compared to model-free and model-based baselines with replay buffers of the same-size, ARROW demonstrates substantially less forgetting on tasks without shared structure, while maintaining comparable forward transfer. Our findings highlight the potential of model-based RL and bio-inspired approaches for continual reinforcement learning, warranting further research.

</details>


### [356] [Grammar of the Wave: Towards Explainable Multivariate Time Series Event Detection via Neuro-Symbolic VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11479)
*Sky Chenwei Wan,Yifei Y. Wang,Tianjun Hou,Xiqing Chang,Aymeric Jan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种语言引导的时间序列事件检测方法，通过事件逻辑树（ELT）将自然语言描述转化为结构化时序逻辑，并利用神经符号VLM代理框架SELA实现零/少样本事件检测，在能源和气候领域基准上超越监督微调和现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列事件检测（TSED）在关键领域有重要应用，但事件通常由自然语言定义且具有内部时序逻辑结构，而密集的事件标注成本高昂，纯监督学习难以实现。

Method: 提出Event Logic Tree (ELT)将文本描述转化为信号基元上的结构化时序逻辑；在此基础上构建SELA神经符号VLM代理框架，通过迭代从信号可视化中基元并组合成ELT约束，生成事件区间和树形解释。

Result: 在能源和气候领域构建的真实基准数据集上，SELA在零/少样本设定下优于有监督微调和现有时间序列推理基线。

Conclusion: 语言引导的TSED结合ELT与神经符号方法可有效利用文本描述实现事件检测，降低对标注数据的依赖，且生成的结构化解释具有可解释性。

Abstract: Time Series Event Detection (TSED) aims to localize semantically meaningful events in time series data, with critical applications in high-stakes domains. Unlike statistical anomalies, events are often defined by natural-language descriptions with internal temporal-logic structures across multiple physical channels. However, in real-world settings, dense event annotations are expensive to obtain, making purely supervised learning difficult. We introduce Language-guided TSED, a setting where a model is given textual event descriptions and must ground them to intervals in multivariate signals with little or no labeled data. To address this problem, we propose Event Logic Tree (ELT), a knowledge representation framework that converts linguistic descriptions into structured temporal logic over signal primitives. Building on ELT, we present SELA, a neuro-symbolic VLM agent framework that iteratively grounds primitives from signal visualizations and composes them under ELT constraints, producing both event intervals and faithful tree-structured explanations. We further release a real-world benchmark across energy and climate domains with expert knowledge and annotations. Experiments show that SELA improves over supervised fine-tuning and existing zero/few-shot time series reasoning baselines.

</details>


### [357] [Mixing Makes Markovian Contexts Cheap for Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12530)
*Kaan Buyukkalayci,Osama Hanna,Christina Fragouli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将马尔可夫上下文线性赌博机问题约简为标准线性赌博机的方法，通过构建平稳替代动作集和延迟更新策略，在均匀几何遍历性条件下实现了与底层线性赌博机相当的最坏情况遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"上下文廉价"的约简方法依赖于上下文独立同分布假设，无法推广到实践中常见的时域相关（如马尔可夫）上下文场景。本文旨在填补这一空白，将线性上下文赌博机的约简视角扩展到马尔可夫上下文过程。

Method: 1) 构建平稳替代动作集，利用均匀几何遍历性；2) 采用延迟更新方案控制非平稳条件上下文分布引起的偏差；3) 对于未知平稳分布，提出分阶段在线学习替代映射的算法。

Result: 在充分快速混合条件下，获得了与底层线性赌博机oracle匹配的高概率最坏情况遗憾界，并在实际数据集上验证了优于LinUCB基线的表现。

Conclusion: 成功将"上下文廉价"视角从独立同分布扩展到马尔可夫上下文，为处理时域相关上下文提供了理论保证和实用算法。

Abstract: Recent work shows that when contexts are drawn i.i.d., linear contextual bandits can be reduced to single-context linear bandits. This ``contexts are cheap'' perspective is highly advantageous, as it allows for sharper finite-time analyses and leverages mature techniques from the linear bandit literature, such as those for misspecification and adversarial corruption. However, this reduction crucially relies on the independence of contexts and does not extend to settings with temporally correlated (e.g., Markovian) contexts, which arise frequently in practice. Motivated by applications with temporally correlated availability, we extend this perspective to linear bandits with Markovian context processes, where the action set evolves via an exogenous Markov chain. Our main contribution is a reduction that applies under uniform geometric ergodicity. We construct a stationary surrogate action set to solve the problem using a standard linear bandit oracle, employing a delayed-update scheme to control the bias induced by the nonstationary conditional context distributions. We further provide a phased algorithm for unknown stationary distributions that learns the surrogate mapping online. In both settings, we obtain a high-probability worst-case regret bound matching that of the underlying linear bandit oracle in sufficiently fast mixing regimes. We then validate our results on a real-world instance, where we show practical gains over a LinUCB baseline.

</details>


### [358] [Towards One-for-All Anomaly Detection for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14407)
*Shiyuan Li,Yixin Liu,Yu Zheng,Xiaofeng Cao,Shirui Pan,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OFA-TAD，一种一次性训练即可跨域泛化的通用表格异常检测框架，通过多视角邻居距离表示和MoE评分网络实现无需数据集特定训练的高效检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格异常检测方法遵循"一个数据集一个模型"范式，需要针对每个数据集单独训练，计算成本高且对未见过的域泛化能力有限。

Method: OFA-TAD：1）提取邻居距离模式作为可迁移线索；2）引入多视图邻居距离表示（来自多个变换诱导的度量空间）以缓解变换敏感性；3）使用MoE评分网络进行视图特定异常评分和熵正则化门控融合；4）采用多策略异常合成机制支持单类约束下的训练。

Result: 在来自14个领域的34个数据集上，OFA-TAD在严格的OFA设置下实现了优越的异常检测性能和强大的跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: OFA-TAD验证了通用一次性训练范式在表格异常检测任务中的可行性，显著降低了计算开销并提升了跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Tabular anomaly detection (TAD) aims to identify samples that deviate from the majority in tabular data and is critical in many real-world applications. However, existing methods follow a ``one model for one dataset (OFO)'' paradigm, which relies on dataset-specific training and thus incurs high computational cost and yields limited generalization to unseen domains. To address these limitations, we propose OFA-TAD, a generalist one-for-all (OFA) TAD framework that only requires one-time training on multiple source datasets and can generalize to unseen datasets from diverse domains on-the-fly. To realize one-for-all tabular anomaly detection, OFA-TAD extracts neighbor-distance patterns as transferable cues, and introduces multi-view neighbor-distance representations from multiple transformation-induced metric spaces to mitigate the transformation sensitivity of distance profiles. To adaptively combine multi-view distance evidence, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) scoring network is employed for view-specific anomaly scoring and entropy-regularized gated fusion, with a multi-strategy anomaly synthesis mechanism to support training under the one-class constraint. Extensive experiments on 34 datasets from 14 domains demonstrate that OFA-TAD achieves superior anomaly detection performance and strong cross-domain generalizability under the strict OFA setting. The source code is available at https://github.com/Shiy-Li/OFA-TAD.

</details>


### [359] [Disentangling Dynamical Systems: Causal Representation Learning Meets Local Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14483)
*Markus W. Baumgartner,Anson Lei,Joe Watson,Ingmar Posner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文结合因果表示学习与变分推断，提出一种无需显式函数库即可从原始轨迹数据中解缠系统参数的新方法，并通过理论证明局部因果结构是实现完全可辨识性的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化系统辨识方法依赖预定义的候选函数库，缺乏灵活性；深度学习方法虽能建模复杂系统，但黑箱近似无法揭示系统的显式结构。本文旨在弥合两者鸿沟，实现无需结构假设的系统参数解缠。

Method: 利用因果表示学习推导新的可辨识性定理，给出系统参数从原始轨迹数据中唯一解缠（至多置换和微分同胚）的图准则；将系统参数辨识建模为变分推断问题，采用稀疏正则化Transformer学习状态依赖的因果结构。

Result: 在四个合成领域上验证，该方法能恢复高度解缠的表征，显著优于基线方法；实验结果证实局部因果结构对于完全可辨识性往往是必要的。

Conclusion: 全局因果结构提供了解缠保证的下界，而局部（状态依赖的）因果结构是实现完全可辨识性的关键；所提框架有效结合了系统辨识的可解释性与深度学习的表达能力。

Abstract: Parametric system identification methods estimate the parameters of explicitly defined physical systems from data. Yet, they remain constrained by the need to provide an explicit function space, typically through a predefined library of candidate functions chosen via available domain knowledge. In contrast, deep learning can demonstrably model systems of broad complexity with high fidelity, but black-box function approximation typically fails to yield explicit descriptive or disentangled representations revealing the structure of a system. We develop a novel identifiability theorem, leveraging causal representation learning, to uncover disentangled representations of system parameters without structural assumptions. We derive a graphical criterion specifying when system parameters can be uniquely disentangled from raw trajectory data, up to permutation and diffeomorphism. Crucially, our analysis demonstrates that global causal structures provide a lower bound on the disentanglement guarantees achievable when considering local state-dependent causal structures. We instantiate system parameter identification as a variational inference problem, leveraging a sparsity-regularised transformer to uncover state-dependent causal structures. We empirically validate our approach across four synthetic domains, demonstrating its ability to recover highly disentangled representations that baselines fail to recover. Corroborating our theoretical analysis, our results confirm that enforcing local causal structure is often necessary for full identifiability.

</details>


### [360] [Point-Identification of a Robust Predictor Under Latent Shift with Imperfect Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15158)
*Zahra Rahiminasab,Reza Soumi,Arto Klami,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在等价类（LECs）概念，在代理变量不完全（非单射）条件下，通过跨域秩条件实现鲁棒预测器的点识别，并引入主动查询多样域集的PQAL框架，在多个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于代理变量的域适应方法依赖强完备性假设（代理变量需充分捕捉隐混淆因子信息），但实际中代理变量往往不完美（非单射），导致预测器只能被集识别而非点识别。本文旨在放宽完备性假设，在更弱的条件下实现鲁棒预测器的点识别。

Method: 1) 提出潜在等价类（LECs）概念，将诱导相同条件代理分布的隐混淆因子聚为一类；2) 证明当多个域在混合LECs形成鲁棒预测器的方式上存在充分差异时（形式化为跨域混合权重的秩条件），点识别依然可达；3) 提出Proximal Quasi-Bayesian Active Learning (PQAL)框架，主动查询满足秩条件的少量多样化域，恢复点识别预测器。

Result: 在合成数据和半合成dSprites、IHDP、ACS Folktales数据集上，PQAL对不同程度的分布偏移展现出鲁棒性，并优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过LECs和跨域秩条件，本文显著放宽了代理变量域适应中的完备性假设；PQAL框架通过主动查询高效实现了点识别，为不完美代理下的鲁棒预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Addressing the domain adaptation problem becomes more challenging when distribution shifts across domains stem from latent confounders that affect both covariates and outcomes. Existing proxy-based approaches that address latent shift rely on a strong completeness assumption to uniquely determine (point-identify) a robust predictor. Completeness requires that proxies have sufficient information about variations in latent confounders. For imperfect proxies the mapping from confounders to the space of proxy distributions is non-injective, and multiple latent confounder values can generate the same proxy distribution. This breaks the completeness assumption and observed data are consistent with multiple potential predictors (set-identified). To address this, we introduce latent equivalent classes (LECs). LECs are defined as groups of latent confounders that induce the same conditional proxy distribution. We show that point-identification for the robust predictor remains achievable as long as multiple domains differ sufficiently in how they mix proxy-induced LECs to form the robust predictor. This domain diversity condition is formalized as a cross-domain rank condition on the mixture weights, which is substantially weaker assumption than completeness.
  We introduce the Proximal Quasi-Bayesian Active learning (PQAL) framework, which actively queries
  a small, targeted set of diverse domains that satisfy this rank condition. PQAL can recover the point-identified predictor, demonstrates robustness to varying degrees of shift and outperforms previous methods on synthetic data and semi-synthetic dSprites, IHDP, ACS Folktables datasets.

</details>


### [361] [Variational Graph Neural Networks for Uncertainty Quantification in Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29515)
*David Gonzalez,Alba Muixi,Beatriz Moya,Elias Cueto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种变分图神经网络（VGNN）架构，通过在解码器中引入变分层来建模权重概率分布，以较低成本同时估计认知不确定性和统计不确定性，并在固体力学逆问题中验证了该方法能高精度恢复物理参数并提供物理一致的置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数字孪生等关键应用中，深度学习方法不仅需要快速响应，还需提供可靠结果和置信度度量。传统确定性方法和经典深度神经网络无法量化预测不确定性，尤其在逆问题中解不唯一或数据含噪时问题更为突出。全贝叶斯网络计算成本过高，亟需一种高效的不确定性量化方法。

Method: 提出变分图神经网络（VGNN）架构，在解码器中策略性地引入变分层（而非整个网络），用于建模权重概率分布，从而以相对较低的计算成本同时估计认知不确定性（认知不确定性）和统计不确定性（偶然不确定性）。

Result: 在固体力学两个案例中验证：1）2D弹性问题中识别非线性分布的弹性模量；2）3D超弹性梁上载荷定位与量化。仅以位移场为输入，模型不仅高精度恢复物理参数，还提供与物理问题一致的置信区间，并能定位载荷位置并估计其值及置信区间。

Conclusion: VGNN在保持高精度的同时，能够以较低计算代价提供物理一致的置信区间，适用于需要不确定性量化的计算力学逆问题，为数字孪生等关键应用提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: The increasingly wide use of deep machine learning techniques in computational mechanics has significantly accelerated simulations of problems that were considered unapproachable just a few years ago. However, in critical applications such as Digital Twins for engineering or medicine, fast responses are not enough; reliable results must also be provided. In certain cases, traditional deterministic methods may not be optimal as they do not provide a measure of confidence in their predictions or results, especially in inverse problems where the solution may not be unique or the initial data may not be entirely reliable due to the presence of noise, for instance. Classic deep neural networks also lack a clear measure to quantify the uncertainty of their predictions. In this work, we present a variational graph neural network (VGNN) architecture that integrates variational layers into its architecture to model the probability distribution of weights. Unlike computationally expensive full Bayesian networks, our approach strategically introduces variational layers exclusively in the decoder, allowing us to estimate cognitive uncertainty and statistical uncertainty at a relatively lower cost.
  In this work, we validate the proposed methodology in two cases of solid mechanics: the identification of the value of the elastic modulus with nonlinear distribution in a 2D elastic problem and the location and quantification of the loads applied to a 3D hyperelastic beam, in both cases using only the displacement field of each test as input data. The results show that the model not only recovers the physical parameters with high precision, but also provides confidence intervals consistent with the physics of the problem, as well as being able to locate the position of the applied load and estimate its value, giving a confidence interval for that experiment.

</details>


### [362] [Allocating Human Oversight in AI-Enabled Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12497)
*Zikun Ye,Jiameng Lyu,Rui Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在异构且未知可靠性的AI辅助任务中，分配有限的人工验证预算。基于调优预测驱动推断框架，提出一种上置信界策略在线学习任务难度并引导验证流向AI最不可靠的任务。理论证明预算增长时效率损失趋于零，实验表明该方法在合成数据和真实数字孪生调查中显著优于均匀分配、epsilon-贪心及先探索后分配等策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业广泛部署AI作为客户决策中的低代价预测层，但AI生成信号在不同任务、产品和客户群体间的可靠性存在显著差异，因此仍需昂贵的人工验证来锚定AI输出。由于人工标注本身存在噪声，需要收集并平均多个标签，导致验证成本高昂。如何在可靠性异构且部署前未知的情况下，高效分配有限的人工验证预算，是本文要解决的核心问题。

Method: 将问题建模为调优预测驱动推断框架：每个人工标注同时优化AI辅助估计并揭示任务的校正难度（即AI预测作为控制变量后剩余的方差）。若难度已知，最优分配遵循Neyman平方根规则；由于难度未知，提出基于上置信界的在线学习策略，动态引导验证流向AI最不可靠的任务。

Result: 理论证明该策略的终端效率损失随预算增长收敛到零。合成实验和包含68个任务、2000余名受访者的真实数字孪生调查显示：在可靠性异构场景下，该方法显著优于均匀分配和epsilon-贪心策略；在调查数据上还优于"先探索后分配"的试点设计，将均匀分配的10-12%效率差距缩小到2-6%。

Conclusion: AI的价值不仅取决于模型准确性，还取决于将人工监督精准投向AI易出错环节的操作策略。本文提出的自适应预算分配策略在理论最优性和实践有效性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly deploy AI as a low-cost prediction layer in customer-facing decision processes, including demand sensing, service-quality monitoring, product testing, and market research, but AI-generated signals are unevenly reliable across tasks, products, and customer segments. Firms therefore still need scarce human validation (labels, audits, survey responses, or follow-up measurements) to anchor AI outputs to ground truth. Because human ground truth is itself noisy, varying across labelers and even across repeated judgments, the firm must collect and average several human labels per task, which makes human validation costly. We study how to allocate a limited human-validation budget across many AI-assisted tasks when reliability is heterogeneous and unknown before deployment. We cast this within tuned prediction-powered inference. Each human label both sharpens the AI-assisted estimate and reveals the task's rectification difficulty, the variance that remains after the AI prediction is optimally used as a control variate. If difficulties were known, the optimal allocation would follow a Neyman square-root rule; because they are unknown, we propose a policy based on upper confidence bounds that learns them online and steers validation toward tasks where AI is least reliable. We prove that the policy's terminal efficiency loss relative to the oracle allocation vanishes as the budget grows. In synthetic experiments and a real digital-twin survey with 68 tasks and over 2000 respondents, it closes most of the gap to the oracle when reliability is heterogeneous, outperforming uniform and epsilon-greedy allocation; on the survey data it also outperforms explore-then-commit pilot designs and cuts uniform's 10--12% gap to 2--6%. The value of AI depends not only on model accuracy but also on the operational policy that targets human oversight where AI errors matter most.

</details>


### [363] [Machine Learning-based Two-Stage Graph Sparsification for the Travelling Salesman Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20236)
*Bo-Cheng Lin,Yi Mei,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段TSP候选图稀疏化方法：先用α-Nearest和POPMUSIC的并集获得近完全召回（~6N条边），再训练轻量级分类器仅对单源边进行剪枝，最终在保持≥99.69%最优边的前提下将密度降低37-47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSP求解器依赖候选图稀疏化，但主流方法α-Nearest（高密度、稳定召回）和POPMUSIC（低密度但召回随规模衰减）各自存在密度-召回平衡缺陷；学习型方法需在完整图上评分，成本高且多限于欧几里得实例。

Method: 两阶段流水线：Stage1取α-Nearest和POPMUSIC的并集（~6N条边），利用双源标注为每条边记录来源（单源或双源）；Stage2仅对单源子集训练轻量级分类器并剪枝低分边，双源边直接保留。

Result: 在4种距离类型、5种空间分布、50-500节点规模上，候选图密度降低37-47%，最优边召回率≥99.69%；在TSP500上以更低密度达到或超越近期神经稀疏化方法的覆盖效果。

Conclusion: 通过将学习问题从全图分类简化为单源边过滤，实现了高效、通用（不限于欧几里得）的候选图稀疏化，在不牺牲求解质量的前提下显著降低密度。

Abstract: High-performance TSP solvers such as Lin-Kernighan-Helsgaun (LKH) search within a \emph{candidate graph} -- a small subset of edges pre-selected for the solver -- rather than over the complete graph. The two leading sparsification heuristics, $α$-Nearest and POPMUSIC, each fall short of the density-coverage balance: $α$-Nearest is dense with stable recall, while POPMUSIC is sparser but its recall degrades with scale. Their union closes the recall gap while remaining far below the complete graph in density, leaving room for further reduction. Existing learning-based sparsifiers score edges on the complete graph, an approach that is expensive and largely limited to Euclidean instances. We propose a two-stage method that inverts this logic. Stage~1 takes the union of $α$-Nearest and POPMUSIC, achieving near-perfect recall at ${\sim}6N$ edges. Crucially, the union annotates each edge with its \emph{source provenance} -- whether it was endorsed by $α$-Nearest, POPMUSIC, or both.
