Stage~2 trains a lightweight classifier on these annotated edges and prunes the lowest-scoring ones. Because dual-source edges are almost always optimal, the learning problem reduces to filtering the single-source subset -- a substantially easier task than classifying all $O(N^2)$ edges from scratch. Across four distance types, five spatial distributions, and problem sizes from 50 to 500, the pipeline reduces candidate-graph density by $37$-$47\%$ while retaining ${\geq}99.69\%$ of optimal-tour edges, and matches or exceeds the coverage of recent Euclidean-only neural sparsifiers at lower density at TSP500.

</details>


### [364] [Optimal Spatio-Temporal Decoupling for Bayesian Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00432)
*Yu-Hsueh Fang,Chia-Yen Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SA-BCP是一种状态自适应贝叶斯共形预测方法，通过门控凸组合平衡长期时间惯性和局部空间证据，实现快速适应分布漂移的同时保持稳定覆盖，在金融波动和天气数据集上相比现有方法取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线共形预测需要在快速适应分布漂移和稳定覆盖之间取得平衡：反馈驱动方法反应快但易波动，而强折扣贝叶斯方法滞后且在高覆盖率下区间过宽。现有方法难以同时兼顾适应速度与区间效率。

Method: 将预测分位数构建为长期时间惯性与核密度估计局部空间证据的门控凸组合，由单一可解释证据阈值K控制；推导了MSE最优阈值K*的闭式解；提出滚动原点在线选择K的程序，具有亚线性动态遗憾界。

Result: 在四个金融波动和天气数据集、三个目标覆盖率水平、八种基线方法上，SA-BCP在大多数设置中达到或超过名义覆盖率，同时产生显著更窄的预测区间——在最紧覆盖率下Winkler得分比折扣贝叶斯CP低约3倍。覆盖匹配审计确认效率提升并非欠覆盖的伪像。

Conclusion: SA-BCP有效平衡了适应性与稳定性，在多种实际应用中优于现有方法；局限是波动性专用的共形GARCH竞争方法在其原生波动率基线上效率更高，但无法跨领域迁移。

Abstract: Online conformal prediction must balance fast adaptation to distribution shift against stable coverage: feedback-driven methods react quickly but become volatile, while strongly discounted Bayesian methods lag and inflate intervals at tight coverage. We introduce \textbf{State-Adaptive Bayesian Conformal Prediction (SA-BCP)}, which forms the predictive quantile as a gated convex combination of long-term temporal inertia and local spatial evidence from a kernel density estimate, controlled by a single interpretable evidence threshold $K$. We establish three results: (i) asymptotic marginal validity of the resulting intervals; (ii) a closed-form expression for the MSE-optimal threshold, $K^*_{\mathrm{MSE}}=α(1-α)/M^{\mathcal{T}}$, trading the coverage-indicator (Bernoulli) variance against the temporal structural bias $M^{\mathcal{T}}$; and (iii) a rolling-origin procedure for selecting $K$ online -- consistent under stationarity, with $O(\sqrt{T\log N})$ regret against the best fixed $K$ and, for a segmented variant, a sublinear dynamic-regret bound under bounded drift. Across four financial-volatility and weather datasets, three target coverage levels, and eight baselines (including the strongest recent conditional-quantile methods, SPCI and KOWCPI), SA-BCP attains at-or-above-nominal coverage in most settings while producing substantially sharper intervals -- up to roughly $3\times$ lower Winkler score than discounted Bayesian CP at the tightest coverage -- and a coverage-matched audit confirms these efficiency gains are not an artifact of under-coverage. We disclose one principal limitation: a volatility-specialized conformal-GARCH competitor remains more efficient on its home volatility-base series, though it does not transfer across domains.

</details>


### [365] [The Safety-Aware Denoiser for Text Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08116)
*Amman Yusuf,Zhejun Jiang,Mijung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对文本扩散模型安全问题，提出安全感知去噪器（SAD），在推理阶段通过引导去噪过程将文本样本约束到可证明的安全区域，在不需重训练的情况下有效提升安全性，同时保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全方法主要面向自回归模型（如后处理过滤或推理时干预），不适用于文本扩散模型；文本扩散模型的安全控制尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出SAD框架，在迭代去噪过程中引入安全引导，使最终生成的文本样本被引导至文本空间中可证明安全的区域；该方法仅需推理时干预，无需重新训练扩散模型。

Result: 在危害分类、记忆化和越狱攻击等评估维度上，SAD显著降低了不安全文本生成，同时保持了生成质量、多样性和流畅性，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 去噪过程中的安全引导为文本扩散模型提供了一种有效且可扩展的安全性约束机制。

Abstract: Recent work on text diffusion models offers a promising alternative to autoregressive generation, but controlling their safety remains underexplored. Existing safety approaches are geared toward autoregressive models and typically rely on post-hoc filtering or inference-time interventions. These are inadequate for effectively addressing safety risks in text diffusion models. We propose the Safety-Aware Denoiser (SAD), a safety-guidance framework in text diffusion models. The SAD modifies the iterative denoising process such that the text sample at the final denoising step is steered toward provably safe regions of the text space. This inference-time method can integrate safety constraints into the denoiser, avoiding computationally expensive retraining of the underlying diffusion model and enabling flexible, lightweight safety guidance. We evaluate the safety of the generated text using the SAD, with respect to hazard taxonomy, memorization, and jailbreak. Experimental results show that SAD substantially reduces unsafe generations while preserving generation quality, diversity, and fluency, outperforming existing methods. These results demonstrate that our safety guidance during denoising provides an effective and scalable mechanism for enforcing safety in text diffusion models.

</details>


### [366] [COSMOS: Model-Agnostic Personalized Federated Learning with Clustered Server Models and Pseudo-Label-Only Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11165)
*Ben Rachmut,Luise Ge,William Yeoh,Ning Zhang,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COSMOS是一个模型无关的联邦学习框架，通过伪标签通信实现服务端个性化。客户端在公共数据上预测，服务端根据预测相似度聚类客户端，为每组训练专属模型，再蒸馏回客户端。理论证明蒸馏可产生指数级个性化风险收缩，实验表明在异构环境下优于所有模型无关的FL基线，与最先进的个性化FL方法竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在异构环境中面临客户端模型架构不同和数据分布不同的双重挑战，现有方法难以同时处理架构异构性和统计异构性。

Method: 客户端在公共数据上训练本地模型并进行预测；服务端根据预测相似度对客户端进行聚类，为每组使用自身算力训练聚类专属模型，然后将这些模型蒸馏回客户端。

Result: 在所有模型无关的FL基线上持续取得最优表现，与最先进的个性化FL方法相比也具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 基于伪标签的个性化服务端学习是异构环境下可扩展、模型无关联邦学习的一个有前景的新范式。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) in heterogeneous environments remains challenging because client models often differ in both architecture and data distribution. While recent approaches attempt to address this challenge through client clustering and knowledge distillation, simultaneously handling architectural and statistical heterogeneity remains difficult. We introduce COSMOS, a model-agnostic framework that enables server-side personalization using only pseudo-label communication. Clients train local models and predict on the public data; the server clusters clients by prediction similarity, trains a cluster-specific model for each group using its own compute, and distills the resulting models back to clients. We provide the first theoretical analysis showing that distillation from the learned cluster models can yield exponential personalization risk contraction, going beyond the convergence-to-stationarity guarantees typically provided in model-agnostic FL. Experiments across benchmarks demonstrate that COSMOS consistently outperforms all model-agnostic FL baselines while remaining competitive with state-of-the-art personalized FL methods. More broadly, our results highlight personalized server-side learning with pseudo-labels as a promising paradigm for scalable and model-agnostic federated learning in highly heterogeneous environments.

</details>


### [367] [Strategic PAC Learnability via Geometric Definability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13426)
*Yuval Filmus,Shay Moran,Elizaveta Nesterova,Nir Rosenfeld,Alexander Shlimovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究策略分类问题中，个体为影响分类器决策而修改特征的成本与样本复杂度关系。发现即使VC维为1的假设类，在简单区间邻域下也可能导致策略诱导类VC维无限，使可学习问题变为不可学习。通过引入一阶逻辑可定义性（$\mathbb{R}_{\mathtt{exp}}$）假设，证明可学习性得以保持，样本复杂度由定义公式的复杂度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究策略分类中诱导假设类的样本复杂度与原始假设类及成本结构复杂度的关系，解决现有研究仅覆盖特定场景（如线性分类器+范数成本）而缺乏一般性保证的问题，并揭示策略行为可能使原本简单的问题变得不可学习这一根本性挑战。

Method: 首先通过反例证明一般情形下策略行为会导致VC维爆炸（即使原始类VC维为1）。然后引入$\mathbb{R}_{\mathtt{exp}}$上的一阶逻辑可定义性假设，要求假设类和成本诱导的邻域关系均可由包含算术运算、指数、对数、比较的一阶公式定义，从而刻画广泛的自然类别和成本函数（如$\ell_p$距离、Wasserstein距离、信息散度），并在此假设下证明学习性保持。

Result: 证明了在没有可定义性假设时，即使最简单的设置（VC维1的假设类+区间邻域）也可能导致诱导类VC维无限，即学习性丧失。而在$\mathbb{R}_{\mathtt{exp}}$可定义性假设下，学习性得以保持，样本复杂度可由定义公式的复杂度进行控制。

Conclusion: 策略行为在一般情形下可能严重破坏学习性，但通过几何可定义性假设可以恢复学习保证，为广泛的实际成本函数（包括度量距离和散度）提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Strategic classification studies learning settings in which individuals can modify their features, at a cost, in order to influence the classifier's decision. A central question is how the sample complexity of the induced (strategic) hypothesis class depends on the complexities of the underlying hypothesis class and the cost structure governing feasible manipulations. Prior work has shown that in several natural settings, such as linear classifiers with norm costs, the induced complexity can be controlled. We begin by showing that such guarantees fail in general - even in simple cases: there exist hypothesis classes of VC dimension $1$ on the real line such that, even under the simplest interval neighborhoods, the induced class has infinite VC dimension. Thus, strategic behavior can turn an easy learning problem into a non-learnable one. To overcome this, we introduce structure via a geometric definability assumption: both the hypothesis class and the cost-induced neighborhood relation can be defined by first-order formulas over $\mathbb{R}_{\mathtt{exp}}$. Intuitively, this means that hypotheses and costs can be described using arithmetic operations, exponentiation, logarithms, and comparisons. This captures a broad range of natural classes and cost functions, including $\ell_p$ distances, Wasserstein distance, and information-theoretic divergences. Under this assumption, we prove that learnability is preserved, with sample complexity controlled by the complexity of the defining formulas.

</details>


### [368] [A Theory of Training Profit-Optimal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16430)
*Sophie Hao,William Merrill*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将缩放定律与微观经济学理论相结合，构建了一个LLM训练企业的经济模型，分析了在计算受限和数据受限两种场景下，模型规模、训练数据量与利润最优化的关系，并评估了当前行业训练支出趋势的经济合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已知扩大LLM规模能可靠地提升模型质量（以损失或下游评估衡量），但这些质量提升如何转化为潜在收入，以及收入增长能否抵消大规模训练和推理的成本，这一问题尚不清楚。因此需要建立经济模型来定量分析LLM训练企业的理性行为。

Method: 将缩放定律（scaling laws）与微观经济学理论结合，构建LLM训练企业的利润最大化模型。模型假设：模型质量随参数和训练token增加而提升，从而吸引更多消费者（每个消费者有质量阈值）；同时参数和训练token的增加带来额外成本。分别在计算受限和数据受限两种情景下分析利润最优化问题。

Result: 在计算受限场景下，最优模型规模和token预算与硬件效率（FLOPs/$）呈近线性关系，总训练成本随硬件效率呈次二次方增长；数据效率提升会激励更大模型和更多训练支出。在数据受限场景下，利润最优的训练支出与D^2/E成正比（D为数据量，E为硬件效率）。当前行业趋势在计算受限场景下与模型预测一致，但在数据受限场景或假设硬件进步停滞的情况下并非利润最优。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了LLM利润最优训练的理论框架，为批判性地审视行业声明和支撑长期经济决策奠定了基础。

Abstract: Scaling LLMs requires tremendous computational resources, and recent advances in AI have gone hand in hand with massive amounts of capital expenditure. While it is established that scaling up LLMs reliably increases model quality (quantified in terms of loss or downstream evaluations), it is unclear how these quality improvements translate to potential revenue, and whether revenue increases would offset costs of larger-scale training and inference. In this work, we develop an economic model for characterizing the rational behavior of an LLM training firm by combining scaling laws with microeconomic theory. Under our model of firm behavior, LLM quality can be increased with more parameters and training tokens, leading to more potential adoption by consumers, who each have a quality threshold for using the LLM. On the other hand, additional parameters and training tokens both incur additional costs. We analyze the profit maximization problem for this model under compute-bound and data-bound regimes. In the compute-bound regime, optimal model size and token budget track hardware efficiency $E$ (FLOPs/\$) at a near-linear rate; total training cost then scales sub-quadratically in $E$. Data efficiency improvements incentivize larger models and training expenditure. When we are limited to $D$ data, profit-optimal training expenditure scales as $D^2/E$, i.e, increase with data and decreases with hardware efficiency (as well as data efficiency). Finally, we analyze practical trends in training expenditure: current trends are consistent with our most permissive model variants in the compute-bound regime, but are not profit-optimal in the data-bound regime or assuming hardware advances will stall. Overall, our results provide a theory of profit-optimal LLM training, providing a foundation for engaging critically with industry statements and supporting long-term economic decision making.

</details>


### [369] [Attacking the First-Principle: A Black-Box, Query-Free Targeted Mimicry Attack on Binary Function Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18231)
*Gabriel Sauger,Jean-Yves Marion,Sazzadur Rahaman,Victor Matrat,Vincent Tourneur,Muaz Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Kelpie框架，首次在黑盒零查询场景下对二进制函数分类器执行模仿攻击，通过保持功能的代码变换实现目标逃逸，成功攻击六种先进模型并进行了实际验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于机器学习的二进制函数分类器易受对抗攻击，现有方法依赖于查询目标分类器且多为非定向逃逸攻击，缺乏更强大、更隐蔽的定向模仿攻击研究。

Method: 提出Kelpie框架，利用保持功能性的代码变换，在黑盒零查询设定下对恶意载荷进行改造，使其被目标分类器误分类为指定的良性类别，无需与分类器交互。

Result: Kelpie成功对六种不同架构的先进二进制函数分类器执行了模仿攻击，并通过将键盘记录器和擦除器隐藏在良性函数中的实际演示验证了攻击有效性。

Conclusion: 首次证明了黑盒零查询场景下的模仿攻击是可行的，对现有机器学习二进制函数分类器的可靠性和安全性提出了重要质疑。

Abstract: Binary function classifiers play a crucial role in maintaining the security and integrity of software systems by detecting malicious code and unauthorized modifications. However, machine learning-based classifiers are vulnerable to adversarial attacks that can evade detection. In this study, we present Kelpie, a novel framework for executing mimicry attacks, a stronger type of targeted evasion attacks, on binary function classifiers in a black-box, zero-query setting. Unlike previous approaches that rely on querying the target classifier to refine untargeted evasion attacks, Kelpie leverages code transformations that preserve the functionality of malicious payloads while causing them to be misclassified as we want. Through extensive experimentation, we demonstrate that Kelpie can successfully execute mimicry attacks against six state-of-the-art binary function classifiers representing different model architectures without requiring direct interaction with them. We further validate our approach with a practical demonstration, involving a keylogger and a wiper concealed within benign-looking functions embedded in an application. This work, to our best knowledge, is the first to demonstrate such a mimicry attack in a black-box, zero-query context, raising important questions about the reliability and security of existing machine learning-based binary function classifiers.

</details>


### [370] [Multi-Token Residual Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18817)
*Yufeng Xu,Zishuo Bao,Qian Wang,Zeshen Zhang,Haoqi Zhang,Bowen Peng,Ang Li,Rahul Chalamala,Yucheng Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多token残差预测（MRP）模块，利用扩散语言模型相邻去噪步logit分布高度相似的特性，通过从骨干网络隐状态预测步间残差实现单次前向传播中依赖感知的多token去噪。在静态解码场景下作为推测解码草稿模型实现1.4x无损加速；在动态解码场景下通过反悔机制恢复低阈值解码损失的大部分精度，在HumanEval和GSM8K上分别提升22.6和17.7个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型在解码时通过置信度阈值控制每步生成的token数量，但每步去噪更多token会导致质量单调下降，缺乏高效的依赖感知多token去噪方案。

Method: MRP模块利用相邻去噪步logit分布高度相似的性质，不额外运行骨干网络，而是从当前步的隐状态直接预测下一步logit的残差，从而在单次前向传播中完成多token去噪。在静态解码中用于推测解码的草稿模型并经过验证；在动态解码中驱动反悔机制撤销过早暴露的token。

Result: 静态解码：在SGLang中实现最高1.4x无损加速；动态解码：HumanEval上准确率提升22.6点，GSM8K上提升17.7点，有效恢复低阈值解码损失的精度。

Conclusion: MRP是一种轻量高效的模块，通过残差预测实现依赖感知的多token去噪，在保持或提升质量的同时显著提升扩散语言模型的解码效率，适用于不同的解码场景。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising masked token sequences, offering a tradeoff between parallelism and quality compared to autoregressive models. In current practice, the number of tokens decoded per step is controlled by a confidence threshold, and quality degrades monotonically as more tokens are denoised per step. We introduce Multi-token Residual Prediction (MRP), a lightweight module that enables dependency-aware multi-token denoising within a single backbone forward pass. MRP exploits a key property of the denoising process: the logit distributions at adjacent denoising steps are remarkably similar. Rather than running the backbone a second time to obtain the next-step logits, MRP predicts the residual between steps from the backbone's hidden states, effectively denoising more tokens per backbone forward at a fraction of the cost. We apply MRP across the two operating regimes of DLM decoding. In the high-quality-low-throughput static denoising regime, MRP serves as a drafter for speculative decoding: its proposals are verified against the backbone, yielding lossless acceleration of up to 1.4x in SGLang. In the low-quality-high-throughput dynamic denoising regime, MRP instead drives a remasking scheme that revokes over-eager reveals, recovering most of the accuracy lost to aggressive low-threshold decoding and improving accuracy by up to 22.6 points on code generation task HumanEval and 17.7 points on reasoning task GSM8K.

</details>


### [371] [ShapeBench: A Scalable Benchmark and Diagnostic Suite for Standardized Evaluation in Aerodynamic Shape Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20763)
*Shaghayegh Fazliani,Krissh Chawla,Jack Guo,Yiren Shen,Matthias Ihme,Madeleine Udell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ShapeBench是一个开源的气动外形优化基准测试框架，包含103个任务、8个形状类别和多种优化方法，揭示了不同任务间优化器排名差异巨大（平均Spearman ρ=0.013），且现有方法难以跨类别通用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气动外形优化(ASO)进展迅速，但缺乏标准化的评估框架，导致不同方法之间难以进行公平比较。

Method: 构建了包含103个任务、8个形状类别的统一基准ShapeBench，提供验证过的代理模型用于快速搜索和高保真CFD管道用于最终验证，引入了ShapeEvolve作为领域特化的进化LLM基线。

Result: 不同形状类别和问题形式下优化器排名差异巨大，平均Spearman ρ=0.013，说明单任务结论无法泛化；基准远未饱和，经典方法难以跨类别适用。

Conclusion: 需要更通用的优化方法，现有方法无法可靠地在不同问题类别间泛化。

Abstract: Rapid progress in aerodynamic shape optimization (ASO) has outpaced currently-available standardized evaluation frameworks. Fair comparison requires a unified benchmark spanning diverse shape classes, objective formulations, and matched-budget state-of-the-art baselines. We introduce ShapeBench, an open-source ASO benchmark with a unified API spanning 103 tasks across eight shape categories and multiple optimization regimes. Each ShapeBench task includes a validated surrogate for fast search; when feasible, a high-fidelity Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) pipeline for final verification is available, enabling systematic fidelity-gap analysis. ShapeBench provides a reproducible protocol with well-configured baselines to compare fairly using a consistent budget metric, allowing for comparison among both classical and LLM-driven methods, including general-purpose optimizers and a new domain-specialized evolutionary LLM baseline, ShapeEvolve. Results on ShapeBench demonstrate substantial variance in optimizer rankings across shape categories and problem formulations, with mean pairwise Spearman $ρ= 0.013$, so single-task conclusions do not reliably generalize across problem classes. The benchmark is also far from saturation; classical methods are rarely applicable across all shape categories and tasks, further highlighting the need for more general-purpose approaches.

</details>


### [372] [Transformer Field Theory: A Response-Theoretic Approach to Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25225)
*David N. Olivieri,Antonio F. Pérez Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Transformer场论，将残差流视为随层深和token位置变化的场，用响应理论统一解释激活修补、因果追踪、路径修补和 steering方向等机制可解释性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机制可解释性研究通过激活修补、因果追踪等方法干预Transformer内部激活，但缺乏统一的数学框架来系统描述这些干预行为及其传播效应。

Method: 将单向传播的残差流形式化为Transformer场，修补视为局部源插入，一阶敏感场预测修补效果，Green函数描述下游传播，修补选择建模为伴随逆问题。

Result: 在GPT-2自回归Transformer上验证：局部干预存在有界线性区域；一阶敏感性可跨层-位置预测修补效果；局部源产生各向异性场传播；高敏感位点和切片Green算子提供低维描述；提示诱导的场位移可部分传递答案行为。

Conclusion: 敏感性、Transformer场响应和切片Green算子是组织修补实验的实用工具，为修补位点推断和跨尺度响应迁移提供了正向数学基础。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability often studies Transformer behavior by intervening on internal activations through activation patching, causal tracing, path patching, and steering directions. This paper develops Transformer Field Theory: a response-theoretic framework in which the residual stream of a fixed forward pass is treated as a Transformer field over layer depth and token position. In this formulation, patching becomes a localized source insertion into the Transformer field, first-order sensitivity fields predict patch effects, Green functions describe downstream propagation, and patch selection is posed as an adjoint inverse problem. Empirically, we test the theory's forward response objects in GPT-2-style autoregressive Transformers. Localized Transformer-field interventions exhibit a bounded local linear regime; first-order sensitivities predict patch effects across layer-token sites; localized sources generate structured anisotropic Transformer-field propagation; high-sensitivity sites and sliced Green operators provide reduced response descriptions; and prompt-induced Transformer-field displacements partially transfer answer behavior. These results establish sensitivities, Transformer-field responses, and sliced Green operators as practical objects for organizing patching experiments, while providing the forward mathematical basis for patch-site inference and cross-scale response transfer.

</details>


### [373] [Universal Time Series Generation with Neural Controlled Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28507)
*Torben Berndt,Elyes Farjallah,Leif Seute,Raeid Saqur,Benjamin Walker,Jan Stühmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明结构化线性受控微分方程（SLiCEs）是通用的时间序列生成器，并据此提出G-SLiCE模型用于路径空间上的流匹配，在概率预测及不规则网格任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将状态空间模型在判别设置中的序列普适性理论扩展到生成式时间序列建模，填补连续时间生成模型在表达性方面的理论空白。

Method: 首先证明最大表达力的SLiCEs是通用时间序列生成器，能近似紧致潜集上的连续因果前推的诱导路径律；随后基于该理论提出G-SLiCE，一种用于路径空间流匹配的最大表达力连续时间模型。

Result: G-SLiCE在概率预测及下游任务中表现优于现有方法，并能泛化到任意观测网格，尤其在不规则网格上优于固定网格模型。

Conclusion: 高表达力连续时间模型在生成式时间序列建模中兼具理论保证与实践优势，特别适合不规则采样场景。

Abstract: Recent work on the sequence universality of State Space Models (SSMs) has introduced efficient, maximally expressive continuous-time approaches for time-series modelling. While these works focus on discriminative settings, we extend this perspective to generative time-series modelling by proving that maximally expressive Structured Linear Controlled Differential Equations (SLiCEs) are universal time-series generators, in the sense that they can approximate the induced path laws of continuous causal pushforwards on compact latent sets in $W_\infty$. Building on these theoretical results, we propose Generative SLiCEs (G-SLiCEs), a maximally expressive continuous-time model for flow matching on path-space. Empirically, we show that expressivity improves performance in probabilistic forecasting and downstream tasks, while retaining the advantages of continuous-time models such as generalising to arbitrary observation grids. This is particularly beneficial for irregular grids, where fixed-grid models often struggle.

</details>


### [374] [Plan, Don't Pose: Long Composite Motion Generation with Text-Aligned BFM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29906)
*Nikolay Shvetsov,Maksim Bobrin,Nazar Buzun,Anton Bozhedarov,Dmitry V. Dylov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Text2BFM 提出首个将自然语言与预训练行为基础模型（BFM）对齐的文本到动作生成框架，通过解耦语义规划与运动执行，在BFM隐策略空间中实现高效、鲁棒的T2M生成，尤其擅长处理长序列和组合性文本描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2M方法将语义理解、长期结构规划和低层物理实现耦合在单一模型中，导致训练成本高，且对长序列、组合性或语义密集的提示不可靠。

Method: Text2BFM 在冻结的BFM隐策略空间中操作，通过文本对齐的变分行为瓶颈将BFM策略隐序列压缩为与语言兼容的紧凑行为表示，利用轻量条件生成器在该流形中生成行为，再解码为策略隐变量驱动冻结的BFM。

Result: Text2BFM 实现了高效、鲁棒的T2M生成，在长序列和组合性文本描述上展现出强性能。

Conclusion: 通过解耦语义规划与运动执行，利用预训练BFM作为可执行运动先验，可以有效提升T2M生成的效率和可靠性。

Abstract: Text-to-motion (T2M) generation has broad applications in character animation, virtual avatars, and human-robot interaction. Existing methods typically generate pose trajectories or motion tokens directly from language, forcing a single model to handle semantic interpretation, long-horizon structure, and low-level physical realization. This coupling makes them costly and often unreliable for long, compositional, or semantically dense prompts. We propose Text2BFM, the first framework that aligns natural language with pretrained Behavioral Foundation Models (BFMs) for T2M generation without relying on heavy end-to-end motion generators. Text2BFM operates in the latent policy space of a frozen BFM, using it as an executable motion prior. A text-aligned variational behavioral bottleneck compresses BFM policy-latent sequences into compact motion representations that are compatible with language and preserve long-horizon behavioral structure. Generation is performed in this compact behavioral manifold with a lightweight conditional generator, and the resulting latent encoded behaviors are decoded into policy latents that drive the pretrained frozen BFM. By decoupling semantic planning from motion execution, Text2BFM achieves efficient, robust T2M generation and strong performance on long, compositional textual descriptions.

</details>


### [375] [Revisiting Neural Processes via Fourier Transform and Volterra Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01172)
*Peiman Mohseni,Nick Duffield,Raymond K. W. Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对现有平移等变神经过程（NP）的两个局限——非线性组件堆叠导致可解释性差，以及卷积设计依赖密集均匀网格——提出了基于Volterra展开的连续平移等变算子理论框架和集合傅里叶卷积（SFConvs）方法。SFConvs在频域参数化，直接处理不规则采样点，具有近似全局感受野和线性复杂度。基于此提出SFConvCNP和SFVConvCNP两种条件神经过程，在合成和真实数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平移等变神经过程存在两个局限：(i) 堆叠带非线性的通用组件，模糊了诱导函数类，限制了可解释性；(ii) 卷积设计依赖局部感受野的核和密集均匀输入网格，而注意力方法虽避免这些问题但随观测数二次缩放。需要一种兼具可解释性、能处理不规则采样且计算高效的平移等变模型。

Method: (1) 利用Volterra展开将连续平移等变算子表征为高阶卷积的和，提供分析透明度，并通过一阶卷积实现高效近似。(2) 提出集合傅里叶卷积（SFConvs），一种频域参数化方法，直接在非规则采样点上操作，实现近似全局感受野且线性复杂度。(3) 基于此构建两种条件神经过程：SFConvCNP（堆叠SFConv块+非线性）和SFVConvCNP（集成Volterra公式化）。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，所提方法相对于现有最先进基线具有有效性。

Conclusion: 通过Volterra展开和SFConvs，成功解决了现有平移等变NP的可解释性和计算效率问题，实现了在不规则采样点上具有全局感受野和线性复杂度的条件神经过程。

Abstract: Modeling unknown latent functions from finite, irregularly sampled measurements is a recurring challenge across science and engineering. Neural processes (NPs), a family of probabilistic functional models, are promising solutions -- especially when endowed with domain-specific symmetries like translation equivariance, which improve sample efficiency and generalization. Yet existing translation-equivariant NPs face two limitations: (i) they stack generic components with non-linearities, obscuring the induced function class and limiting interpretability; and (ii) convolutional designs rely on kernels with local receptive fields and require dense uniform input grids, while attention-based methods avoid these issues but scale quadratically with the number of observations. We address both with two contributions. First, using the Volterra expansion, we characterize continuous translation-equivariant operators as sums of higher-order convolutions, yielding analytical transparency while admitting efficient approximation by first-order convolutions. Second, we introduce set Fourier convolutions (SFConvs), a frequency-domain parameterization that operates directly on irregularly sampled points, achieves approximately global receptive fields, and scales linearly in the number of observations. Building on these ideas, we propose two conditional NPs (CNPs): SFConvCNPs, which stack SFConv blocks with non-linearities, and SFVConvCNPs, which integrate the Volterra formulation. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate our methods' efficacy against state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [376] [Realistic noise synthesis reduces bias and improves tissue microstructure estimation with supervised machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02044)
*Bradley G. Karat,Maëliss Jallais,Ali R. Khan,Santiago Aja-Fernández,Jelle Veraart,Marco Palombo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种真实噪声合成（RNS）框架，通过在模拟训练信号中引入Rician期望和有效后处理噪声方差，弥合模拟数据与真实采集数据之间的噪声分布差异（协变量偏移），从而有效减少低信噪比条件下基于监督学习的微结构参数估计的系统性偏差，提升估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散MRI中基于监督学习的微结构参数估计面临模拟数据与真实采集数据之间噪声特性的不匹配（协变量偏移），导致参数估计产生系统性偏差，尤其在低SNR条件下问题突出。现有方法未能充分解决这一噪声差异带来的影响。

Method: 提出真实噪声合成（RNS）框架，包含两个关键部分：(1) 利用MPPCA估计的噪声标准差建模Rician期望；(2) 从预处理数据的球谐残差中推导有效后处理噪声方差。将两者纳入模拟训练信号，并采用cylinder-zeppelin和SANDI模型在多SNR水平及in vivo重复采集数据上评估。

Result: 忽略幅度诱导噪声效应会导致与SNR相关的系统性参数偏差；引入Rician期望后偏差显著降低，达到与噪声感知非线性最小二乘拟合相当的水平；进一步建模有效标准差提升了估计精度；方法性能对回归架构不敏感，但对噪声估计准确性敏感。

Conclusion: 在模拟训练数据中进行真实噪声建模能够减轻信号域的协变量偏移，对于实现无偏的监督微结构估计至关重要，尤其适用于高b值或高空间分辨率对应的低SNR场景。

Abstract: Diffusion MRI enables non-invasive probing of tissue microstructure, but accurate parameter estimation is challenged by noise-related effects. In supervised machine learning frameworks trained on simulated data, discrepancies between the noise characteristics of simulated and acquired signals introduce a form of covariate shift, whereby the input signal distribution differs between training and inference. We investigated the impact of this mismatch on microstructure parameter estimation and propose a realistic noise synthesis (RNS) framework to mitigate it. RNS incorporates both the Rician expectation and the effective post-processing noise variance into simulated training signals. The Rician expectation was modelled using a noise standard deviation estimated with MPPCA, while the effective standard deviation was derived from spherical harmonic residuals of preprocessed data. The method was evaluated using the cylinder-zeppelin and the SANDI models on simulated datasets across multiple SNR levels and on in vivo diffusion data with repeated acquisitions. Sensitivity to noise misestimation was also assessed. Ignoring magnitude-induced noise effects during training produced systematic, SNR-dependent parameter bias, particularly at low SNR. Incorporating the Rician expectation substantially reduced bias to the level of noise-aware nonlinear least-squares fitting. Modelling the effective standard deviation further improved precision. Performance was largely independent of regression architecture but sensitive to accurate noise estimation. These findings demonstrate that realistic noise modelling in simulated training data mitigates signal-domain covariate shift and is essential for unbiased supervised microstructure estimation, particularly in low-SNR regimes associated with high b-values or high spatial resolution.

</details>


### [377] [Variational Learning for Insertion-based Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02133)
*Yangtian Zhang,Zhe Wang,Arthur Gretton,Rex Ying,David van Dijk,Michalis K. Titsias,Jiaxin Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出插入过程（IP），一种通过排列变分推断联合学习插入位置、内容和终止条件的随机生成模型，支持变长生成并学习数据驱动的插入顺序，在非单调序列生成任务上优于固定画布方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非单调序列模型（如掩码扩散模型）是顺序无关的且依赖固定长度网格，无法支持变长生成和自适应插入顺序，需要一种能灵活学习插入顺序的概率框架。

Method: 形式化插入轨迹与排列之间的双射对应关系，重参数化数据似然为排列上的求和；提出插入过程（IP），基于排列变分推断联合学习插入位置、插入内容与终止条件。

Result: 在目标条件规划和分子字符串生成实验中，学习插入顺序在建模质量和泛化能力上均优于固定画布方法。

Conclusion: 变长非单调序列模型中学习数据驱动的插入顺序是有益的，尤其在缺乏规范从左到右结构的领域。

Abstract: Non-monotonic sequence generation methods, such as masked diffusion models, provide a flexible alternative to left-to-right autoregressive modeling by allowing tokens to be generated in non-fixed and prescribed orders. Despite their practical advantages, most existing non-monotonic models are order-agnostic and rely on a fixed-length grid, limiting their ability to support variable-length generation and adaptive insertion order. In this work, we introduce a probabilistic framework for learning insertion order in variable-length insertion models. We formalize a bijective correspondence between insertion trajectories and permutations, which enables an exact reparameterization of the data likelihood as a sum over permutations. Building on this result, we propose the Insertion Process (IP), a stochastic generative model that jointly learns where to insert, what to insert, and when to terminate, trained via permutation-based variational inference. Unlike prior fixed-canvas approaches, IP natively supports variable-length generation and learns data-driven preferences over insertion orders. Experiments on goal-conditioned planning and molecular string generation demonstrate that learning insertion order improves both modeling quality and generalization in domains without a canonical left-to-right structure.

</details>


### [378] [Benchmarking Counterfactual Prediction in Epidemic Time Series with Time-Varying Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05692)
*Wenhao Mu,Facundo Yan,Anik Mumssen,Marisa Eisenberg,Alexander Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对时间序列因果推断中缺乏具有可观测反事实结果的真实基准这一问题，本文开发了一个基于流行病时间序列的大规模基准数据集，支持静态/时变处理、单/多策略干预，基于校准的智能体模型生成美国150多个县的真实反事实轨迹，并评估了多种因果推断方法，揭示了显著性能差异和真实时间序列因果推理的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列因果推断数据集存在两难困境：真实世界观测数据缺乏反事实ground-truth，而简化模拟数据无法捕捉复杂因果动态。迫切需要填补这一空白，构建兼具真实性和可观测反事实的基准。

Method: 利用基于真实人口、流动性、流行病学和政策数据校准的智能体模型，生成150多个美国县在动态干预下的反事实轨迹；基准支持静态与时变治疗、单政策与多政策干预多种场景。

Result: 对多种广泛使用和最新的因果推断方法进行评估，发现不同方法之间存在显著的性能差异，揭示了现实时间序列因果推理面临的挑战。

Conclusion: 该基准填补了时间序列因果推断中缺乏可观测反事实真实基准的空白，为评估和推动因果推断方法在真实复杂动态场景中的应用提供了重要平台。

Abstract: Deep learning has enabled significant advances in time-series causal inference, yet progress remains constrained by the lack of realistic benchmarks with observable counterfactual outcomes. Existing datasets either rely on real-world observations without ground-truth counterfactuals or on simplified simulations that fail to capture complex causal dynamics. To address this gap, we develop a large-scale benchmark for counterfactual prediction in epidemic time series under dynamic interventions. Unlike existing benchmarks, it supports static and time-varying treatments, as well as both single-policy and multi-policy intervention settings, enabling evaluation of causal inference methods across a broad range of causal inference scenarios. Leveraging a calibrated agent-based model grounded in real-world demographic, mobility, epidemiological, and policy data, we generate realistic counterfactual trajectories across more than 150 U.S. counties. Using this benchmark, we evaluate widely used and state-of-the-art causal inference methods, revealing substantial performance differences and highlighting the challenges of realistic time-series causal reasoning.

</details>


### [379] [GenAutoML: An Agentic Framework for Dynamic Architecture Generation and Optimization in Time-Series Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05860)
*Oleeviya Babu Poikarayil,Cédric Schockaert,Abdulrahman Nahhas,Christian Daase,Mursal Dawodi,Jawid Ahmad Baktash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GenAutoML是一个利用大语言模型作为神经架构师的自动化机器学习框架，能够根据自然语言需求动态生成适配时间序列预测和异常检测任务的轻量级PyTorch网络架构，其中生成的WaveInterferenceNet在保持竞争力的同时实现了低于0.01ms/样本的推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AutoML系统依赖静态预定义的搜索空间，难以适应多样化的数据特征，且设计时间序列网络架构需要大量领域专长和资源。

Method: 提出GenAutoML框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于LLM的神经架构生成器，将自然语言需求转化为PyTorch代码；(2) 沙盒反射循环(Sandboxed Reflection Loop)实现自主代码精炼；(3) 签名感知运行时(Signature-Aware Runtime)保证架构一致性和执行安全。此外引入动态可逆实例归一化(Dyn-RevIN)包装器增强非平稳条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在ETTh1、ETTm1和Weather基准测试上，GenAutoML能够动态生成适配数据集特性的任务特定架构。其中WaveInterferenceNet在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，推理延迟低于0.01ms/样本。

Conclusion: GenAutoML通过强调计算效率、架构自适应性和稳定优化行为，实现了适用于资源受限和延迟敏感的Edge AI部署的超轻量级神经网络生成。

Abstract: Designing neural architectures for time-series forecasting and anomaly detection remains a resource-intensive task that often requires substantial domain expertise. Traditional Automated Machine Learning (AutoML) systems typically rely on static, predefined search spaces, limiting their ability to adapt to diverse data characteristics. We present GenAutoML, an agentic framework that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) as neural architects to bridge natural-language requirements and executable PyTorch implementations. The framework incorporates a Sandboxed Reflection Loop for autonomous code refinement and a Signature-Aware Runtime that enforces architectural consistency and execution safety. To improve robustness under non-stationary conditions, we further introduce a Dynamic Reversible Instance Normalization (Dyn-RevIN) wrapper. Experiments on the ETTh1, ETTm1, and Weather benchmarks demonstrate that GenAutoML can dynamically generate task-specific neural architectures tailored to dataset characteristics. Among the generated models, WaveInterferenceNet achieves inference latency below 0.01 ms per sample while maintaining competitive predictive performance. By emphasizing computational efficiency, architectural adaptability, and stable optimization behavior, GenAutoML enables the creation of ultra-lightweight neural networks suitable for resource-constrained and latency-sensitive Edge AI deployments.

</details>


### [380] [A Unifying Lens on Reward Uncertainty in RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09073)
*Ely Hahami,Yoel Zimmermann,Ray Zhou,Jack Benarroch Jedlicki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出RLHF因奖励破解而受限，提出分布奖励模型$p(r|x,y)$，并从贝叶斯推断和KL-DRO角度推导出闭式有效奖励$\tilde r(x,y) = \pmβ\log\mathbb{E}_p[e^{\pm r/β}]$，该表达式统一了均值聚合、最差情况优化和不确定性加权优化等现有启发式方法，并揭示了它们各自的隐含假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF中的奖励破解问题严重制约了性能提升，现有标量奖励模型缺乏对不确定性的原则性度量，导致策略利用代理RM的错误产生高分但无实际质量提升。需要一种理论上更严谨的方法来处理RM不确定性，以缓解奖励破解。

Method: 提出使用分布奖励模型$p(r|x,y)$替代标量RM；从贝叶斯推断和KL-DRO两个视角，推导出KL正则化RLHF目标下的闭式有效奖励$\tilde r(x,y) = \pmβ\log\mathbb{E}_p[e^{\pm r/β}]$；并证明均值聚合、WCO、UWO等现有启发式方法均为该表达式的极限或截断形式。

Result: 推导出了统一的闭式有效奖励表达式，该表达式在悲观分支下能够统一解释现有的多种RM集成聚合启发式方法（均值聚合、WCO、UWO），并明确了每种方法隐含的假设条件。

Conclusion: 分布奖励模型是处理RLHF中不确定性的正确工具，所提出的统一框架为设计更鲁棒的RLHF算法提供了理论指导，有助于缓解奖励破解问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) is bottlenecked by reward hacking, where the policy exploits errors in a proxy reward model (RM) and produces high RM scores without genuine quality gains. A natural mitigation is pessimism: lowering rewards in regions where the RM is uncertain. However, standard scalar RMs provide no principled notion of uncertainty. We argue that the right object is a distributional reward model $p(r\mid x,y)$. Under either a Bayesian inference or a KL-distributionally robust optimization (KL-DRO) lens, the KL-regularized RLHF objective admits a closed-form effective reward $\tilde r(x,y) = \pmβ\log\mathbb{E}_p[e^{\pm r/β}]$. The pessimistic branch unifies the prior heuristics for RM ensemble aggregation: mean aggregation, worst-case optimization (WCO), and uncertainty-weighted optimization (UWO) all emerge as limits or truncations of this single expression. This also clarifies the implicit assumptions of each existing rule.

</details>


### [381] [Using Seismic Statistical Features and VQ-VAE to Improve Spatiotemporal Seismicity Predictability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10069)
*Wei Quan,Denise Gorse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在前序研究基础上，使用日本地震目录数据，将地震预测从全区域（15天内M≥5.0）改进为局部化预测（半径24km），并提出一种基于VQ-VAE自编码器从二维地震图提取的新型空间特征，该特征能够增强预测性能并几乎完全替代传统b值特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将地震预测从粗粒度的全区域预测推进到细粒度的局部化预测，并引入能够捕捉地壳应力二维空间分布的新型特征，以提升预测精度和特征的可解释性。

Method: 1) 使用60个地震统计特征（SSFs）进行局部化预测（半径24km）；2) 训练VQ-VAE模型重建二维地震图，以重建误差作为新型空间特征；3) 结合SSFs与VQ-VAE特征，通过SHAP分析进行特征重要性排序。

Result: 局部化预测基于SSFs即可达到与前序全区域研究相当的AUC值；引入VQ-VAE特征后预测性能进一步提升；SHAP分析显示该新型特征排名最高，且几乎完全取代传统b值的功能。

Conclusion: 一维目录数据提取的SSFs在局部化预测中依然有效，而VQ-VAE提取的二维空间特征能提供互补信息并增强模型性能，为地震预测提供了新的特征工程方向。

Abstract: In this paper we build upon a previous study in which we demonstrated, using XGBoost and earthquake catalogue data from Japan and Chile, that a set of 60 seismic statistical features (SSFs) had much greater predictive value than a set of 428 generic time series features from the tsfresh package. We here extend this previous work in two key ways, focusing on data from Japan as a large dataset is necessary in order to allow for the training of a deep learning (autoencoder) model. First, we move from whole-region prediction (considering, for each candidate event, the likelihood of an event M $\geq$ 5.0 anywhere in the region in the next 15 days) to localised predictions in which both the region of feature computation and the region of prediction are restricted to a circle of radius 24 km around the candidate event, and we show that performance remains excellent, similar to our previous whole-region study for the same area. Second, we here couple this proven set of SSFs, based on one-dimensional (catalogue) data, with a novel feature based on two-dimensional seismic maps, obtained by training a VQ-VAE model to reproduce such maps as output and identifying a measure of its error in doing so with a localised build-up of crustal stress. We show that while localised prediction based on SSFs can be effective alone, with test AUC values as high as those obtained in the case of Japan in our previous whole-region study, the inclusion of the new natively-spatial VQ-VAE-derived feature, top-ranked by SHAP analysis, can enhance performance and additionally appears to near-wholly replace the traditionally-computed $b$-value in terms of feature usage.

</details>


### [382] [PhysMetrics.Weather: An Evaluation Framework for Physical Consistency in ML Weather Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10642)
*Emma Kasteleyn,Timo Maier,Axel Lauer,Veronika Eyring,Pierre Gentine,Ana Lucic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysMetrics.Weather评估框架，用于衡量机器学习天气预报模型的物理真实性，涵盖守恒性、频谱和动力学三类指标，以弥补纯数据驱动模型缺乏物理一致性保证的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLWP模型主要依赖数据驱动，且仅用像素级误差指标（如RMSE）评估，无法保证预测结果符合已知物理定律，因此需要一套评估物理真实性的方法。

Method: 构建PhysMetrics.Weather评估框架，包含三大类物理指标：（1）守恒性指标（如能量、质量守恒）、（2）频谱指标（如功率谱密度）、（3）动力学指标（如大气环流特征），用以量化模型的物理真实性。

Result: 该框架能够量化MLWP模型的物理真实性，指导物理信息架构的开发，并评估模型是否足够可靠以用于实际业务运行。

Conclusion: PhysMetrics.Weather为MLWP模型提供了超越传统误差指标的物理一致性评估手段，有助于推动更可靠、物理上更合理的天气预报模型发展。

Abstract: Machine learning weather prediction (MLWP) models have achieved impressive forecasting performance at a small fraction of the computational costs required for traditional physics-based methods. However, they are primarily (1) data-driven and (2) evaluated using pixel-wide error metrics (e.g., RMSE), so there are no guarantees that their forecasts are consistent with known physical laws. We introduce PhysMetrics$.$Weather, an evaluation framework that assesses the physical realism of MLWP models across three types of metrics: conservation, spectral, and dynamical. By quantifying physical realism, this tool guides the development of physics-informed architectures and helps evaluate whether MLWP models are reliable for operational use. Our framework is available on Github at https://github.com/Emmakast/PhysMetrics.Weather.

</details>


### [383] [One Step Closer to Ground Truth: A Multi-Scale Residual-Aware Representation Learning Pipeline for Predicting Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10678)
*Amrijit Biswas,Mustafa Kamal,Robin Krambroeckers,M. M. Lutfe Elahi,Sifat Momen,Nabeel Mohammed,Shafin Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段、模型无关的框架，将基础Transformer预测与专门的元校正器解耦，通过显式学习结构化残差模式来消除单阶段预测架构中的系统性偏差，在8个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单阶段Transformer预测架构存在持续的系统性残差偏差，这些偏差源于结构差异、未建模的随机成分或多尺度时序表示不足，而现有方法将残差视为不可约噪声，无法自适应校正结构化误差模式。

Method: 提出两阶段模型无关框架：第一阶段基础Transformer生成初始预测；第二阶段专用元校正器动态建模多变量通道上的结构化误差模式，保留跨变量依赖关系，迭代精炼基础Transformer的残差偏差。该框架形式化为假设空间扩展，实现端到端学习复杂误差动态。

Result: 在8个流行基准数据集上使用既定协议评估，达到SOTA性能，在MSE和MAE标准指标上有显著改进。

Conclusion: 该框架能够缓解系统性偏差，增强对复杂时序动态的鲁棒性，推进了基于Transformer的预测模型的实际应用性。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have emerged as leading paradigms in time-series forecasting in recent years, employing self-attention mechanisms to capture long-range dependencies. Despite their success, these single-stage forecasting architectures exhibit persistent systematic residual biases arising from structural discrepancies, unmodeled stochastic components, or inadequate multi-scale temporal representations. This limitation persists when residuals are treated as irreducible noise, precluding adaptive correction of structured error patterns. To address this limitation, we introduce a two-stage, model-agnostic framework that explicitly decouples forecasting and residual learning into distinct stages of representation learning. A base transformer first generates the initial predictions. Subsequently, a dedicated meta-corrector dynamically models structured error patterns across multivariate channels, preserves cross-variable dependencies, and iteratively refines the residual bias of the base transformer. By formalizing this pipeline as a hypothesis space expansion, our framework addresses approximation limitations inherent in single-stage architectures, removes reliance on restrictive assumptions, and enables end-to-end learning of complex error dynamics. Evaluated on eight popular benchmark datasets using established protocols, our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance, with significant improvements in standard metrics (MSE, MAE). The results demonstrate the framework's ability to mitigate systematic biases and enhance robustness to complex temporal dynamics, advancing the practical applicability of transformer-based forecasting models.

</details>


### [384] [When to Align, When to Predict: A Phase Diagram for Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11190)
*Ilay Kamai,Hugues Van Assel,Aviv Regev,Hagai B. Perets,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的线性框架，系统分析了多模态表征学习中跨模态对齐（CA）和跨模态预测（CP）两种范式的成功与失败条件，并给出了一个四象限相图（Both、CA only、CP only、Neither），帮助实践者在训练前诊断多模态问题并选择正确目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对跨模态对齐和跨模态预测两种范式何时成功、何时失败以及跨模态训练何时完全无益的系统性理解，这使得在科学领域（如生物医学、天体物理学）中，面对异质仪器和多层次测量数据时，从业者无法诊断为何标准多模态方法不如最佳单模态表现。

Method: 在带结构化跨模态干扰相关的尖峰信号加噪声模型下，推导了对齐和预测目标的分离比（separation ratios），揭示了两者互补的失败模式：对齐会对各模态进行白化处理，当干扰在跨模态间高度相关时失效；预测通过单向白化编码可跨模态预测的信息，恢复质量由源模态质量决定。基于此构建四区域相图，并提出一种数据驱动流程，利用少量有标签子样本在相图中定位真实数据集。

Result: 在合成数据、立体视觉基准、图像-字幕对以及真实天体物理数据上的实验验证了非线性情况下的理论预测，包括跨模态训练会带来负面影响的"Neither"区域。

Conclusion: 该框架让从业者能够在投入训练前诊断多模态问题并选择合适的训练目标，从而避免盲目选择跨模态方法带来的性能损失。

Abstract: Cross-modal alignment (CA) and cross-modal prediction (CP) are the dominant paradigms for multimodal representation learning, yet there is no systematic understanding of when each succeeds, when each fails, and when cross-modal training helps at all -- a gap that leaves practitioners, especially in scientific domains like biomedicine or astrophysics, with heterogeneous instruments and multiple levels of organization and measurement, unable to diagnose why standard methods underperform the best single modality. We develop a unified linear framework that addresses both questions. Under a spiked signal-plus-noise model with structured cross-modal nuisance correlation, we derive separation ratios for both objectives that expose complementary failure modes: alignment whitens each modality and fails when nuisance is strongly correlated across views; prediction encodes whatever is cross-predictable through a one-sided whitening, with recovery governed by source-modality quality. The resulting phase diagram partitions multimodal problems into four regimes: Both, CA only, CP only, and Neither. We present a data-driven procedure to locate real-world datasets in this diagram using a small labeled subsample, identifying the preferred objective and prediction direction before any cross-modal training. Experiments on synthetic data, stereo-vision benchmarks, image-caption pairs, and real astrophysical data validate the predictions in the nonlinear regime, including the Neither regime where cross-modal training is actively harmful. Our framework lets practitioners diagnose their multimodal problem and choose the right objective before committing to training. Code to reproduce the results is available at https://github.com/IlayMalinyak/mm_align_vs_pred.

</details>


### [385] [Bernstein-Schur Kernels: Random Features by Sketched Modulation and Radial Randomization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11255)
*Taha Bouhsine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对Bernstein-Schur核（有限特征核与完全单调平移不变核的乘积）的双重随机特征构造方法，通过随机化调制因子和径向因子，将特征维度降低至Dm（避免O(d²)的精确调制特征），并在保留精确调制时证明了无偏性、精确方差和基于矩阵Bernstein的算子范数界。通过岭回归白化处理，将有效维度d_eff作为矩阵方差的精确内在维度，使径向采样保持核岭解；进一步通过白化杠杆倾斜采样达到有效维度计数。该框架适用于整个核类，并以bias yat-kernel作为旗舰实例，其族通过有限差分包含反多二次核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bernstein-Schur核介于平移不变核和点积核之间，既不能用Bochner采样也不能用多项式sketching直接处理，现有随机特征方法无法直接应用于这类非平稳核，需要一种统一的随机特征构造方案。

Method: 提出双重随机化方法：1) 对有限调制因子进行sketching；2) 对径向因子的Bernstein-Widder尺度进行一维采样；3) 应用高斯随机傅里叶特征。通过矩阵Bernstein不等式推导算子范数界，利用岭回归白化技术得到有效内在维度，并引入白化杠杆改进采样复杂度。

Result: 特征维度降至Dm（避免O(d²)）；证明了无偏性和精确方差；得到O((1+||P||_op/λ)log(d_eff/δ))的径向采样复杂度，经白化杠杆改进后达到O((1+d_eff)log(d_eff/δ))；所有保证均可通过条件化推广到双重随机化估计器。

Conclusion: 该框架为Bernstein-Schur核类提供了统一的随机特征构造方法，实现了从精确调制到完全随机化的平滑过渡，并以yat-kernel为例展示了其应用，该核族通过有限差分可逼近反多二次核。

Abstract: Bernstein--Schur kernels are products of a finite-feature kernel and a completely monotone shift-invariant kernel: nonstationary kernels falling between the shift-invariant and dot-product templates random features exploit, so neither Bochner sampling nor polynomial sketching applies to the full kernel directly. We give one random-feature construction for the whole class that randomizes both factors: it sketches the finite modulation and samples the radial factor's one-dimensional Bernstein--Widder scale before applying Gaussian random Fourier features, giving feature dimension $Dm$, free of the $O(d^2)$ size of the exact modulation feature. With the modulation kept exact (the $m\to\infty$ limit), we prove unbiasedness, an exact variance, and a matrix-Bernstein operator-norm bound controlled by the top kernel and modulation eigenvalues and an intrinsic dimension rather than the crude $N\max_{ij}$ route. Whitening this argument at the ridge makes the effective dimension $d_{\mathrm{eff}}(λ)$ the \emph{exact} intrinsic dimension of the matrix variance, so $O((1+\|P\|_{\mathrm{op}}/λ)\log(d_{\mathrm{eff}}/δ))$ radial draws preserve the kernel-ridge solution; tilting the draw by a closed-form whitened leverage improves this to the effective-dimension count $O((1+d_{\mathrm{eff}})\log(d_{\mathrm{eff}}/δ))$. Conditioning on the sketch carries every guarantee to the deployed doubly-randomized estimator up to one additive sketch term, and all hold for the whole class with the modulation Gram in place of the polynomial one. The flagship instance is the biased $yat$-kernel $k_{yat,b}(w,x)=(w^\top x+b)^2/(\|w-x\|^2+\varepsilon)$, whose family span contains the inverse-multiquadric kernel by finite differences in $b$.

</details>


### [386] [Scalable Deep Learning Framework for Global High-Resolution Land Use Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11793)
*Amirpasha Mozaffari,Marina Castaño,Stefano Materia,Etienne Tourigny,Oscar Molina-Sedano,Jordi Varela-Agrelo,Dario Garcia-Gasulla,Miguel Castrillo Melguizo,Mario Acosta,Amanda Duarte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI4Land框架，一种基于U-Net架构的数据驱动方法，用于生成高分辨率陆地表面变量（如土地利用/覆盖）的历史重建和未来预测，旨在减少地球系统模型中陆地碳循环的不确定性，并通过GPU加速HPC基础设施实现实时耦合数字孪生平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陆地碳循环的不确定性是气候预测的主要制约因素，部分源于地球系统模型中陆地表面表征的变异性和不确定性，亟需改进以提升下一代气候模拟的预测能力。

Method: 采用两阶段U-Net架构框架：第一阶段（本文重点）通过整合粗分辨率情景数据与静态地理特征，重建年度土地利用/覆盖；第二阶段（规划中）将利用高分辨率地图预测动态生物物理变量（如叶面积指数）。模型基于地球观测数据训练，并在MareNostrum5超级计算机上进行GPU加速训练。

Result: 模型能够学习生成空间显式且物理一致的陆地表面模式，并将时间覆盖扩展到缺乏直接观测的时期；最终产品是一套开源仿真器，支持与数字孪生平台（如Destination Earth）的实时耦合。

Conclusion: AI4Land通过按需提供逼真且动态演变的陆地表面条件，有望减少关键不确定性，提升下一代气候模拟的预测能力。

Abstract: Uncertainty in the terrestrial carbon cycle remains a major constraint in climate projections, partly driven by the uncertainties affecting the land surface representation and variability in Earth system models. To address this limitation, we present a data-driven framework AI4Land, for generating high-resolution historical reconstructions and future projections of key land surface variables. The framework follows a two-phase approach using a U-Net architecture. In the first phase, which is the focus of this work, it reconstructs annual land use and land cover by integrating coarse-resolution scenario data with static geophysical features. In a planned second phase, the resulting high-resolution maps will be used to predict dynamic biophysical variables, particularly leaf area index, at finer temporal scales. Trained on Earth observation data, the models learn to reproduce spatially explicit and physically consistent land surface patterns, extending temporal coverage to periods lacking direct observations. AI4Land was developed and trained on MareNostrum5, demonstrating how GPU-accelerated HPC infrastructure enables global-scale climate AI pipelines. The final product is a suite of open-source emulators designed for real-time coupling with digital twin platforms, such as those developed under the Destination Earth initiative. By delivering realistic and evolving land surface conditions on demand, this work aims to reduce critical uncertainties and improve the predictive power of next-generation climate simulations.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [387] [A quantum-like benchmark for context-sensitive associative memory with adaptive plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12449)
*Yashine H. Goolam Hossen,Lea Gassab,Travis J. A. Craddock*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个顺序敏感的适应性可塑性基准（order-sensitive adaptive-plasticity benchmark），用于在弱结构支持下比较量子类联想记忆模型与经典对照模型（无相位模型、马尔可夫率模型）的分阶段联想回忆表现。研究发现弱结构支持本身不能挽救无可塑性消融下的回忆，最有用的回忆增益来自适应性可塑性（尤其是稳态稳定化）；马尔可夫率模型在原始回忆强度上更强，但量子类模型在保持顺序敏感性和阶段依赖组织方面更一致。研究不支持通用的量子类优势，而主张通过多目标概况（回忆、时间组织、上下文敏感性）来区分模型类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习和记忆需要在可塑性与稳定性之间取得平衡，而联想记忆模型在固定背景连接已承担部分任务时可能表现出虚假的学习成功，难以区分真正的回忆动力学与结构辅助。因此需要一个受控基准框架来测试在弱结构支持下、受调控可塑性和匹配经典比较条件下的上下文敏感记忆动力学。

Method: 设计了一个顺序敏感的适应性可塑性基准，用于分阶段联想回忆。在该基准中比较了量子类联想记忆模型与匹配的实数无相位和马尔可夫率对照模型，所有模型在相同的任务调度、扰动轮廓、弱支持条件和可塑性设置下运行。先筛选弱结构支持，再固定保守工作点进行跨模型家族和可塑性机制的因子比较。

Result: 有用的弱支持范围狭窄且非单调；弱结构本身无法挽救无可塑性消融下的回忆；大多数有用的回忆增益来自适应性可塑性（尤其是稳态稳定化）；马尔可夫率控制模型通常获得更强的原始回忆，但量子类模型在保持顺序敏感性和阶段依赖组织方面更一致；不支持通用的量子类优势。

Conclusion: 模型类别应通过结合回忆、时间组织和上下文敏感性的多目标概况来区分，而非任何单一的回忆得分。该基准为在弱支持下、受调控可塑性和匹配经典比较条件下研究上下文敏感记忆动力学提供了一个受控框架。

Abstract: Learning and memory require a balance between plasticity and stability: synaptic connections must encode new information without collapsing, saturating, or erasing previously useful structure. Associative-memory models can appear to learn successfully when fixed background connectivity already carries part of the task, making it difficult to distinguish genuine recall dynamics from structural assistance. We test this issue using an order-sensitive adaptive-plasticity benchmark for staged associative recall. The benchmark compares a quantum-like associative-memory model with matched real-valued no-phase and Markov-rate controls under the same task schedule, perturbation profiles, weak-support conditions, and plasticity settings. Here, "quantum-like" refers to the modeling formalism, not to a biological claim about quantum computation. We first screen weak structural support and then fix a conservative operating point for factorial comparisons across model families and plasticity mechanisms. The useful weak-support regime is narrow and non-monotonic. Weak structure alone does not rescue recall in the no-plasticity ablation, whereas most useful recall gains arise from adaptive plasticity, especially homeostatic stabilization. The Markov-rate control often achieves stronger raw recall, but the quantum-like model more consistently preserves order sensitivity and stage-dependent organization. These results do not support a universal quantum-like advantage. Instead, they show that model classes are better distinguished by a multi-objective profile combining recall, temporal organization, and context sensitivity than by any single recall score. The benchmark therefore provides a controlled framework for studying context-sensitive memory dynamics under weak support, regulated plasticity, and matched classical comparison.

</details>


### [388] [Multifractal human signals at the edge of life reveal a heart-brain anti-correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12600)
*Yago Emanoel Ramos,Maria Eloá do Ó,Henrique Ferraz de Arruda,Mauro Copelli,G. Camelo-Neto,Pedro V. Carelli*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究使用多重分形去趋势波动分析（MF-DFA）方法，揭示了临终阶段脑电与心电信号的多重分形谱出现方向相反的变化——神经活动分形性坍缩而心脏信号谱宽异常增大，两者负相关表明神经-心脏系统发生功能性解耦，这种身体驱动的大脑崩溃模式不同于均匀衰退，体现了外周功能障碍压倒中枢调控的跨系统解整合过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人体临终阶段神经生理功能的崩溃机制，特别是大脑与心脏这两个关键系统之间的耦合关系在死亡过程中的演变规律。

Method: 采用多重分形去趋势波动分析（MF-DFA）方法，对临终患者同步采集的脑电图（EEG）和心电图（ECG）时间序列进行多重分形谱的量化分析，追踪其复杂性的时间演化。

Result: 神经活动的多重分形谱宽度发生坍缩（趋于受限状态），而心脏信号则出现异常的谱宽增大（表明非线性波动增加和动力学不稳定性）；两个谱宽之间存在负相关，提示功能性解耦和反相关动力学的出现。

Conclusion: 临终过程并非均匀的生理衰退，而是表现为身体-大脑的崩溃模式——外周功能障碍逐步压倒中枢调节过程。这种跨系统解整合代表了层级协调机制崩溃的极端形式，类似于其他外周驱动适应过程中观察到的反向动力学模式。

Abstract: This study investigates the terminal breakdown of human neurophysiological function through the lens of non-linear dynamics by analyzing the multifractal spectrum. Using Multifractal Detrended Fluctuation Analysis (MF-DFA), we quantify the temporal evolution of complexity in synchronized electroencephalogram (EEG) and electrocardiogram (ECG) time series from patients in the terminal stage. Our results reveal a marked divergence in multifractal spectrum width: while neural activity exhibits a collapse of multifractality toward a more constrained state, cardiac signals undergo anomalous spectral broadening, indicating increased non-linear fluctuations and dynamical instability. A negative correlation between these spectral widths suggests effective functional decoupling and the emergence of anti-correlated dynamics between neural and cardiac systems. Rather than reflecting a uniform physiological decline, this divergence is consistent with a body-to-brain breakdown in which peripheral dysfunction progressively overwhelms central regulatory processes. In a broader context, the observed opposing trends resemble patterns reported in other body-driven adaptive processes, suggesting that inverse dynamics across coupled systems may emerge when constraints originate from peripheral rather than central mechanisms. Ultimately, the dying process appears to represent an extreme form of cross-system disintegration, marked by the collapse of the hierarchical coordination that normally sustains integrated physiological function.

</details>


### [389] [Phase model analysis of the effect of M-current on neural synchrony in hippocampal networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12684)
*Megha Manoj,Sue Ann Campbell*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该研究通过计算模型分析了乙酰胆碱（ACh）对海马神经集群形成的双向调控作用：低ACh促进网络完全同步（记忆巩固），高ACh促使网络分裂为多个稳定对称集群（记忆编码），揭示了M电流下调在其中的关键机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有假说认为ACh在记忆编码和巩固中扮演双向角色（高ACh促进编码，低ACh促进巩固），但其如何通过影响神经元同步性来调节神经集群形成的具体机制尚不清楚。

Method: 构建包含锥体神经元（具慢速电压依赖性非失活钾电流/M电流）的网络模型，ACh通过下调M电流发挥作用。利用弱耦合相位模型简化研究不同ACh水平下的成对神经元同步性，进而预测全局同质、对称距离依赖和最近邻三种耦合架构下大网络的对称集群解。

Result: 低ACh条件下网络可实现完全同步；高ACh条件下网络去同步化为多个稳定对称集群解，代表不同的神经集群。

Conclusion: 该模型为ACh双向调控记忆编码与巩固的假说提供了机制层面的计算支持，即通过调节网络同步与集群分化来实现功能切换。

Abstract: Neural assemblies, transiently coordinated groups of neurons, observed in the hippocampus are thought to underlie the formation of episodic memories. Acetylcholine (ACh), a neuromodulator, that is received by the hippocampus, plays a critical role in memory and learning. A well supported hypothesis suggests that high levels of ACh during active exploration and rapid eye movement (REM) sleep promote memory encoding, while low levels during quiet waking and slow-wave sleep (SWS) support memory consolidation. We study this bidirectional role of ACh in neural assembly formation through its effect on the synchrony among neurons. We consider a network model of pyramidal neurons, each equipped with a slow, voltage-dependent, non-inactivating potassium current (M-current), which is downregulated in the presence of ACh. Neural assemblies are represented as cluster solutions to this system. Using a one-dimensional phase model reduction of a pair of weakly coupled pyramidal neurons under different levels of the M-current, we predict the symmetric cluster solutions that may emerge in larger networks equipped with all-to-all globally homogeneous, symmetric distance-dependent and nearest-neighbours coupling architectures. We find that under low ACh conditions, the network can fully synchronize, whereas high levels can desynchronize the network into multiple stable symmetric cluster solutions representing distinct neural assemblies.

</details>


### [390] [Deep Sleep Classification via EEG Signal Criticality: A Passive BCI Approach for Sleep-Improvement Neurofeedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13017)
*Stanisław Narębski,Tomasz Komendziński,Tomasz M. Rutkowski*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文利用去趋势波动分析（DFA）提取的临界性特征，结合朴素贝叶斯分类器，在290名老年女性的347,232个EEG epochs上实现了87.17%的平衡准确率用于N3深度睡眠分期，显著优于深度神经网络，证明EEG临界性特征可用于高精度被动脑机接口（pBCI）神经反馈应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现自动睡眠分期是pBCI的基本应用，通过解码自发神经状态实现不依赖用户意图的闭环干预，本研究旨在评估DFA临界性特征对深度睡眠（N3）的特异性识别能力。

Method: 分析了290名老年女性的347,232个EEG epochs，使用UMAP流形学习可视化状态转换；随后通过10折交叉验证对六个分类器进行基准测试，以平衡准确率确定最优"状态感知"引擎。

Result: 朴素贝叶斯取得了最高平均平衡准确率（87.17% ± 0.24%），显著优于全连接深度神经网络（FNN: 81.58%）和随机森林（80.97%）；线性模型表现较差（LDA: 57.21%; SVM: 51.01%），表明DFA临界性特征位于非线性流形上。

Conclusion: EEG临界性的概率解码为pBCI提供了高精度感知机制，该鲁棒分类流程支持状态依赖的神经反馈应用（如靶向听觉刺激）以增强认知恢复。

Abstract: Automated sleep staging is a fundamental application of passive Brain-Computer Interfaces (pBCI), decoding spontaneous neural states to enable closed-loop interventions independent of user intent. This study evaluates criticality features derived from Detrended Fluctuation Analysis (DFA) for the specific identification of deep sleep (N3).
  We analyzed $347,232$ EEG epochs from $290$ older women using UMAP manifold learning to visualize state transitions. Subsequently, six classifiers were benchmarked via 10-fold cross-validation, using balanced accuracy to determine the optimal "state-sensing" engine for neurofeedback.Naive Bayes achieved the highest mean balanced accuracy ($87.17\% \pm 0.24\%$), significantly outperforming a fully connected deep neural network (FNN: $81.58\%$) and Random Forest ($80.97\%$). Linear models (LDA: $57.21\%$; SVM: $51.01\%$) performed poorly, indicating that DFA-derived criticality features reside on a distinct, non-linear manifold.
  Probabilistic decoding of EEG criticality provides a high-accuracy sensing mechanism for pBCIs. This robust classification pipeline supports the development of state-dependent neurofeedback, such as targeted auditory stimulation, to enhance cognitive recovery.

</details>


### [391] [Including the Cost of Irreducible Uncertainty in the Policy Compression Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13132)
*Álvaro Garrido-Pérez,Pieter Simoens,Amrapali Pednekar,Yara Khaluf*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了策略压缩框架，将认知成本定义为策略复杂度与加权条件熵之和，引入新参数η来控制条件熵的权重，使得最优策略更尖锐，政策精度与奖励敏感性可更独立变化，从而更好地解释人类决策偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略压缩框架将条件熵视为无成本的，但实证研究表明条件熵会调节反应时间，因此该框架对人类决策偏差的解释不完整，需要扩展。

Method: 在原有策略压缩框架（奖励最大化与策略复杂度之间的权衡）基础上，引入加权条件熵项作为额外认知成本，通过新参数η控制其权重，推导出扩展后的最优策略形式。

Result: 扩展后的最优策略保留标准指数形式，但随着η增加策略变得更尖锐，使政策精度可更独立于奖励敏感性变化；标准框架可能低估了行动选择的认知成本。

Conclusion: 该扩展能更好地解释人类决策偏差，但增加了模型拟合人类数据的复杂度，需要未来工作解决。

Abstract: AI decision-support systems can benefit from anticipating biases in human decision-making. Many such biases may arise from human cognitive limitations. The policy compression framework models decision-making as a trade-off between reward maximization and the cognitive cost of encoding state-dependent action policies, formalized as the mutual information between states and actions (policy complexity). We argue that this account is incomplete because it treats conditional entropy--the irreducible uncertainty about which action should be selected given a state--as costless, even though empirical evidence suggests that it modulates reaction times. We therefore extend the framework by defining cognitive cost as the sum of policy complexity and a weighted conditional-entropy term, governed by a new parameter, $η$. The resulting optimal policy retains the standard exponential form but becomes sharper as $η$ increases, allowing policy precision to vary more independently of reward sensitivity. This modification implies that the standard policy compression framework may underestimate the cognitive cost of action selection, and it has the potential to better account for biases in human decision-making. At the same time, it introduces additional complexity for fitting the model to human data, which future work will need to address.

</details>


### [392] [Robust State-space Reconstruction of Brain Dynamics via Bootstrap Monte Carlo SSA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00011)
*Sir-Lord Wiafe,Carter Hinsley,Vince D. Calhoun*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出BMC-SSA方法，结合蒙特卡洛奇异谱分析与自助法稳定性，从短时、高噪声的fMRI时间序列中提取可靠的振荡成分，改善延迟嵌入重构的状态空间几何，提升功能测量的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统延迟坐标嵌入理论需要长时、无噪声记录，而现实场景（如fMRI）中数据为短时、高噪声、粗采样，且存在自相关背景结构掩盖振荡成分、破坏嵌入稳定性，因此亟需一种能在有限噪声数据中稳健提取状态空间几何的方法。

Method: 将蒙特卡洛奇异谱分析（MC-SSA）与自助法（bootstrap）稳定性相结合：先通过MC-SSA识别统计上显著的振荡模式，再通过重采样评估其可重复性，仅保留既统计显著又在重采样中稳定的成分，从而构建去噪重建序列。

Result: BMC-SSA能有效保留可靠振荡结构，增强重建序列的确定性，稳定后续延迟嵌入；在fMRI数据中改善了功能连接等测量指标的可靠性，并揭示了状态空间动力学的组间差异。

Conclusion: BMC-SSA为短时、高噪声信号的鲁棒嵌入提供了一个通用框架，尤其适用于fMRI等神经影像数据，提升了非线性动力学重建的实用性。

Abstract: Reconstructing latent state-space geometry from time series provides a powerful route to studying nonlinear dynamics across complex systems. Delay-coordinate embedding provides the theoretical basis but assumes long, noise-free recordings, which many domains violate. In many real-world domains, recordings are short, noisy, and coarsely sampled; in neuroimaging, for example, fMRI additionally contains autocorrelated background structure that can obscure oscillatory components and destabilize embeddings. We propose bootstrap Monte Carlo singular spectrum analysis (BMC-SSA), which combines Monte Carlo SSA with bootstrap stability to retain oscillatory modes that are statistically supported and reproducible across resampled data. This produces reconstructions that emphasize reliable oscillatory structure, enhancing determinism and stabilizing subsequent embeddings. Our results show that BMC-SSA improves the reliability of functional measures and uncovers differences in state-space dynamics in fMRI, offering a general framework for robust embedding of noisy, finite signals.

</details>


### [393] [Cognitive Field Theory of Learning, Inference, and Emergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.10221)
*Byung Gyu Chae*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种认知场论（Cognitive Field Theory），将认知描述为一种集体非平衡现象，受学习得到的认知流形的几何与弛豫谱控制。通过记忆修饰的认知场方程，引入非局部记忆核和延迟自能反馈，并利用时间尺度态密度（TDOS）作为认知的基本动力学描述符，揭示了弱阻尼集体模式的积累如何抑制认知遗忘间隙、增强集体响应，并驱动系统进入近临界态，实现长时上下文持久性与集体认知相干性，从而统一描述学习、记忆、推理、自我意识与涌现认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物与人工系统中的学习、推理、记忆和涌现现象通常采用不同的理论框架描述，缺乏统一的动力学理论。作者旨在建立一个统一的认知场论，将认知视为集体非平衡现象，从几何与动力学角度揭示认知过程的共同底层机制。

Method: 从自适应黎曼认知流形上的随机认知场方程出发，推导出包含非局部记忆核和延迟自能反馈的记忆修饰认知场方程。分析学习得到的认知几何的局部稳定性结构，导出集体弛豫模式谱，定义时间尺度态密度（TDOS）作为认知的基本动力学描述符，并研究弱阻尼模式的集体效应对认知遗忘间隙、集体响应和近临界态的影响。

Result: TDOS决定了涌现的记忆核、集体响应和红外时间组织。弱阻尼集体模式的积累抑制了认知遗忘间隙，增强了集体敏感性，驱动系统进入受保护的近临界态，表现出长时上下文持久性和集体认知相干性。

Conclusion: 该框架以记忆修饰认知场的集体组织为统一基础，为学习、记忆、推理、自我意识和涌现认知提供了统一的动力学描述，有望成为连接认知科学、神经科学、统计物理学和机器学习的新理论范式。

Abstract: Learning, inference, memory, and emergence in biological and artificial systems are often described using disparate theoretical frameworks. Here we develop a cognitive field theory in which cognition is described as a collective nonequilibrium phenomenon governed by the geometry and relaxation spectrum of a learned cognitive manifold. Starting from a stochastic cognitive-field equation on an adaptive Riemannian cognitive manifold, we derive a memory-dressed cognitive field equation incorporating nonlocal memory kernels and retarded self-energy feedback. We show that the local stability structure of learned cognitive geometry generates a spectrum of collective relaxation modes whose distribution is characterized by a time-scale density of states (TDOS). The TDOS provides a fundamental dynamical descriptor of cognition and determines the emergent memory kernel, collective response, and infrared temporal organization of the cognitive field. The accumulation of weakly damped collective modes suppresses the cognitive forgetting gap, enhances collective susceptibility, and drives the system toward a protected near-critical regime characterized by long-time contextual persistence and collective cognitive coherence. The resulting framework provides a unified dynamical description of learning, memory, inference, selfhood, and emergent cognition in terms of the collective organization of a memory-dressed cognitive field.

</details>


### [394] [Fusion Learning from Dynamic Functional Connectivity: Combining the Amplitude and Phase of fMRI Signals to Identify Brain Disorders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24603)
*Jinlong Hu,Jiatong Huang,Zijian Cai*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多尺度融合学习框架（MSFL），通过结合滑动窗口相关（SWC）和相位同步（PS）两种互补的动态功能连接特征，利用fMRI信号的幅度和相位信息，在自闭症谱系障碍和重度抑郁症分类任务上显著优于现有模型，并通过SHAP分析验证了两种特征的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于滑动窗口相关的动态功能连接方法仅利用fMRI信号的幅度信息，忽略了相位信息，限制了脑疾病检测的性能。因此，本文旨在通过融合幅度和相位信息来提升脑疾病检测能力。

Method: 提出多尺度融合学习框架（MSFL），将滑动窗口相关（SWC）提取的幅度相关性特征与相位同步（PS）提取的相位一致性特征进行融合，构建互补的动态功能连接表示，用于脑疾病分类。

Result: 在ABIDE I（自闭症谱系障碍）和REST-meta-MDD（重度抑郁症）两个公开数据集上，MSFL显著优于现有对比模型。SHAP解释分析表明，SWC和PS两种动态功能连接特征均对疾病检测有贡献。

Conclusion: 融合幅度和相位信息的动态功能连接特征能够互补提升脑疾病检测性能，MSFL框架在自闭症谱系障碍和重度抑郁症分类任务中表现出优越性。

Abstract: Dynamic functional connectivity (dFC) derived from resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) has been extensively utilized in brain science research. The sliding window correlation (SWC) method is a widely used approach for constructing dFC by computing correlation coefficients between amplitude time series of signals from pairs of brain regions. In this study, we propose an integrated approach that incorporates both amplitude and phase information of fMRI signals to improve the detection of brain disorders. Specifically, we introduce a multi-scale fusion learning framework, namely MSFL, which leverages two complementary dFC features derived from SWC and phase synchronization (PS). Here, SWC captures amplitude correlations, while PS measures phase coherence within dFC. We evaluated the efficacy of MSFL in classifying autism spectrum disorder and major depressive disorder using two publicly available datasets: ABIDE I and REST-meta-MDD, respectively. The results indicate that MSFL significantly outperforms existing comparative models. Moreover, we performed model explanation analysis using the SHAP framework, which showed that both types of dFC features from SWC and PS contribute to detecting brain disorders.

</details>


### [395] [Contextual Role Modulates Object Representational Geometry in the Human Brain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23111)
*Julien Dirani,Shankar Chawla,Leila Wehbe,Bradford Z. Mahon*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究通过自然观影fMRI实验发现，人脑对同一物体的神经表征会根据其情境角色（被动场景元素 vs 目标导向行为的目标）进行动态重映射，目标物体激活顶叶动作网络并按动作 affordance 维度组织，被动物体激活枕颞视觉网络并按语义维度组织，而视觉表征结构保持情境不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前尚不清楚当同一物体在不同情境角色间转换时，大脑如何动态重映射物体表征，即物体在大脑中的表征如何兼具跨实例不变性和情境灵活性。

Method: 使用fMRI在自然观影条件下记录被试脑活动，对比同一物体作为被动场景元素与作为目标导向行为的目标时的神经表征差异，分析情境选择性网络内外的表征几何结构。

Result: 1）目标物体激活以缘上回和中央后回为中心的顶叶动作网络；被动物体激活分布式枕颞视觉物体识别网络。2）情境选择性网络中，目标物体按动作 affordance 和手姿势 affordance 维度组织，被动物体按语义维度组织。3）视觉表征结构跨情境保持不变，网络外部的表征内容也保持不变性。

Conclusion: 大脑在同一表征系统的不同层级上同时实现了灵活性与不变性：情境选择性网络中的表征几何随角色动态重映射，而视觉和网络外部表征保持跨情境不变，证明了自然场景中物体表征随情境角色实时变化的神经重映射机制。

Abstract: The human brain represents objects in a way that is both invariant across instances and flexible enough to support different contexts and tasks. Yet it remains unknown how object representations are dynamically remapped as the same object shifts across contextual roles. Using fMRI during naturalistic movie viewing we investigated how the same objects are represented when they are passive scene elements versus targets of goal-directed actions. Action targets engaged a parietal action network centered in the supramarginal and postcentral gyri, while passive objects recruited a distributed occipito-temporal network involved in visual object recognition. Within context-selective networks, representational geometry showed a double dissociation: target objects were organized by action affordance and hand posture affordance dimensions, while passive objects aligned with semantic dimensions. Visual representational structure was invariant to context. Outside these networks, representational content retained invariance, indicating that flexibility and invariance operate at different levels of the same representational system. These findings demonstrate neural remapping of object representations depending on moment-to-moment changes in contextual roles during a naturalistic scene.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [396] [AI SciBrief as a Gateway to Research: A Framework for Onboarding Students into New Research Areas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12413)
*Andrei Lazarev,Dmitrii Sedov*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个利用LLM驱动的AI SciBrief平台自动生成科学趋势摘要的教学框架，旨在降低高等教育学生的信息过载和研究入门障碍，帮助学生更快地从信息搜索过渡到知识创造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高等教育各阶段学生普遍面临信息过载问题，这阻碍了研究初期的进展并抑制了学习动机，亟需有效的教学方法来降低这一"入门门槛"。

Method: 提出一个基于AI SciBrief（由LLM驱动的自动科学趋势摘要生成平台）的教学框架，涵盖金融、医学、教育等多学科，并设计了具体的教学应用方法（如辅助学期论文选题、加速文献综述、帮助研究生持续追踪新兴趋势）。

Result: 该框架能够有效降低学生的认知负荷，使学生能够更快地从信息搜索阶段过渡到知识创造阶段。

Conclusion: AI SciBrief作为"研究门户"，能够有效降低学生的认知负荷，赋能学生更快速地从信息搜索转向知识创造。

Abstract: Students at all levels of higher education face a significant barrier in the form of information overload, which often paralyzes the initial stages of the research process and suppresses motivation. In response, this article introduces a pedagogical framework that leverages AI SciBrief, a platform powered by a Large Language Model (LLM) designed to automatically generate digests of scientific trends. We describe how this multidisciplinary tool - with initial coverage in finance, medicine, and education - can be integrated into the curriculum to overcome this "entry barrier." The framework provides concrete methodologies for utilizing these digests to facilitate topic selection for term papers, accelerate literature reviews for dissertations, and enable postgraduate students to continuously monitor emerging trends. We conclude that AI SciBrief functions as a "gateway to research" effectively reducing students' cognitive load and empowering them to transition more rapidly from information searching to knowledge creation.

</details>


### [397] [The Khipu Problem: Institutional Legibility Under Distributed Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12414)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 分布式AI系统中，由于认知分散于模型、工具、人类等多个组件，传统的以“有界模型/智能体”为对象的AI治理失效。本文提出“khipu问题”：记录虽存，但解读记录所需制度性实践却可能消亡。核心治理困境不再是“系统做了什么”，而是“后续机构能否读懂系统是什么”。作者主张用“解释连续性”替代“可观测性”作为治理基础，并提出治理工作空间与收据承载型治理表面作为制度性解读基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI治理默认治理对象是“有界模型”或“有界智能体”，但现实系统已将认知功能分布到模型、工具、人类、上下文存储、检索层、运行时策略、授权边界和委托制度角色中。这种结构性变化导致传统治理框架失效——当系统行为跨越多个组件和时间维度时，单纯保留痕迹不足以支撑事后审计与问责。作者试图揭示一种被忽视的治理失败类型：记录存在但解读能力消失。

Method: 概念分析和框架构建。通过引入“khipu问题”（源自印加结绳记事的隐喻，结绳保存但解读方法失传）作为理论透镜，区分缺失证据、歧义证据和结构性不可读证据三类治理困境；论证许多AI系统输出应被视为“分布式认知片段”而非有界模型输出；提出治理工作空间和收据承载型治理表面作为制度性解读基础设施的设计方案。

Result: 识别出一种新的治理失败类型——解释连续性丧失，它不同于普通缺乏可观测性；证明分布式AI系统的可读性依赖于周围解释脚手架，缺失该脚手架将导致结构性不可读证据；提出三类证据（缺失、歧义、结构性不可读）的分析框架；给出治理工作空间和收据承载型治理表面作为保留解释连续性的具体方案。

Conclusion: 分布式AI治理不能仅停留于痕迹保留，而必须主动维护解释连续性。机构需要建立制度性解读基础设施，以确保跨越时间和组件的行动身份、权限、边界真值、证据范围和结果性后果可被后续机构读取和理解。这是对当前AI治理范式的根本性扩展——从“追踪系统输出了什么”转向“保留系统可被读懂的制度条件”。

Abstract: AI governance still tends to assume that the relevant object is a bounded model or a bounded agent. That assumption is getting weaker. Real systems increasingly distribute cognition across models, tools, humans, context stores, retrieval layers, runtime policies, authorization boundaries, and delegated institutional roles. In such systems, the central governance problem is no longer only what the system did, but whether later institutions can still read what the system was. This paper introduces the khipu problem for distributed AI: the record can survive while the reading practice needed to interpret it decays. Logs, traces, model versions, tool calls, outputs, and approval artifacts may remain available while the institutional capacity to read them as parts of one coherent cognitive episode disappears. We argue that this failure is better understood as loss of interpretive continuity than as ordinary lack of observability. The result is a distinct governance failure. Institutions must classify, trust, audit, and constrain systems whose relevant identity is distributed across components and whose legibility depends on surrounding interpretive scaffolding. The problem is not merely missing data. It is a structural mismatch between what can be represented and what must still be decided under consequential conditions. We therefore argue that governance for distributed AI requires preservation of interpretive continuity, not only trace retention. The paper distinguishes missing evidence, ambiguous evidence, and structurally unreadable evidence; argues that many consequential outcomes are better understood as distributed cognitive episodes than as bounded model outputs; and proposes governance workspaces together with receipt-bearing governance surfaces as interpretive infrastructure for preserving action identity, authority, boundary truth, evidential scope, and consequential outcomes.

</details>


### [398] [The AI Legal Specialist: A Juridically Autonomous Professional Profile for AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12415)
*Nicola Fabiano*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出，面对全球AI监管的快速扩张（以欧盟《人工智能法案》为代表），现有的数据保护官、隐私律师、合规官等角色无法充分覆盖新兴的AI法律专业领域，需要建立一种全新的专业身份——AI法律专家。文章给出了该角色的法律定义，论证其相对于相邻职业的自主性，并提出了基于欧洲电子能力框架（e-CF）的能力架构及关键绩效指标的测量条件，旨在为该职业的国际标准化提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球多个司法管辖区（欧盟、美国、英国、加拿大、巴西、中国、日本、新加坡等）纷纷出台AI监管法规，产生了对AI法律专业人才的大量需求。然而，目前市场仅以碎片化的方式应对——数据保护官扩展其职责范围、隐私律师转向AI、合规官在现有手册中增加AI章节。本文认为这些适应性调整均不足以覆盖新兴的全球AI监管格局所开辟的职业空间，因而需要论证一种独立且专门化的职业身份。

Method: 本文采用法律推理与规范性分析的方法：首先从全球AI监管规范（尤其是欧盟AI法案、欧洲委员会AI框架公约、美国行政与行业框架等）的结构性义务出发，推导出AI法律专家的法律定义及其存在的法律依据；其次，通过比较分析论证该角色相对于相邻职业（数据保护官、隐私律师、合规官等）和国际技术标准的自主性；再次，借鉴欧洲电子能力框架（e-CF, EN 16234-1）作为方法论选择，提出参考能力架构；最后，明确通过关键绩效指标进行操作性测量的条件。

Result: 本文得出以下成果：（1）提出了一个具有法律依据的"AI法律专家"职业定义，该角色是接受过高级法律培训、在法律解释与AI治理交叉领域运作的法学家；（2）论证了该角色的法律自主性——它来源于实质性AI监管产生的监管义务结构，而非技术标准或相邻角色的延伸；（3）构建了与欧洲电子能力框架（e-CF）对齐的参考能力架构；（4）阐明了该角色操作性测量的条件，包括关键绩效指标的设计原则。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于为AI法律专家职业的国际标准化奠定基础，并为实践、课程设置以及跨司法管辖区的采用提供参考。文章主张，面对全球AI监管的复杂格局，迫切需要将AI法律专家确立为一种独立、统一的专业身份，而非现有角色的简单延伸。

Abstract: The rapid global expansion of artificial intelligence regulation has generated, across multiple jurisdictions, a demand for legal expertise dedicated to AI that the market has addressed in a fragmented manner. Data protection officers extend their remit beyond data protection law; privacy lawyers reposition themselves toward AI; compliance officers add AI chapters to their existing manuals. This paper argues that none of these adaptive responses adequately covers the professional space opened by the emerging global AI regulatory landscape, of which the EU Artificial Intelligence Act (Regulation (EU) 2024/1689) is the most comprehensive instance, alongside the Council of Europe Framework Convention on AI, the United States executive and sectoral framework, and analogous initiatives in the United Kingdom, Canada, Brazil, China, Japan, Singapore, and beyond. A distinct professional profile is required: the AI Legal Specialist, conceived as a jurist -- understood broadly to encompass any professional with advanced legal training -- operating at the intersection of legal interpretation and AI governance.
