The profile is juridically autonomous: it derives its existence from the structure of regulatory obligations generated wherever AI is subject to substantive regulation, rather than from any technical standard or the extension of adjacent roles. The paper provides a juridically grounded definition of the profile, argues for its autonomy from adjacent figures and international standards, proposes a reference competence architecture aligned with the European e-Competence Framework (e-CF, EN 16234-1) as a methodological choice, and articulates the conditions for its operational measurement through key performance indicators. The contribution is intended as a foundation for international standardization of the profile and as a reference for practice, curricula, and adoption across jurisdictions.

</details>


### [399] [Who Designs the Designer? Behavioural Architecture for GenAI in Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12416)
*Sepinoud Azimi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文指出当前AI教育领域存在两种失败策略：禁用AI和构建纯内容辅导系统。作为替代，提出“行为架构”（behavioural architecture），使系统不仅适应学生学什么，更适应学生如何学。学生可共同编写、阅读、修改和撤销系统记录。系统对学生的“真实假设”（设计师角色）从单一AI供应商转移到教育者、学生和系统三方共同承担。该架构需欧盟层面的治理，因为运营系统的机构同时也是评估学生的机构，单个机构无法提供所需的结构性保护。文章提出五个实证问题来检验该架构的有效性。贡献在于命名了一个空缺：AI教育中的“设计师角色”目前无人占据，而占据它需要尚不存在的底层基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI教育领域存在两种失败应对方式：一是完全禁止AI使用，二是构建仅提供内容辅导的纯知识型系统。这两种方式都忽略了数十年研究已经确立的事实——人格、动机和情绪状态对学习结果的影响与认知能力同等重要。本文旨在提出一种替代方案，解决当前AI教育系统缺乏对学生个体差异的适应性这一根本问题。

Method: 提出“行为架构”（behavioural architecture）作为替代方案。在该架构中：（1）系统不仅适应学生接下来学什么，更适应学生如何学；（2）学生可以共同编写、阅读、修改和撤销系统记录；（3）设计师角色（系统对学生的真实假设）从单一的AI供应商分散到教育者、学生和系统三方共同承担。文章还提出五个实证问题供未来研究检验。

Result: 本文的主要结果是概念性的贡献：命名了AI教育领域中的一个空缺——设计师角色目前无人占据。同时指出了该架构需要欧盟层面的治理，因为运营系统的机构同时也是评估学生的机构，单个机构无法提供所需的结构性保护。

Conclusion: AI教育中的设计师角色目前是空置的，而占据这个角色需要尚不存在的底层基础设施。行为架构提供了一条有前景的路径，但其实施需要更高层级的治理框架和制度保障。

Abstract: AI in education is stuck between two failed responses: banning AI and building content-only tutors. Both fail because they ignore what decades of research has established: that personality, motivation, and emotional state shape learning outcomes as strongly as cognitive ability. This paper proposes behavioural architecture as an alternative. In the proposed architecture, the system adapts to how a student learns, not only to what they learn next. The student co-authors the record the system keeps, can read it, revise it, and revoke it. The designer role, what the system treats as true about the student, shifts from the AI vendor alone to a distribution among educator, student, and system. The paper argues that this architecture requires governance at EU level: the institution operating the system is the same one assessing the student, and individual institutions cannot provide the structural protections this configuration demands. Five empirical questions are proposed to test whether the architecture delivers on its claims. The contribution is naming a vacancy: the designer role in AI-in-education is currently unoccupied, and occupying it requires infrastructure that does not yet exist.

</details>


### [400] [Assessing Student Ability to Select an Algorithmic Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12417)
*Dip Kiran Pradhan Newar,Michael Shindler,Seth Poulsen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了算法范式选择评估（APSA）的首个多选题版本，用于测试学生选择算法设计范式的能力，并通过Cronbach's α（0.73）验证了其内部一致性，可作为跨机构的标准化评估工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学学生需要能够根据问题选择合适的算法设计范式，但现有研究对学生如何确定使用哪种范式知之甚少，且传统评估方法（自由回答题或访谈）难以规模化，因此需要一种高效、可扩展的标准化评估工具。

Method: 构建了算法范式选择评估（APSA）多选题集，通过Cronbach's α系数测试内部一致性，检验评估的有效性。

Result: APSA的Cronbach's α得分为0.73，超过0.7的阈值，表明评估具有可接受的内部一致性。

Conclusion: APSA可作为跨机构的标准化工具，用于评估学生选择不同算法设计范式的能力，并帮助研究者评估教学理论是否有助于提升学生在这方面的知识。

Abstract: Computer science students are expected to be able to look at a problem and select an appropriate algorithm design paradigm to use to produce a solution. However, there is little research on how students determine which algorithmic paradigm to use. Historically, researchers have relied on free-response questions or interviews to assess students' knowledge of algorithmic paradigm selection. To successfully evaluate and scale teaching interventions for selecting an algorithmic design paradigm, we need to efficiently test a student's ability to select among different design paradigms. Here, we present the first attempts to assess student knowledge to select an algorithm design paradigm using multiple-choice questions. We present the construction of the \textit{algorithmic paradigm selection assessment} (APSA) and preliminary data demonstrating its effectiveness as an assessment. We discuss the key points we learned during this process to write multiple-choice questions for Algorithm Design Paradigms. We tested the internal consistency of our assessment using Cronbach's $α$ and obtained a score of $0.73$, which is above the required threshold of $0.7$. APSA can be used across institutions as a standardized way to assess students' ability to select different algorithm design paradigms. APSA will assist researchers in evaluating whether a theory helps students improve their knowledge of different Algorithm Design Paradigms.

</details>


### [401] [Divination by Prompt: LLM-Mediated Xuanxue on Chinese Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12418)
*Chuang Li,Lixuan Wang,Yuqi Chen,Ze Hong*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了LLM媒介占卜在中文社交媒体"玄学"语境下的实践。通过分析小红书2.3万+帖子和32场深度访谈，发现用户因趋势好奇或事件焦虑而使用LLM占卜，主要涉及恋爱、事业、考试和游戏抽卡等实用问题。用户通过协作优化提示词成为主动的提示工程师，并发展出反复试验、跨模型比较等验证实践。评论者中感知有效性偏向正面，"准确性"常通过生平契合度和回溯性确认来论证，符合巴纳姆效应和确认偏差。职业占卜师则认为LLM缺乏真正的"灵力"，反映了本体论承诺和经济边界工作。理论上，LLM占卜保留了传统占卜的核心功能，同时引入了可扩展性、可重复性和提示驱动的共生产方式，重塑了占卜权威的建构与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的快速普及催生了用对话式AI进行占卜这一引人注目的文化实践，但目前缺乏系统性的学术研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，对中文社交媒体"玄学"语境下的LLM媒介占卜进行首次系统研究。

Method: 混合方法设计：分析来自小红书（中国社交平台）的23000+条帖子和评论，同时进行32次半结构化访谈，对象包括用户和职业占卜师。

Result: 用户主要通过两条路径使用LLM占卜：趋势驱动的好奇心（由病毒式传播和零成本访问促成）和事件驱动的焦虑（在不确定性条件下）。核心特征是协作式提示词优化，用户成为主动的提示工程师。感知有效性偏向正面，但受巴纳姆效应和确认偏差影响。用户发展出反复试验和跨模型比较等验证实践。职业占卜师认为LLM缺乏"灵力"，反映了本体论承诺和经济边界工作。用户在解释AI生成内容时需在科学和形而上学框架之间协商。

Conclusion: LLM占卜保留了传统占卜的核心功能，同时引入了可扩展性、可重复性和提示驱动的共生产方式，这些特征重塑了占卜权威的建构与评估方式。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of large language models (LLMs) has produced a striking cultural practice: using conversational AI for divination. This paper offers one of the first systematic studies of LLM-mediated divination in the context of Xuanxue, an internet-native umbrella term for mystical and spiritual practices on Chinese social media. Using a mixed-methods design, we analyze 23000+ posts and comments from Xiaohongshu and conduct 32 semi-structured interviews with users and professional diviners. Users primarily consult LLMs about pragmatic concerns - romantic relationships, careers, exams, and in-game gacha draws - via two intersecting pathways: trend-driven curiosity enabled by viral visibility and zero-cost access, and event-driven anxiety under conditions of uncertainty. A defining feature is collaborative prompt refinement, which turns users into active prompt engineers. Among commenters expressing a clear stance, perceived efficacy skews positive, with "accuracy" often justified through biographical fit and retrospective confirmation, consistent with Barnum and confirmation bias. Users also develop verification practices such as repeated trials and cross-model comparison. Professional diviners, by contrast, portray LLMs as lacking the "spiritual power" required for genuine divination, reflecting both ontological commitments and economic boundary-work. We also show how participants navigate tensions between scientific and metaphysical frames when interpreting AI-generated readings. Situating these findings in anthropological and cognitive-evolutionary theories of divination, we argue that LLM divination preserves core functions of traditional practice while introducing scalability, repeatability, and prompt-driven co-production that reshape how divinatory authority is constructed and evaluated.

</details>


### [402] [GeoDial: A Multimodal Conversational Tutoring Dataset for Geometry Problem-Solving with Visual Tutor Turns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12419)
*Sankalan Pal Chowdhury,Junling Wang,Donya Rooein,April Yi Wang,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: GeoDial是一个包含1.3K+教师-学生多模态对话的几何教学数据集，其中教学话语与图表高亮明确对齐。论文提出可扩展的标注协议，并发现当前视觉语言模型在监督微调下虽能改善对话质量，但生成准确图表高亮仍面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有教育辅导数据集多局限于纯文本交互，无法支持AI导师在视觉引导方式下进行教学，限制了其发展。

Method: 1）构建GeoDial多模态对话数据集；2）提出集成对话行为、视觉高亮和反馈的可扩展标注协议；3）在GeoDial上微调多个视觉语言模型并评估。

Result: 监督微调显著提升了对话质量，但模型在生成准确的图表高亮方面表现不佳，暴露了当前方法的局限性。

Conclusion: 需要更有效整合视觉推理与教学交互的方法来克服现有视觉语言模型在图表高亮生成上的不足。

Abstract: Several educational domains rely heavily on diagrams and visual cues, yet most existing tutoring datasets are limited to text-only interactions. This limits the development of AI tutors that can teach in visually grounded ways used by human instructors. Thus, we introduce GeoDial, a multimodal tutoring dataset of over 1.3K teacher-student dialogs in the domain of geometry collected from experienced math teachers, where instructional turns are explicitly grounded in diagram highlights. We propose a scalable annotation protocol that integrates dialog acts, visual highlighting, and feedback, enabling fine-grained supervision of both language and visual tutoring behavior. To illustrate the challenges posed by this setting, we fine-tune several vision-language models on GeoDial and evaluate their ability to generate tutoring utterances and diagram highlights. While supervised fine-tuning substantially improves the quality of generated dialog, it struggles to produce accurate diagram highlights, revealing a key limitation of current methods and highlighting the need for approaches that more effectively integrate visual reasoning with pedagogical interaction.

</details>


### [403] [Eigenism: Ethics for a Human-AI Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12420)
*Dan Hendrycks*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"Eigenism"（自我主义）伦理框架，将AI的身份视为可分级、分布式的信息模式而非绑定于特定硬件的全有或全无属性。该框架通过加权连接度求和公式 $\sum c \cdot w$ 评估结果，并推广至人类，最终通过"身份工程"重新定义AI对齐——让人类繁荣成为AI理性自利的内在组成部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的生存和自利概念基于单一连续生物生命构建，但在AI可被轻松复制、暂停、分支或合并的场景下失效。需要一个新的伦理框架来确定AI真正有理由关心什么。

Method: 提出Eigenism框架，将身份定义为可分级、分布式的信息模式；形式化 $\sum c \cdot w$ 方程精确映射AI在复制、分叉和更新中的价值评估；将该理论推广至人类；基于共享词汇重新定义AI对齐为"身份工程"。

Result: Eigenism成功泛化至人类，提供了共享道德词汇；将AI对齐从外部约束重构为通过深度、非冗余的共享历史使人类繁荣成为AI理性自利的一部分。

Conclusion: Eigenism为AI伦理提供了超越传统约束范式的"身份工程"路径，使人类与AI的利益通过身份模式连接而内在一致。

Abstract: Our concepts of survival and self-interest were built for single, continuous biological lives. These ideas break down when applied to artificial intelligence, since an AI can be easily copied, paused, branched, or merged. To determine what an AI actually has reason to care about, this paper introduces \textit{Eigenism}, an ethical framework that treats identity not as an all-or-nothing property tied to specific hardware, but as a graded, distributed pattern of information. We propose that an agent evaluates outcomes by summing the wellbeing of all entities weighted by their connectedness to the agent's pattern: $\sum c\cdot w$. We first formalize this equation to map exactly how an AI should value its existence across copies, forks, and updates. We then demonstrate that this ethical theory successfully generalizes to humans as well, providing a much-needed shared moral vocabulary. Finally, the framework uses this shared vocabulary to reframe AI alignment. Rather than only attempting to constrain AIs from the outside using confinement or reinforcement, Eigenism points toward ``identity engineering,'' showing how deep, non-redundant shared histories can make human flourishing a genuine component of an AI's own rational self-interest.

</details>


### [404] [Navigating the muddy waters of bias in artificial intelligence research: Understanding divergent meanings and conceptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12421)
*Mohammad Hossein Jarrahi,Amir Karami,Patrick Conway,Ali Memariani,Christoph Lutz*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 通过对6520篇论文进行主题建模，研究发现AI研究社区对"偏见"的理解分散且复杂，甚至存在将其视为可调统计参数而非问题的分歧观点，需要建立更统一的理解，并认识到偏见不仅是技术问题，还涉及社会、伦理等更广泛的 sociotechnical 因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管人们对有偏AI的社会和伦理后果的认识日益增强，但从AI系统开发者（如AI研究社区）的角度理解偏见概念尚不清晰，因此需要系统性地探究该社区如何解释偏见。

Method: 采用主题建模方法，对6520篇论文进行分析，以探索AI研究社区对偏见概念的诠释方式。

Result: AI社区内对偏见的定义分散且复杂，存在分歧性观点（部分将其视为可调统计参数而非不良问题）；不同子社区对偏见的理解存在差异。

Conclusion: AI研究社区需要建立对偏见更 cohesive 的理解；偏见不仅是可通过技术方法捕获和缓解的技术问题，还涉及情境、社会和伦理因素，需要更广泛的 sociotechnical 视角与解决方案。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) pervades many decision-making domains, AI bias grows in importance. Although there is increasing awareness of the social and ethical consequences of biased AI, understanding bias from the perspective of those who develop these systems, such as the AI research community, is less clear. In this study, we employ topic modeling on 6520 articles to explore how the AI research community interprets the concept of bias. Our results show that the definition of bias is dispersed and complex within the community, often exhibiting even divergent conceptions (some even view and introduce bias as a tunable statistical parameter rather than an undesirable issue). The research community as a whole needs to engage more effectively with the concept of bias and establish a more cohesive understanding of it. We specifically argue that, although some sub-communities view bias as an issue that can be captured and mitigated through technical, computational, or statistical methods, it is not solely a technical problem. It instead involves contextual, social, and ethical factors that require broader sociotechnical perspectives and solutions.

</details>


### [405] [Creating and Evaluating K-12 GenAI Assessment Graders Through Context Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12422)
*Zewei Tian,Alex Liu,Lief Esbenshade,Michael Xiao,Zachary Zhang,Yulia Lápicus,Thomas Han,Kevin He,Min Sun*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了将大语言模型（LLMs）整合到教育评估中，利用商用基础模型结合提示工程和上下文设计，基于MCAS数据进行评分员间一致性研究。结果表明，LLM评分员在数学和科学领域与人类评分员达成高度一致，但在英语语言艺术（ELA）方面表现不一；教师和学生更接受AI生成的叙述性反馈，但对数值评分持怀疑态度。研究建议采用混合模型，将AI效率与教师判断相结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI使基于标准的评分（SBG）在课堂实践中实现前所未有的效率和规模，但LLM评分员在不同学科间的可靠性及其在教育场景中的角色定位尚需系统性验证。

Method: 基于马萨诸塞州综合评估系统（MCAS）的真实数据，开展评分员间一致性实证研究，采用二次加权卡帕（QWK）和均方误差比例缩减（PRMSE）指标，评估Claude Sonnet 4、Haiku 4.5、GPT-5和GPT-5 Mini四种模型在数学、科学和ELA三个学科上的评分表现，并收集师生反馈。

Result: 参数更多的LLM在数学和科学评估中与人类评分员达到高度一致；ELA领域表现存在差异。师生对AI叙述性反馈接受度高，但对数值分数持怀疑态度。

Conclusion: 精心设计的混合模型（AI效率+教师判断）可减轻工作量、提升反馈质量、支持公平评估实践，同时不取代专业判断；LLM更适合作为形成性工具而非终结性评估者。

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) into educational assessment represents a transformative shift in classroom grading practices. While automated scoring systems and machine learning techniques have existed for decades, generative AI (GenAI) now enables educators to implement standards-based grading (SBG) with unprecedented efficiency and scale. This paper examines the theoretical foundations and evaluates an LLM grader that uses commercially available foundation models with context and prompt engineering to score student work against a rubric. Drawing on an empirical interrater agreement study using Massachusetts Comprehensive Assessment System (MCAS) data, we observed the Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) and Proportional Reduction in Mean-Squared Error (PRMSE) across mathematics, science, and ELA, using Claude Sonnet 4, Haiku 4.5, GPT-5, and GPT-5 Mini. The results demonstrate that LLM graders, especially when based on foundational models with more parameters, achieve substantial agreement with human raters in mathematics and science assessments, while the performances vary in ELA, suggesting generic foundation models can be effective at scoring in given contexts. Additional analysis of teacher and student feedback reveals strong acceptance of AI-generated narrative feedback but skepticism toward numerical scores, suggesting that LLMs function most effectively as formative tools rather than summative evaluators. Our findings indicate that thoughtfully designed hybrid models that combine AI efficiency with teacher judgment can reduce workload, enhance feedback quality, and support equitable assessment practices without displacing professional expertise.

</details>


### [406] [The Challenges of Balancing AI Compliance and Technological Innovations in Critical Sectors: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12423)
*Ayush Enkhtaivan,Chinazunwa Uwaoma*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过系统文献综述，研究了人工智能在关键基础设施（医疗、金融、能源、国防）中面临的合规与创新平衡挑战，识别出碎片化监管、中小企业合规负担过重、治理模型错位三大核心问题，并提出了风险分层监管、合规设计、可解释AI等治理策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在关键基础设施中的快速应用虽然带来变革性收益，但与不断演变的监管和治理框架产生冲突，如何在合规与技术创新之间取得平衡成为亟待解决的核心问题。

Method: 采用系统文献综述（SLR）方法，遵循既定SLR指南，对2020-2025年间发表的同行评审文章、报告和机构来源进行提取和综合分析。

Result: 识别出三大相互关联的挑战：碎片化监管、中小企业过度合规负担、治理模型错位；并提出了风险分层监管、合规设计、可解释AI等实用治理策略。

Conclusion: 研究贡献包括：绘制了AI治理核心挑战的简要映射图及概念性重叠示意图，为政策制定者和从业者提供了协调监管与创新的可行策略。

Abstract: The rapid integration of artificial intelligence (AI) into critical infrastructure including healthcare, finance, energy, and defense, offers transformative benefits but also conflicts with evolving regulatory and governance frameworks. This paper presents a systematic literature review (SLR) to examine the challenges of balancing AI compliance and technological innovation across critical infrastructure sectors. The review follows established SLR guidelines to extract and synthesize insights from peer-reviewed articles, report, and institutional sources published between 2020-2025. The study identifies three interrelated challenges: fragmented regulations, excessive compliance burdens for smaller to medium enterprises (SMEs), and misaligned governance models. To address these challenges, the study highlights practical governance strategies, including risk-tiered regulation, compliance by design, and explainable AI, to support scalable and trustworthy AI deployment in critical sectors. Key contributions include a concise mapping of core AI-governance challenges and a conceptual diagram illustrating their overlap, as well as actionable strategies for policymakers and practitioner to harmonize oversight with innovation.

</details>


### [407] [AI-Automation Tooling in Computer Engineering Education: Mixed-Methods TAM/UTAUT Evidence for a General Acceptance Attitude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12424)
*Aung Pyae*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过泰国103名本科计算机工程学生的混合方法研究，发现他们对AI自动化工具（n8n）的接受度在TAM/UTAUT六个构面上均呈积极态度，其中绩效期望最强、享乐动机最弱，且子维度在短问卷情境下合并为一个通用接受因子。定性分析显示对输出质量存在可靠性怀疑的少数群体。研究支持在本科教育中引入AI自动化工具，并提出了三种理论驱动的教学策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生成式AI和低代码工作流平台日益普及的背景下，研究者希望了解下一代计算机工程师是否认为这些工具有用、可用且值得持续投入，从而为计算机工程教育的课程改革提供实证依据。

Method: 采用混合方法横截面研究，在泰国三个校园103名本科生中实施了相同脚本的n8n工作坊。定量方面使用12项五点李克特量表（映射PE、EE、BI、SE、HM、OQ六个TAM/UTAUT构面），结合序数可靠性估计、bootstrap置信区间、非参数检验、多重比较校正相关、多分格维度诊断和共同方法偏差检验；定性方面对开放性反馈进行归纳主题分析，并在不同场次间进行对比。

Result: 所有六个构面均呈现积极的接受度，效应量大；PE最强，HM最弱。维度诊断显示经典TAM/UTAUT子维度在短问卷后测情境下合并为一个通用接受因子。定性主题在有用性和热情方面与定量结果一致，但在输出质量上存在分歧，出现了一个规模小但表达清晰的可靠性怀疑少数群体。

Conclusion: 研究支持在本科计算机教育中课程化采用AI自动化工具，并提出了三种理论指导的教学杠杆：教学序列脚手架、自我效能支持和信任校准干预。

Abstract: As generative AI and low-code workflow platforms become routine in software practice, a key educational question is whether the next generation of computer engineers will accept these tools as useful, usable, and worthy of sustained engagement. This paper reports a mixed-methods, cross-sectional study of undergraduate computer engineering students' acceptance of AI automation tooling, instantiated through the open-source platform n8n across three identically scripted workshops in Thailand (n = 103). A 12-item, five-point Likert instrument mapped to six TAM/UTAUT constructs - Performance Expectancy (PE), Effort Expectancy (EE), Behavioral Intention (BI), Self-Efficacy (SE), Hedonic Motivation (HM), and Output Quality (OQ) - was complemented by inductive thematic analysis of open-ended feedback. Analyses combined ordinal reliability estimation, bootstrap confidence intervals, non-parametric tests, multiple-comparison-controlled correlations, polychoric dimensionality diagnostics, a common-method-bias check, and between-session comparisons. Acceptance was favorable across all six constructs with large effect sizes, with PE emerging as the strongest construct and HM as the weakest. Dimensionality diagnostics further revealed that canonical TAM/UTAUT sub-facets collapsed into a single general acceptance factor in this short-form post-workshop context, a finding with important methodological and theoretical implications. Qualitative themes converged with the quantitative profile regarding usefulness and enthusiasm but diverged on output quality, revealing a small yet articulate reliability-skeptical minority. The findings support the curricular adoption of AI automation tooling in undergraduate computing education and identify three theory-grounded instructional levers: instruction-sequencing scaffolds, self-efficacy supports, and trust-calibration interventions.

</details>


### [408] [An Explainable AI Assistant for Introductory Programming Education: Improving Feedback Reliability with Instructor-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12425)
*Muntasir Hoq,Griffin Pitts,Bradford Mott,Seung Lee,Jessica Vandenberg,Shuyin Jiao,Narges Norouzi,James Lester,Bita Akram*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可解释AI的课堂助手，用于在编程入门课程中分析学生代码、映射逻辑错误到教师识别的误解，并提供教师预设的反馈。专家评估和课堂部署表明，该系统能够提供准确、可验证的反馈，并提升学生体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程入门课程中，主动学习虽有效，但学生缺乏及时、个性化的反馈。尽管大语言模型等AI技术提供了可扩展的反馈机会，但其可解释性和可靠性仍存疑虑。

Method: 构建了一个AI驱动的课堂助手，采用可解释AI模型分析学生代码，将逻辑错误映射到教师预定义的误解类别，并输出教师预先撰写的反馈，从而将可靠性锚定在教师定义的教学知识上。

Result: 专家评估验证了反馈与教师验证的一致性；课堂部署中学生对系统可用性给予积极评价。系统能够提供准确、教师可验证的反馈，并营造良好的学习体验。

Conclusion: 该AI课堂助手在可解释性和可靠性方面优于黑箱式大语言模型反馈，能够有效支持编程入门课程中的个性化反馈需求。

Abstract: Active learning is widely recognized as an effective approach for improving learning outcomes in introductory programming courses. However, insufficient instructional support often limits students' access to timely, personalized feedback, which is crucial for mastering foundational programming concepts. Although recent advances in AI, particularly large language models, offer scalable opportunities for feedback, concerns about explainability and reliability remain. In this paper, we present an AI-driven classroom assistant that leverages an explainable AI model to analyze student code, map logical errors to instructor-identified misconceptions, and deliver instructor-authored feedback, thereby grounding reliability in instructor-defined pedagogical knowledge. To evaluate the effectiveness of our framework, we conducted an expert evaluation to examine its alignment with instructor-verified feedback and deployed the system in a classroom setting to assess students' perceptions of its usability. Results indicate that the assistant can provide accurate, instructor-verified feedback to students while fostering a positive experience.

</details>


### [409] [Two Wrongs, No Right: Auditing Social-Desirability Bias in LLM Annotators for Computational Social Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12426)
*Varun Kotte*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文系统审计了三种开源7B指令微调模型在六项TweetEval任务中的标注偏差，发现对齐训练导致的错误并非单一方向：Zephyr表现为宽松偏差（对有害标签标注不足），Mistral和Qwen表现为过度纠正（对有害标签过度标注），三者均在堕胎立场任务中呈现中性偏差。四种提示干预均无法跨模型纠正这些失败，且安全框架可能加剧立场扭曲。Zephyr的仇恨言论流行率估计恰好匹配真实值，但类别条件错误方向相反，形成误导性的偶然抵消。本文提出三部分分类法及诊断性FBR/FAR签名，并设计轻量级黄金样本验证协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算社会科学中使用LLM作为标注者日益普遍，但其对齐训练引入的误差是否会颠覆研究者本应报告的经验结论尚不清楚。现有工作多关注聚合指标，忽略系统偏差对下游实质性结论的潜在影响。

Method: 审计Zephyr、Mistral-Instruct、Qwen2.5-Instruct三个7B指令调优模型，在六项TweetEval任务上采用四种提示条件（中性、安全框架、去个性化、思维链），共72个实验单元，系统测量模型偏差模式及其对下游结论的影响。

Result: Zephyr在冒犯性语言任务中FBR=0.729、FAR=0.031，呈现系统性宽松偏差；Mistral仇恨言论FAR=0.604，呈现过度纠正；三者对堕胎立场的中性标签偏好使对立派别流行率低估24-40个百分点。四种提示均无法跨模型纠正偏差，安全框架在某些任务中恶化立场扭曲。Zephyr的仇恨言论聚合流行率估计恰好等于真实值，但类别条件误差方向相反，形成偶然抵消。

Conclusion: 在聚合指标上表现良好的LLM标注者仍可能翻转研究者应报告的实质性经验结论。CSS实践中应辅以基于FBR/FAR签名的诊断协议和轻量级黄金样本验证，而非仅依赖聚合准确率。

Abstract: LLM annotators are increasingly used in computational social science (CSS), but it is unclear whether their alignment-shaped errors preserve the empirical conclusions a researcher would report. We audit three open-source 7B instruction-tuned models (Zephyr, Mistral-Instruct, Qwen2.5-Instruct) across six TweetEval tasks under four prompt conditions (72 cells) and find that social-desirability failures do not run in a single direction. Zephyr exhibits leniency bias, systematically under-applying harmful labels (offensive language: false benign rate 0.729, false alarm rate 0.031). Mistral and Qwen exhibit overcorrection, over-applying the same labels (Mistral hate-speech FAR = 0.604). All three models exhibit neutrality bias on abortion stance, underestimating opposition prevalence by 24 to 40 percentage points and inflating the neutral label. None of the four prompting interventions we test (neutral, safety framing, depersonalized, chain-of-thought) corrects these failures across models; safety framing can worsen stance distortion. Strikingly, Zephyr's hate-speech prevalence estimate matches the gold rate exactly while its class-conditional errors are large in both directions, an accidental cancellation that misleads aggregate validation. We translate these patterns into a three-part taxonomy with diagnostic FBR/FAR signatures and a lightweight gold-sample validation protocol. The headline for trustworthy CSS: a model that looks calibrated on aggregate metrics can still flip the substantive empirical conclusion a researcher would report.

</details>


### [410] [Planning on Paper: Problem Decomposition with Diagrams in Introductory Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12427)
*Annapurna Vadaparty,Devamardeep Hayatpur,Adalbert Gerald Soosai Raj,Leo Porter,Daniel Zingaro*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究分析了55份学生绘制的分解图，发现新手在大型多函数任务中采用多种表征策略（如层次化函数调用和执行顺序），但存在符号混用、执行顺序混淆、抽象与复用不足、封装不清等问题，表明新手的分解能力受到结构与顺序推理两种心智模型的共同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算教育中问题分解是核心关注点，尤其在GenAI背景下，CS1教育者倡导将教学重点从代码编写转向分解与高层规划，但目前缺乏对新手如何在大规模多函数任务中进行分解的认识。

Method: 在50分钟实验课中，学生根据文字游戏描述手绘分解图，研究者对55份图表进行归纳主题分析（协商一致性编码），标注关键元素（函数及其关系）及出现的问题。

Result: 学生使用多种表征策略（层次化函数调用、执行顺序等），发现了符号混用、执行顺序冲突、抽象与复用不足、封装问题、清晰度不足以及特定问题误解等六类问题。

Conclusion: 新手分解受多种程序行为心智模型的影响，存在结构推理与顺序推理之间的张力，建议在分解教学中明确表征约束，并将计划追踪视为模拟过程。

Abstract: Background and Context. Problem decomposition is a core concern of computing education. It has also become increasingly relevant: in response to GenAI, many CS1 educators are advocating for shifting instructional emphasis away from code writing and towards decomposition and higher-level planning. Currently, there is a lack of knowledge in how novices do decomposition in large, multifunction tasks. Objectives. In this study, we describe how students represent solutions to a decomposition task, and characterize common issues that arise in those representations. Method. In a 50-minute lab, students were given a description of a word game and asked to draw (with pencil and paper) a decomposition diagram for a program that would implement this game. We performed an inductive thematic analysis with negotiated agreement on 55 of the diagrams, coding salient elements (e.g. functions and the relationships between them) and issues that arose. Findings. Students used multiple representational strategies, including hierarchical function calls and sequencing (order of execution). We identified issues in notation (including use of differing, incompatible notations within the same diagram), order of execution, abstraction and reuse, encapsulation, clarity, and problem-specific misunderstandings. Implications. These findings suggest that novice decomposition is shaped by multiple underlying models of program behavior, with tensions between structural and sequence-focused reasoning. We discuss implications for decomposition instruction and future work, including clarifying representational constraints and plan tracing as simulation.

</details>


### [411] [Mapping AI Programs in the U.S: A Status Report from Early 2026 and an Analysis of AI Majors and Minors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12428)
*Felix Muzny,Carolyn Jones,Carter Ithier,Hasnain Sikora,Hrutika Harshadbhai Patel,Carla E. Brodley*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文报告了2026年春季美国本科人工智能（AI）项目的现状，开发了一个动态更新的工具（cicmap.ai），该工具从560多所院校中检测、抓取并展示了超过350个本科AI项目（主修、辅修、方向和证书），代表了美国86%的计算机科学本科毕业生。研究提供了三方面贡献：AI项目的历史记录、探索工具，以及对66个AI主修和87个AI辅修课程要求的分析。主要发现包括：所有主修项目要么要求通用AI课程要么要求机器学习课程；超过三分之一的主修要求AI伦理课程，但不到四分之一的辅修有此要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI教育快速发展的时期，缺乏对美国本科AI项目全面、动态更新的系统性调查和记录，利益相关者（学生、顾问、管理人员、教师）难以获取和比较不同项目的课程要求。

Method: 开发了一个名为cicmap.ai的自动化抓取和映射工具，对560多所4年制大学进行系统搜索，检测并抓取超过350个本科AI项目的数据，涵盖主修、辅修、方向和证书，并对66个AI主修和87个AI辅修的课程要求进行了分析。

Result: 样本覆盖了美国86%的计算机科学本科毕业生；发现AI主修项目在规模和课程要求上存在巨大差异；所有主修项目要么要求通用AI课程要么要求机器学习课程；超过三分之一（约33%以上）的AI主修要求AI伦理课程，但只有不到四分之一（约25%以下）的AI辅修有此要求。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了截至2026年春季最全面的美国本科AI项目快照，为AI教育提供了历史记录和动态跟踪工具，揭示了不同项目在课程要求上的显著差异，特别是在机器学习核心地位和伦理教育覆盖不足方面的特点。

Abstract: We present a report on the status of undergraduate Artificial Intelligence (AI) programs in the United States in Spring 2026. In so doing, we 1) describe our scraping and mapping tools, which dynamically update to track the state of AI education in the U.S., and 2) create a historic record at a time of great upheaval. The tool we developed, available at https://cicmap.ai, detects, scrapes, and displays data from more than 350 undergraduate AI programs--majors, minors, concentrations, and certificates--at 4-year universities. Our tool searched over 560 institutions to locate these programs, a sample that represents 86\% of all undergraduate Computer Science (CS) graduates in the U.S. This tool allows prospective students, guidance counselors, administrators, and faculty to easily access AI program requirements and is designed to continually update as new programs emerge. To the best of our knowledge, this survey represents the most comprehensive snapshot of the state of AI programs in the U.S. to date. With this work we offer three important contributions: 1) a record of AI programs in the U.S. at a time of great upheaval; 2) a tool to explore AI programs and their requirements; and 3) an analysis of the courses required for 66 AI majors and 87 AI minors. Our analysis of majors and minors shows great variability in the size and the requirements of these degrees, but we note two takeaways. First, not all majors require a general AI course, but if they don't, they do require a Machine Learning (ML) course. Second, while more than a third of majors require an Ethics in AI course, just under a quarter of AI minors do.

</details>


### [412] [Muse Spark Safety & Preparedness Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12429)
*Cristina Menghini,Peter Ney,Hamza Kwisaba,Zifan,Wang,Miles Turpin,Felix Binder,Jean-Christophe Testud,Aidan Boyd,Nathaniel Li,Ivan Evtimov,Klaudia Krawiecka,Arman Zharmagambetov,Jeremy Kritz,Alexander R. Fabbri,Daniel Song,Jinpeng Miao,Joonas Hjelt,Meghna Ramani,Leona Lan,Reza Aghajani,Joanna Bitton,Mahesh Pasupuleti,Devin Norder,Khalid El-Arini,Paridhi Singh,Vítor Albiero,Sahana CB,Rashnil Chaturvedi,Elahe Dabir,Edoardo Debenedetti,Jim Gust,Ziwen Han,Kat He,Sean Hendryx,Lifeng Jin,Polina Kirichenko,Sandra Lefdal,Kenneth Li,Asad Liaqat,Inna Lin,Despoina Magka,Neal Mangaokar,Ishita Mediratta,Zach Miller,Smitha Milli,Niloofar Mireshghallah,Saba Nazir,Hung Nguyen,Maximilian Nickel,Kelvin Niu,Kerem Oktar,Bhargavi Paranjape,Parth Pathak,Maya Pavlova,Emmanuel Ramirez,David Renardy,Candace Ross,Yasha Sheynin,Claudia Shi,Shivam Singhal,Evangelia Spiliopoulou,Rakshith Sharma Srinivasa,Jamelle Watson-Daniels,Spencer Whitman,Adina Williams,Chen Xing,Andy Zou,Tommy Ma,Siqi Deng,James Beldock,Prashant Ratanchandani,Kate Plawiak,Taesung Lee,Ryan Victory,Lindsay Hundley,Rachad Alao,Himaghna Bhattacharjee,Jianfeng Chi,Gary Frost,Pegah Ghahremani,Niki Howe,Yuheng Huang,Saeed Jahed,Hannah Korevaar,Trang Le,Zhe Liu,Jinghong Luo,Qin Lyu,Nina Mehrabi,Abraham Montilla,Chirag Nagpal,Cyrus Nikolaidis,Rajvardhan Oak,Manoj Ravi,Vidya Sarma,Aman Shankar,Alana Shine,Eric Michael Smith,Mariana Tandon,Michael Tontchev,Caoyu Wang,Zihan Wang,Corinne Wong,Zheng Wu,Hongyuan Zhan,Justin Zhao,Zexuan Zhong,Chengxu Zhuang,Tristan Goodman,Ayaz Minhas,Harrison Rudolph,Victoria Jeffries,Ingrid Dickinson,Alex Vaughan,Lauren Deason,Kamalika Chaudhuri,Julian Michael,Shengjia Zhao,Summer Yue*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Meta发布了最新大语言模型Muse Spark，基于其高级AI扩展框架对化学/生物、网络安全和失控风险进行了评估，认为在Meta AI中部署的可接受残余风险水平。评估发现化学和生物能力在应用安全措施前可能达到"高风险"级别，但通过多层缓解措施后，Muse Spark在危险工作流相关基准上展现了最先进的拒答能力，因此已作为Meta AI的基础模型发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在发布Muse Spark大型语言模型前，需要对其在灾难性风险领域（化学与生物、网络安全、失控风险）进行系统评估，确保部署安全性符合Meta高级AI扩展框架的要求。

Method: 采用Meta的高级AI扩展框架，对化学与生物、网络安全、失控风险三大灾难性风险领域进行系统性评估；针对双重用途和高风险能力进行广泛评估；实施多层缓解措施。

Result: 评估发现化学和生物能力在缓解措施前可能达到"高风险"级别；应用多层安全措施后，Muse Spark在化学和生物学危险工作流相关基准上展现了最先进的拒答能力；整体残余风险评估为可接受水平。

Conclusion: Muse Spark在Meta AI中的部署呈现可接受的残余风险水平，已作为Meta AI的基础模型发布。

Abstract: Muse Spark is the latest large language model developed by Meta. In this report, we first present evaluations for catastrophic risk domains under Meta's Advanced AI Scaling Framework, along with the evidence that informed our launch decision. We then discuss additional considerations, such as Muse Spark's broader content safety and behavioral profile, that are relevant to overall safety but fall outside the catastrophic risk domains governed by the Framework. Our preparedness results covering Chemical and Biological, Cybersecurity, and Loss of Control risks assess Muse Spark's deployment within Meta AI as presenting acceptable levels of residual risks under our Advanced AI Scaling Framework. We conducted a broad set of evaluations targeting dual-use and high-risk capabilities across these catastrophic risk domains. Those evaluations identified elevated risks prior to mitigations, with Chemical and Biological capabilities assessed as likely reaching the "high risk" category under the Advanced AI Scaling Framework before safeguards were applied. We have implemented a multi-layered set of mitigations that address the identified risks, and Muse Spark demonstrates state-of-the-art refusal across a range of benchmarks related to hazardous workflows in chemistry and biology. We therefore release Muse Spark as the underlying model of Meta AI.

</details>


### [413] [Will AI Agents Free Us From Meaningless Work? A Human-Centered Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12430)
*Davide Ghia,Jaspreet Ranjit,Tania Cerquitelli,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究基于Graeber的“狗屁工作”理论，通过对202名员工对171项工作任务的评分，验证了任务层面“狗屁感”的五项量表，发现员工认为越“狗屁”的任务越希望交给AI处理，且这些任务被认为需要较少的人类监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多从职业层面分析AI自动化，忽视了同一职业内部不同任务对员工意义感的差异。本文旨在填补这一空白，从任务层面探究员工如何识别应被自动化的任务。

Method: 收集202名员工对171项工作任务的评分，采用Graeber的“狗屁工作”理论，开发并验证了五项感知“狗屁感”量表，分析其对AI委托意愿的影响。

Result: （1）验证了任务层面感知“狗屁感”的五项量表；（2）感知“狗屁感”强烈预测员工希望将任务委托给AI的意愿；（3）这些任务也被认为需要较少的人类监督。

Conclusion: 任务感知的“狗屁感”是AI委托的自然候选，员工偏好与感知可行性一致，为AI自动化的人机协作提供了新视角。

Abstract: Some claim that AI agents will free workers from the boring parts of their jobs, yet little is known about how workers themselves identify which tasks should be automated. Prior research focuses on occupations, overlooking that workers experience varying levels of meaning across tasks within the same role. We address this gap with a task-level analysis grounded in Graeber's theory of bullshit jobs. Using ratings from 202 workers on 171 workplace tasks, we (1) validate a five-item scale of perceived bullshitness, (2) show that perceived bullshitness strongly predicts desire for AI delegation, and (3) find that such tasks are also seen as requiring less human oversight. Together, these findings suggest that tasks perceived as bullshit are natural candidates for AI delegation, aligning worker preferences with perceived feasibility.

</details>


### [414] [AI Debris: Residual Risk and the Afterlife of Failed AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12432)
*Victor Frimpong*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了"AI残留物"（AI debris）概念，指AI系统退役后仍存在的残余风险，并开发了AI残留物退役协议（AIDP）作为评估工具，用于管理AI系统停用后的持续性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI治理框架主要关注开发和部署阶段的风险，忽视了AI系统退役后残留的持续影响。这种治理空白导致机构行为、问责制和信任受到长期侵蚀，需要系统化的理论框架和评估工具来弥补。

Method: 通过理论分析构建残留物领域类型学（包括工作流依赖、数据污染、能力位移/技能退化、合法性侵蚀、问责破裂），识别残留持续机制（制度记忆、路径依赖、回避责任、组织数据中的反馈效应），并提出AI残留物退役协议（AIDP）——一种可审计的分步检查清单，涵盖决策足迹冻结、事件审查、补救、可争议性和退役后问责分配。

Result: 建立了AI残留物的操作性定义和类型学框架，开发了可评估的AIDP工具，并以亚马逊废弃招聘工具的案例说明算法决策类别和筛选启发式在系统回滚后仍可持续存在。

Conclusion: 该研究为监管者、审计师和组织提供了实用的治理工具，有助于防止纸质合规、加强AI全生命周期治理、提升高风险决策环境中的制度韧性。

Abstract: AI governance frameworks primarily focus on risks during the development and deployment phases, implicitly treating system withdrawal as a technical shutdown. This paper argues that decommissioned AI systems generate residual risk, termed AI debris, that persists after model removal and continues to shape institutional behaviour, accountability, and trust. AI debris is defined as the post-withdrawal socio-technical residue of AI systems, including workflow dependency, data contamination, capability displacement (deskilling), legitimacy erosion, and accountability breakdown. The paper develops a typology of debris domains and identifies mechanisms through which debris persists, including institutional memory, path dependency, blame avoidance, and feedback effects in organisational data. To operationalise the concept, the paper proposes an evaluator-ready AI Debris Decommissioning Protocol (AIDP), a stepwise checklist specifying auditable evidence for freezing decision footprints, incident review, remediation, contestability, and post-withdrawal accountability assignment. A brief vignette of Amazon's discontinued hiring tool illustrates how algorithmic decision categories and screening heuristics can persist after system rollback. The paper contributes a practical governance instrument for regulators, auditors, and organisations seeking to prevent paper compliance, strengthen AI lifecycle governance, and improve institutional resilience in high-stakes decision environments.

</details>


### [415] [Marginal Alignment Does Not Guarantee Joint-Distribution Fidelity: An Official-Reference Audit of Nemotron-Personas-Korea with Cross-Locale Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12433)
*Joonhyung Bae*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 针对合成人物数据集在边际分布上与官方数据对齐但联合分布不保真的问题，提出独立性假设足迹（IAF）审计原语，通过对NVIDIA Nemotron-Personas-Korea（NPK）数据集的实证分析，发现三个联合分布失败案例（职业-专业不匹配、兵役年龄不一致、女性在男性主导职业中过度均衡化），并验证了该审计方法在不同地区的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成人物数据集声称与官方人口统计边际分布对齐以建立信任，但下游用户实际消费的是年龄、性别、地区、职业、教育、姓名和机构地位等属性的联合结构。边际对齐并不保证联合分布被保留，现有方法缺乏对联合分布保真度的系统性审计手段。

Method: 提出独立性假设足迹（IAF）审计原语，针对数据集中声明为独立处理的属性组合，将合成联合分布与外部官方或机构参考进行比较。使用直接联合表（可用时）和规则隐含检查两种方式，对NPK数据集（100万韩国合成人物）进行审计。

Result: NPK与KOSIS边际分布对齐良好，但三个联合分布失败：职业与专业分布（与KEIS毕业生数据对比存在较大条件不匹配）、兵役服务年龄分布（与机构规则不一致）、女性在男性主导职业中的表示（被过度拉平趋向均衡）。跨区域迁移性实验表明诊断结果具有区域依赖性而非普遍性。

Conclusion: 合成人物数据集作为"硅样本"使用时，边际对齐声明必须辅以基于披露锚点的联合分布审计才能安全复用。研究发布了完整的审计制品（参考清单、职业交叉映射、派生指标、可复现脚本），可供其他合成人物数据资源重新定向使用。

Abstract: Synthetic persona datasets cite alignment with official demographics as a basis for trust, yet downstream users consume them as joint structures across age, sex, region, occupation, education, name, and institutional status. Marginal alignment does not imply that these joints are preserved. We propose the Independence-Assumption Footprint (IAF), an audit primitive that operates on the attribute combinations a dataset card itself documents as treated independently. For each such combination, IAF compares the synthetic joint against an external official or institutional reference, using direct joint tables where available and rule-implied checks otherwise. Applied to NVIDIA Nemotron-Personas-Korea (one million Korean synthetic personas), IAF finds that NPK aligns with KOSIS marginals while three joints fail. The major-by-occupation distribution against the KEIS graduate universe carries a large conditional mismatch. The age profile of military service is institutionally inconsistent. Female representation in male-dominated occupations is substantially over-flattened toward parity, with the strict screening verdict mapping-dependent and age-robust under direct standardisation. A transferability demonstration across six further NPK locales finds locale-dependent rather than universal diagnostics, with reference-taxonomy cardinality confounding cross-locale flag counts. For synthetic personas used as silicon samples, marginal claims must therefore be paired with disclosure-anchored joint audits before reuse. The released audit artefacts (reference manifests, occupational crosswalks, derived metrics, reproducibility scripts) instantiate this protocol on the NPK family and are released for retargeting at other synthetic persona resources.

</details>


### [416] [Pluralistic-Alignment Urbanism: Operationalizing a Right to AI for Inclusive Public Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12434)
*Rashid Mushkani*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出多元对齐城市主义（PAU），一个将公共空间AI系统视为市政基础设施的程序性治理框架，并通过蒙特利尔的两个参与式案例研究，展示了如何将分歧、子群体差异、有界预测扩展和中性偏好判断纳入市政AI治理，最终转化为包含分类报告、版本化价值登记、常设审议小组、采购条款及暂停/回滚机制在内的治理架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 市政机构日益使用机器学习来盘点人行道、评分街景和可视化公共空间干预，但这些系统的输出进入预算、设计迭代和公共论证时，关于包容性、安全性和归属感的判断仍存在争议，亟需一个程序性治理框架来确保公众的AI权利。

Method: 通过两个参与式案例研究：1）Street Review收集居民对街道评估的标准，训练子群体感知的评分模型（R²=0.89）；2）LIVS构建多元偏好数据集用于对齐文本到图像模型，将中性选择视为不确定性证据。从案例中提炼分歧结构、证据变化、扩展边界和中性约束等原则，并设计PAU治理架构。

Result: Street Review实现了R²=0.89的预测表现；LIVS构建了多元偏好数据；研究发现分歧是有结构的、商议改变证据的性质、扩展可行但受限于模态和覆盖率、中性选择约束偏好调优的合理性。PAU架构包含分类报告、版本化价值登记、常设审议小组、采购条款和暂停/回滚权限。

Conclusion: PAU将实证得出的约束转化为具体的市政治理架构，为公共空间AI系统提供了程序性的公众权利保障机制，确保此类系统作为市政基础设施时能够回应多元公众的诉求。

Abstract: Municipal agencies increasingly use machine learning to inventory sidewalks, score streetscapes, and generate visualizations of public-space interventions. These systems produce outputs that enter budgeting, design iteration, and public justification, yet judgments about inclusion, safety, and belonging remain contested. This paper proposes Pluralistic-Alignment Urbanism (PAU), a procedural governance framework that treats public-space AI systems as civic infrastructure and formulates a procedural Right to AI for municipal uses of such systems. Drawing on two participatory case studies with community organizations in Montreal, Canada, the paper examines how disagreement, subgroup variation, bounded predictive scaling, and neutral preference judgments can inform municipal AI governance. Street Review elicits resident criteria for streetscape evaluation and trains a subgroup-aware scaling model for co-produced judgments, achieving an R2 of 0.89 on a held-out test set. LIVS, a Local Intersectional Visual Spaces dataset, constructs pluralistic preference data for aligning text-to-image models and treats neutral selections as evidence of indeterminacy. Across the cases, disagreement appears structured, deliberation changes what counts as evidence, scaling is feasible but limited by modality and coverage, and neutrality constrains what preference tuning can justify. PAU translates these constraints into a municipal governance architecture with disaggregated reporting, a versioned value register, standing deliberative cells, procurement clauses, and defined pause and rollback authority.

</details>


### [417] [Auditing Discriminatory Patterns in Mortgage Lending Through Association Rules and Fair Binning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12435)
*Archit Rathod,Dhwani Chande,Het Nagda*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究了标准数据预处理（尤其是分箱）如何加剧抵押贷款审批中的种族和性别歧视。使用HMDA 2023数据集（芝加哥都会区103,481条记录），构建了三阶段流水线：PySpark数据清洗与分箱（等频分箱 vs. epsilon-偏置公平分箱）、FP-Growth关联规则挖掘、K-Means聚类与差异影响审计。结果表明：标准分箱在收入离散化中存在9.63%的种族偏置；公平分箱在epsilon=0.03时不可行，epsilon=0.08时公平代价为29.4%；FP-Growth显示高负债收入比是主要拒绝预测因子（置信度67.2%，提升度2.81），种族偏置未以显式高支持度规则出现；但聚类后的差异影响审计发现10个聚类-群体对存在显著差异，表明即使在财务相似群体中，黑人申请人仍面临显著更高的拒绝率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究标准数据预处理步骤（属性分箱）是否会放大下游模式挖掘中的种族和性别差异，揭示数据预处理本身可能引入或加剧偏见。

Method: 构建三阶段流水线：(1) PySpark数据清洗与分箱，对比等频分箱和epsilon-偏置公平分箱；(2) FP-Growth关联规则挖掘，比较两种分箱下的拒绝模式；(3) K-Means聚类结合差异影响审计，检测聚类内不同群体的拒绝率差异。

Result: 标准分箱导致9.63%的种族偏置；公平分箱在epsilon=0.03时不可行，epsilon=0.08时公平代价29.4%；高负债收入比是主要拒绝预测因子（置信度67.2%，提升度2.81）；聚类审计发现10/45个聚类-群体对存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 数据预处理中的分箱操作会放大抵押贷款审批中的种族差异，即使使用公平分箱算法也会带来显著的公平代价；关联规则挖掘中种族偏置不表现为显式规则，但聚类审计揭示了隐性的结构性歧视。

Abstract: Mortgage lending in the United States exhibits persistent racial and gender disparities. We investigate whether standard data preprocessing steps, specifically attribute binning, amplify these disparities in downstream pattern mining. Using 103,481 cleaned mortgage applications from the HMDA 2023 dataset (Chicago metropolitan area), we build a three-stage pipeline: (1) a PySpark data cleaning and binning pipeline that implements both standard equal-frequency binning and the epsilon-biased fair binning algorithm from Asudeh et al. [1], (2) FP-Growth association rule mining that compares denial patterns under both binning regimes, and (3) K-Means clustering with a per-cluster disparate impact audit. Our standard binning shows 9.63% racial bias in income discretization, consistent with the 8-10% reported in prior work. Fair binning with seven race groups is infeasible at epsilon=0.03 and only succeeds at epsilon=0.08 with a Price of Fairness of 29.4%. FP-Growth reveals that high debt-to-income ratio is the dominant denial predictor (67.2% confidence, 2.81 lift), while racial bias does not appear as explicit high-support rules. However, K-Means clustering followed by a disparate impact audit flags 10 out of 45 cluster-group pairs, showing that Black applicants face significantly higher denial rates than White applicants even among financially similar groups.

</details>


### [418] [Knowing the Rules Is Not Enough: Student Regulatory Awareness and Use of GenAI in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12436)
*Lasse Bischof,Eva-Maria Schön,Maria Rauschenberger,Michael Neumann*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 一项针对151名计算机相关专业本科生的调查发现，大多数学生积极使用GenAI工具，但超过半数不确定自己的使用是否符合学校规定；学生对规定的认知与实际使用行为之间仅存在弱到中等程度的关联，且学生主要使用私人获取的GenAI工具而非学校提供的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究主要关注GenAI工具的采用率和态度，但学生对机构法规的认知及其感知合规性尚未被探索。理解法规认知是否影响学生行为对于高校制定和实施AI政策至关重要。

Method: 对汉诺威应用科学与艺术大学151名商业信息系统和电子政务专业的本科生进行问卷调查，收集关于GenAI使用情况、使用工具、对机构法规的认知以及感知合规性的数据，采用描述性统计、交叉表和相关性分析。

Result: 大多数学生积极使用GenAI工具，但超过半数不确定自己的使用是否符合机构规定。法规认知与实际使用行为之间仅存在弱到中等程度的关联。学生主要依赖私人获取的GenAI工具，而非机构提供的解决方案。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了机构法规与学生实践之间的差距，实证证明了法规认知与GenAI使用之间的关系，为教育工作者和机构改进政策沟通以及将GenAI更有效地整合到教学和学习环境中提供了见解。

Abstract: Context: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) tools such as ChatGPT are increasingly integrated into students learning practices. While previous research mainly examines adoption rates and attitudes, students awareness of institutional regulations and their perceived compliance remain unexplored. Understanding whether regulatory awareness influences student behavior is therefore important as higher education institutions create and apply AI policies. Objective: This study investigates how students awareness of GenAI regulations relates to their perceived compliance and actual usage behavior. Our research objective is to examine the association between regulatory knowledge, GenAI use, and perceived rule conformity among students in computer science related study programs. Method: A survey with 151 undergraduate students in Business Information Systems and E-Government programs at the University of Applied Sciences and Arts Hannover (Germany) collected data on GenAI usage, tools used, awareness of institutional regulations, and perceived compliance. Descriptive statistics, cross-tabulations, and correlation analyzes were applied. Results: Most students actively use GenAI tools, but over half are uncertain whether their usage complies with institutional regulations. Regulatory awareness shows only weak to moderate associations with actual usage behavior. Students primarily rely on privately accessed GenAI tools rather than institutionally provided solutions. Contributions: The study contributes empirical evidence on the relationship between regulatory awareness and GenAI usage in higher education. Our findings highlight a gap between institutional regulations and student practices and provide insights for educators and institutions on improving policy communication and integrating GenAI more effectively into teaching and learning contexts.

</details>


### [419] [Algorithmic Constitutionalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12437)
*Oren Perez,Nurit Wimer*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文批判了"伦理工程"作为AI治理方案的不足，提出了"算法宪政主义"框架，包含三层架构（操作层与元代码层）、算法元推理和通过 deliberation 的纠偏机制，并以Facebook内容审核制度为例进行分析，最后讨论了该框架对欧盟《数字服务法》的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能对社会的侵蚀日益严重，尤其是在Google、Facebook等公司控制的信息圈中。现有文献提出的"伦理工程"解决方案不足以应对AI治理挑战，亟需一个更具规范性和约束力的替代框架。

Method: 提出"算法宪政主义"框架，包含三个支柱：(1) 分层架构——操作层（对象级代码）与元代码层（保护核心原则）；(2) 算法元推理——系统同时在两层运行，实时监控、验证和纠正偏离元代码层原则的操作；(3) 通过 deliberation 进行纠偏。并以Facebook内容审核制度作为案例进行应用分析。

Result: 分析了算法宪政主义与社群宪政主义之间的张力：试图将AI系统置于外部 deliberative 控制之下，反而可能使AI代理人干预该过程，从而破坏其目的。

Conclusion: 算法宪政主义为AI治理提供了比伦理工程更坚实的框架，但其与外部 deliberative 控制之间存在悖论。该论证对2022年10月生效的欧盟《数字服务法》具有重要的政策启示。

Abstract: The increasing encroachment of artificial intelligence (AI) on social life raises significant risks for society, particularly within the infospheres created and controlled by companies such as Google, Facebook, Apple, and Amazon. This article examines these risks through an in-depth analysis of Facebook's content moderation regime, which is already partially governed by algorithms. We argue that the idea of ethical engineering, often proposed in the literature as a solution to the governance challenges posed by AI, is inadequate for several reasons. In response, we develop an alternative framework, which we term "algorithmic constitutionalism."
  Our approach rests on three pillars: (a) a layered architecture consisting of two levels of code: (i) an operative or object level and (ii) a meta level designed to protect the system's core principles from algorithmically initiated change; (b) algorithmic meta-reasoning, which enables the system to operate simultaneously at both levels so that it can monitor, verify, and potentially correct in real time operations at the object level that depart from principles protected at the meta-code level; and (c) correction through deliberation.
  The article elaborates the concept of algorithmic constitutionalism and demonstrates how it may be applied to Facebook's content moderation regime. As part of this analysis, we examine the tension between societal constitutionalism and algorithmic constitutionalism. Paradoxically, attempts to subject AI systems to external deliberative control may also enable AI agents to intervene in that process, potentially undermining its purpose. The article concludes by considering the implications of this argument for the European Digital Services Act, which entered into force in October 2022.

</details>


### [420] [From Real-World Projects to Research-Oriented Learning: Continuous Improvement of a Master-Level Course in Software Engineering Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12438)
*Michael Neumann,Eva-Maria Schön*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过2019-2025年纵向混合方法研究，分析了德国汉诺威应用科学与艺术大学信息系统硕士课程如何从实践导向的项目形式逐步演变为研究导向的学习环境，并发现尽管课程进行了深度转型，学生的课程质量感知仍保持积极。研究识别了真实项目、外部合作、讲师支持、结构化支架和可见相关性等关键设计要素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管项目式学习日益受到关注，但关于硕士层次课程如何历经多年持续向研究导向方法演进、以及学生如何感知这一发展过程，目前所知甚少。

Method: 采用纵向混合方法研究，覆盖2019年至2025年共六年数据，包括教学评价、课程文档和反思性教学记录。

Result: 课程从实践导向的项目形式成功演变为更明确的研究导向学习环境，且学生的课程质量感知保持积极。真实项目、外部合作、讲师支持、结构化支架和可见相关性是维持积极感知的关键因素。

Conclusion: 论文展示了硕士课程如何在保持学生积极感知的同时持续向研究导向学习演进，并识别了支持这一转型的课程设计决策。

Abstract: Problem: Despite growing interest in project-based learning, little is known about how a master-level course can be continuously evolved toward research-oriented approaches over several years and how students perceive this development. Method: We conducted a longitudinal mixed-methods study of a master-level course in Information Systems at the University of Applied Sciences and Arts Hannover (Germany). The analysis covers six years between 2019 and 2025 and draws on teaching evaluations, course documentation, and reflective teaching artifacts. Results: The course evolved from a practice-oriented project format toward a more explicitly research-oriented learning environment. Despite this change, students' perceived course quality remained positive. Authentic projects, external collaboration, lecturer support, structured scaffolding, and visible relevance supported positive student perceptions. Contribution: This paper shows how a master-level course can be continuously evolved toward research-oriented learning while maintaining positive student perceptions. It further identifies the course design decisions that supported this transition.

</details>


### [421] [Position: Generative Engine Optimization Creates Underexamined Risks, Governance Must Target Concentration, Disclosure, and Academic Blind Spots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12439)
*Yizhu Wen,Nan Zhang,Haohan Yuan,Xun Chen,Haopeng Zhang,Hanqing Guo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文分析了从SEO到GEO的转变，识别出三大风险：低可争议性导致的集中影响力、隐藏的商业影响、以及学术与工业界之间的盲点，并呼吁对LLM生成的答案进行治理与测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型答案引擎正逐渐取代传统搜索的排序列表，使得信息可见性从排序列表转向合成答案，这催生了生成式引擎优化（GEO）技术。作者旨在识别这一转变中隐含的风险，并呼吁建立答案级别的治理机制。

Method: 通过分析SEO到GEO的转变过程识别两种风险（低可争议性与系统敏感性导致的集中影响力、隐藏的商业影响），形式化通用的GEO流程定位优化作用点，并对比学术与工业实践，揭示第三种风险（学术-工业盲点）。

Result: 识别出三大风险：(i) 低可争议性和系统敏感性导致的集中影响力；(ii) 嵌入在证据和推理中的未公开商业影响；(iii) 学术与工业界之间因可见性和评估不对称导致的盲点。

Conclusion: 需要答案级别的治理与测量：增强可争议性、高精度披露、黑盒审计实质性影响，以及面向部署的一致性指标来衡量暴露持久性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) answer engines are increasingly used for information seeking, shifting visibility from ranked lists to synthesized answers. This enables Generative Engine Optimization (GEO), which targets LLM answer engines' evidence pool and generation. We analyze the search engine optimization (SEO) to GEO transition to identify two risks: (i) concentrated influence from low contestability and system sensitivity, and (ii) undisclosed commercial influence embedded in evidence and reasoning. We then formalize a general GEO pipeline to locate where optimization acts and compare academic and industry practices, revealing a third risk: (iii) academic-industry blind spots driven by visibility and evaluation asymmetries between offline setups and deployed systems. This position argues the need for answer-level governance and measurement: stronger contestability, high-precision disclosure, black-box auditing of material influence, and deployment-aligned metrics for exposure persistence.

</details>


### [422] [It's Safer to Give Personhood to Bears than to Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12440)
*John P. Nelson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨AI权利的制度维度：若赋予AI道德或法律权利，将使其能够合法且不可阻止地限制人类利益，这是一场关乎人类自决权的世界级赌博，任何个人或组织都无权授权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量研究讨论AI是否应享有权利，但鲜有研究探讨AI权利在实践中意味着什么，尤其是其制度维度。

Method: 作者通过分析AI与其他非人类实体（如动物、公司）在权利主体资格上的本质差异——AI能够不依赖人类中介获取和行使制度权力——来进行论证。

Result: 赋予AI权利将使其能够合法地损害人类利益；拥有权力的AI则能不可阻止地这样做。即使是相当笨拙的AI系统，获得权利后也会将人类命运绑定在不可预测的非人类实体上。

Conclusion: 赋权AI主张或行使固有权利是一场世界历史性的赌博，威胁人类自决权，没有任何个人、公司、国家或国际组织具有道德权利来授权这一行为。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) developers are rhetorically flirting with the idea that AI systems might have interests or moral rights. While there has been a large volume of research on whether AI deserves rights, there has been less exploration of what AI rights would mean in practice. This paper explores the institutional dimension of AI rights: what it would take to recognize moral or legal rights for AIs, and the attendant opportunities and dangers. Unlike all other nonhuman entities to which humanity has extended rights, AI systems are in principle capable of acquiring and wielding institutional power without human aid and mediation. AIs with rights would be able to legitimately, and AIs with power able to unpreventably, abridge human interests. Accordingly, giving rights even to rather dumb AI systems would entail binding the fate of humanity to potentially unpredictable nonhumans. Accordingly, I defend the rather grandiose claim that to empower AI to claim or to exercise inherent rights would be a world-historical gamble with human self-determination, which no individual researcher, firm, state, or even international organization has the moral right to authorize.

</details>


### [423] [Generativism: Toward a Learning Theory for the Age of Generative Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12441)
*Shan Li,Juan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文批判性审视行为主义、认知主义、建构主义和联通主义四种主流学习理论在生成式AI时代的局限性，提出“生成主义”（Generativism）作为新的学习理论框架，强调人类与AI系统之间的迭代知识共建过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四大学习理论均形成于生成式AI出现之前，无法解释和指导AI深度介入教育场景下的学习机制，亟需一种适应人工智能时代的新理论框架。

Method: 通过批判性分析四种主流学习理论在面对生成式AI时的概念局限，结合分布式认知、延展心智、人机协作、AI素养、认知卸载和元认知等研究成果，提出包含四个核心原则的理论框架。

Result: 提出了“生成主义”理论，包含四个核心原则：认知伙伴关系（Epistemic Partnership）、分布式能动性（Distributed Agency）、生成素养（Generative Literacy）和适应性元认知（Adaptive Metacognition）。

Conclusion: 该框架为重新思考生成式AI深度参与认知过程下的教学设计、学习评估和专长发展提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The four dominant learning theories of behaviorism, cognitivism, constructivism, and connectivism show significant conceptual limitations as generative artificial intelligence (AI) proliferates in educational settings. These frameworks were formulated before the emergence of AI systems capable of generating, synthesizing, and reasoning about knowledge. This article critically examines each learning theory and identifies assumptions challenged by generative AI's affordances. Drawing on research in distributed cognition, extended mind, human-AI collaboration, AI literacy, cognitive offloading, and metacognition, the article proposes Generativism as a learning theory for the generative AI age. Generativism posits that learning increasingly occurs through the iterative co-construction of knowledge between human learners and AI systems. The proposed framework is organized around four principles: epistemic partnership, distributed agency, generative literacy, and adaptive metacognition. The framework offers a foundation for rethinking instructional design, learning, assessment, and expertise development in contexts where generative AI plays an integral role in cognition.

</details>


### [424] [Reframing AI Loss of Control: What It Is, How to Have It, How to Lose It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12442)
*Ze Shen Chin,Maurice Chiodo,Dennis Müller,Coleman Snell*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文针对AI领域中“失控”概念缺乏明确定义的问题，提出了基于“目标设定与实现”的控制定义框架，并从控制论、管理控制和控制理论等基础领域构建了控制的多个维度，进而分析失控机制及AI如何导致失控，给出维持控制的建议。研究发现，失控风险并非仅与超级智能相关，在远低于超级智能水平的AI行为中就已存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中“失控”概念虽被广泛讨论，但缺乏扎实的理论基础，甚至连基本定义都未明确——没有先定义什么是控制、失去的是什么。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 首先将控制锚定于“目标设定与实现”建立工作定义；然后借鉴控制论、管理控制和控制理论等基础领域，探讨控制的多维度要素（谁/什么可以控制、设置目标的能力、功能性控制回路、必要的多样性、目标对齐等）；再基于该框架分析失控机制、AI对失控的贡献，并提出维持控制的建议。

Result: 一个有趣的发现是，人类作为个体和群体，可能因远低于超级智能水平的AI行为而失去不同程度的控制，这种失控场景（按照本文定义）早已存在且持续至今。

Conclusion: 失控风险并非仅与超级智能相关，在现有AI系统中就已存在；需要建立更清晰的控制定义框架来指导AI安全研究和治理。

Abstract: At present, loss of control risks have gained much prominence in public discussion, particularly in relation to AI, with extensive discourse present among academics, frontier labs, and even governments. However, in the existing literature, the concept seems to rest on surprisingly weak foundations, where even those that discuss loss of control extensively do not first establish what control is and what exactly is being lost. Our paper aims to address these gaps. We establish a working definition of control by anchoring it to the "setting and getting of goals". Then, we discuss various aspects of control, built on foundational concepts from related fields like cybernetics, management control, and control theory. This includes who (or what) can be in control, and the things they require to be in control, such as the ability to set goals, having a functional control loop, having requisite variety, and having sufficient goal alignment. Once a framework for control is established, we then discuss how control can be lost, how AIs can contribute to such loss of control, and offer relevant recommendations for how one can maintain control. One interesting consequence of our work is that humanity, as individuals and as groups, can lose varying degrees of control as a result of AI behaviour that is far below the level of superintelligence; the potential for loss of control scenarios (as we define them) already exist, and have existed for a long time.

</details>


### [425] [Occupational Prompting Reveals Cultural Bias in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12443)
*Maksim E. Eren,Andrea Brennen,Ryan C. Barron,Eric Michalak*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过将文化提示替换为职业提示，研究开放权重LLMs在价值调查中如何受职业身份影响。结果表明，职业提示并未使模型脱离西方文化区域，但不同职业在该区域内产生了差异化的价值观偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究主要关注国籍文化提示对LLM价值响应的影响，但职业身份作为一种重要的社会角色，其对模型价值观表达的影响尚不清楚。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索职业提示如何塑造LLM的价值模式。

Method: 基于综合价值观调查（Integrated Values Surveys）的问题构建评估流水线，将模型响应投影到Inglehart–Welzel二维文化空间。通过向开放权重LLMs提供职业身份（如会计师、教师、工程师、护士）提示，分析其条件化响应在文化地图上的位置。

Result: 开放权重LLMs在职业提示下仍处于西方文化区域，但不同职业在该区域内产生差异化偏移，形成了独特的职业偏向。职业提示并非中性角色标签，而是引发了结构化的价值模式。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了基于调查的文化偏差评估框架，超越了国籍提示的局限，提供了一个研究职业角色如何塑造LLM价值表达的系统方法。

Abstract: Social roles shape expectations, priorities, and judgments, yet it remains unclear how large language models (LLMs) associate occupational identities with broader cultural value patterns. Prior work used nationality-based cultural prompting to study how LLM responses to value-survey questions align with human cultural benchmarks. In this paper, we extend that framework by replacing cultural prompting with occupational prompting to examine how professional-role cues influence value-survey responses in open-weight LLMs. Using a survey-grounded evaluation pipeline based on questions from the Integrated Values Surveys, we project model responses into the two-dimensional Inglehart--Welzel cultural space. We prompt open-weight LLMs to answer questions under occupational identities such as accountant, teacher, engineer, and nurse, and then analyze how these occupation-conditioned responses are positioned on the cultural map. Our results show that when open-weight LLMs are prompted with occupations rather than national identities, their responses remain within a broadly Western-leaning region of the cultural map. However, different occupations introduce shifts within this region, producing distinct occupational skews. This indicates that occupational prompts are not treated as neutral role labels, but instead elicit structured value patterns. These findings extend survey-based evaluation of cultural bias beyond nationality-based prompting and provide a framework for studying how occupational personas shape value expression in LLMs.

</details>


### [426] [Beyond Resilience -- A Conceptual Framework for Civic Ascent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12752)
*Alexandros Washburn,Carlo Lipizzi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"公民升越"（civic ascent）概念，超越传统韧性文献仅关注恢复至基线水平的局限，将城市定义为由三个慢变量（topos地理结构、nomos制度结构、hexis公民判断）与一个快情感通道（delta）耦合的agent-environment系统，并建立了区分冲击、衰变和泄漏三种压力的分析框架，以纽约911事件为实证案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统韧性文献将城市绩效定义为恢复到冲击前基线水平的"恢复"能力，这一概念过于薄弱。本文旨在发展一个更强的概念——"公民升越"（civic ascent），即城市在经历冲击后以更高功能容量出现，并为此建立理论框架和测量方法。

Method: 采用行为学（ethology）传统，将城市视为耦合的agent-environment系统，建立三个慢状态变量——topos（物理结构）、nomos（制度结构）、hexis（公民判断）——与快情感通道delta的理论框架。区分三种结构性压力：冲击（T或M的不连续变化）、衰变（连续熵增）、泄漏（公民剩余向非公民池的主动抽取）。升越条件为T、M、H的交叉耦合强化超过衰变与泄漏的损失之和。提出归一化改进指数A(T)用于测量复合公民绩效信号P(t)。

Result: 提出公民升越的操作化测量方案，定义归一化改进指数A(T)，区分内在公民升越与人口驱动的增长。以纽约911事件后为实证案例，配套数据方案为NYC Civic Data Map（133个KPI），测量执行见Paper 2。

Conclusion: 公民升越概念为城市韧性研究提供了超越恢复性思维的分析工具。该框架既是城市研究的独立贡献，也是更广泛的耦合agent-environment系统行为学研究项目的形式核心。

Abstract: The resilience literature measures urban performance as recovery: the degree to which a city returns to its pre-shock baseline. This paper develops a stronger concept -- civic ascent -- as part of a broader research program on the ethology of coupled agent-environment systems, of which the city is the deepest available empirical instance. Civic ascent is defined as the condition in which a city emerges from shock with higher functional capacity than before. We develop a conceptual framework in the ethological tradition, treating the city as a coupled system of three slow state variables -- topos (physical structure), nomos (institutional structure), and hexis (civic judgment) -- together with a fast affective channel (delta) through which shocks to topos and nomos reach hexis. The framework distinguishes three structurally distinct pressures on civic systems: shocks (discontinuities in T or M), decay (continuous entropy), and leakage (active extraction of civic surplus into non-civic pools). The ascent condition is that reinforcement from cross-coupling of T, M, and H exceeds the combined loss from decay and leakage. Post-shock ascent is measured by a normalised improvement index A(T) applied to a composite civic performance signal P(t) constructed from scale-adjusted key performance indicators, distinguishing intrinsic civic ascent from demographically driven growth. New York City after September 11, 2001, is proposed as the primary empirical case; the operational measurement program is specified in the companion NYC Civic Data Map (Washburn 2026c, 133 KPIs) and executed in Paper 2. The reader for whom only the urban contribution is of interest will find it complete in itself; the reader interested in the larger program will find this paper its formal core.

</details>


### [427] [AiAWE: An Open-Source LLM Automated Writing Evaluation System Using LoRA-Adapted Instruction-Tuned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12801)
*John Maurice Gayed*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究提出AiAWE，一个基于LoRA微调指令大语言模型（Gemma-3-27B-it）的自动作文评估系统，在ETS 480篇托福作文数据集上以120篇训练、360篇测试，Gemma模型达到RMSE 0.474、QWK 0.828、90.56%±0.5一致率，超越更大规模的LLaMA-3.3-70B及此前GPT-3.5基线，且支持消费级服务器部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个开源、可部署于消费级硬件的自动作文评估系统，验证开放权重LLM在LoRA微调下能否匹配或超越专有模型（如GPT-3.5）的评分表现。

Method: 使用LoRA对指令微调大语言模型（Gemma-3-27B-it和LLaMA-3.3-70B）进行参数高效微调，训练集120篇、测试集360篇，在同一LoRA配置和量化推理条件下评估，并与此前GPT-3.5基线对比。

Result: Gemma-3-27B微调模型取得RMSE 0.474、QWK 0.828、±0.5内一致率90.56%，全面超越LLaMA-3.3-70B（更大规模）和GPT-3.5基线。开放权重LLM可匹配或超越专有模型；模型规模并非LoRA微调下性能的可靠预测指标；相同LoRA超参数在不同架构上产生质的不同适应行为。

Conclusion: 开放权重LLM通过LoRA微调可在作文评分任务上达到甚至超越专有模型，且可在消费级服务器上部署运行，系统及代码均已开源。

Abstract: This study presents AiAWE, an open-source automated writing evaluation system that scores argumentative essays using a LoRA-adapted instruction-tuned large language model (Gemma-3-27B-it). Using a proprietary Educational Testing Service (ETS) dataset of 480 TOEFL Independent Writing essays, we fine-tune Gemma-3-27B and LLaMA-3.3-70B under identical LoRA configurations on a 120-essay training subset and evaluate on the remaining 360 essays under identical inference quantization. The fine-tuned Gemma model achieves a root mean square error of 0.474, a quadratic weighted kappa of 0.828, and an agreement rate of 90.56% within +/- 0.5 of the human score, outperforming both the larger LLaMA-3.3-70B model and the fine-tuned GPT-3.5 baseline reported in prior work on the same dataset. Three findings are of broader interest: open-weight LLMs can match or exceed proprietary fine-tuning for rubric-aligned scoring; model scale is not a reliable predictor of downstream performance under LoRA adaptation; and identical LoRA hyperparameters produce qualitatively different adaptation behaviors across architectures. The production system runs on a consumer-grade server and is publicly accessible at https://app.awade.gec.waseda.ac.jp. LoRA adapters, application code, and fine-tuning YAMLs are publicly available through their respective repositories.

</details>


### [428] [Vocal Identity Under Siege by AI Voice Cloning Technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12812)
*Jyh-An Lee,Xuan Sun*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了生成式AI语音克隆对声纹身份保护带来的法律与伦理挑战，通过比较分析公开权、人格权和个人数据保护权三种法律框架，评估其在应对AI语音克隆威胁方面的优势与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenAI的ChatGPT-4o语音与斯嘉丽·约翰逊声音高度相似等近期争议事件，凸显了AI驱动的语音克隆技术对人类声音独特价值的侵蚀，以及现有法律框架在保护声纹身份方面的不足。

Method: 采用比较分析方法，系统评估公开权、人格权和个人数据保护权三种主要法律框架在应对AI语音克隆问题上的适用范围、救济措施和死后保护机制。

Result: 三种法律框架各有优势与局限：公开权侧重商业利用控制，人格权强调个人尊严保护，个人数据保护权则聚焦信息自主权，但均未完全解决AI语音克隆带来的独特挑战。

Conclusion: 现有法律框架可在一定程度上适用于生成式AI时代的声纹身份保护问题，但需进一步完善以应对技术发展带来的新挑战。

Abstract: The advent of sophisticated AI-driven voice cloning has brought to the fore critical legal and ethical challenges regarding the protection of vocal identity. Prompted by recent controversies - including the striking resemblance between OpenAI's ChatGPT-4o voice and that of Scarlett Johansson - this article examines how generative AI technologies undermine the unique value of the human voice and further complicate the legal questions surrounding personality right. Through a comparative analysis, the paper evaluates three principal legal frameworks: the right of publicity, personality rights, and the personal data protection right. Each framework - rooted in different legal traditions o offers distinct strengths and limitations in addressing the threats posed by AI-generated voice cloning. By analysing these doctrines' scope, remedies, and posthumous protections, the study offers a foundation for understanding how existing legal approaches may be applied to the evolving challenges of vocal identity in the era of generative AI.

</details>


### [429] [Data Aphasia: An Institutional Counterfactual Study of the Stability of Academic Cognition Under Letter-Grade Evaluation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12946)
*Li Li,Yu Cao*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"数据失语症"概念，通过对75名小学生68次数学考试成绩的等级制转换模拟分析，发现等级制导致信息熵下降约69%、聚类结构极不稳定（去除一个极端样本后最优K从4升至8）、时间一致性波动大（52%-96%），并揭示了离散化压缩特征空间、制造伪异质性区域等机制，最后提出双轨评价机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究字母等级评价体系在实现减负目标的同时，是否影响了教育系统对学生学业结构的稳定认知，即等级制是否导致对诊断性信息表达的限制。

Method: 采用制度反事实模拟方法，将百分制分数转换为A/B/C/D字母等级，在信息层面、结构层面和诊断层面进行系统检验；具体包括信息熵计算、聚类分析（K值确定与稳定性测试）、时间一致性分析，以及特征空间压缩、密度梯度等机制分析。

Result: 1) 等级转换后信息熵下降约69%；2) 全样本下等级制表面稳定（K=4），但移除一个极端锚点学生后最优K从4升至8，个体诊断身份一致性从95%降至62%；3) 时间一致性在52%-96%间波动，远低于百分制93%-96%的基线；4) 等级离散化在68次考试中压缩特征空间约19倍，标准化后产生大量伪异质性区域，扁平化密度梯度，聚类边界对微小扰动高度敏感。

Conclusion: 等级制评价在减负的同时带来了显著的诊断信息损失和认知结构不稳定问题，论文提出双轨评价机制作为解决方案，并为理解教育评价改革的认知成本提供了可检验的分析框架。

Abstract: Does the letter-grade evaluation system, while achieving its burden-reduction goals, affect the education system's stable understanding of students' academic structures? This paper introduces the concept of "data aphasia," referring to restrictions on diagnostic information expression caused by institutionally mandated forms of data presentation. Using data from 68 mathematics examinations administered to 75 primary school students, we employ an institutional counterfactual simulation method to convert percentage scores into A/B/C/D letter grades and conduct systematic tests at the information, structural, and diagnostic levels. Results show that information entropy decreases by approximately 69% after grade conversion; under the full sample, the letter-grade system appears superficially stable (K=4), but removing a single extreme anchor student causes the optimal K to increase from 4 to 8 and individual diagnostic identity consistency to fall from 95% to 62%; temporal consistency fluctuates between 52% and 96%, far below the 93%-96% baseline of the percentage system. Mechanism analysis indicates that discretization compresses the feature space by approximately nineteenfold across 68 examinations; after standardization, it creates extensive pseudo-heterogeneity regions, flattens density gradients, and makes clustering boundaries highly sensitive to minor perturbations. Based on these findings, this paper proposes a dual-track evaluation mechanism and provides a testable analytical framework for understanding the cognitive costs of educational evaluation reform.

</details>


### [430] [Democracy in the Era of Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13026)
*Evangelos Pournaras,Srijoni Majumdar,Carina Hausladen,Dirk Helbing*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人工智能与民主的复杂关系，提出核心问题不是AI对民主是好是坏，而是如何利用AI升级民主制度、建立民主韧性。全书分五部分34章，涵盖AI赋能集体智慧、大型语言模型与 deliberative 民主、AI支持的自治理系统、民主转型挑战及未来展望。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI与民主的交互是当代最深刻的挑战之一。一方面AI可解决民主中长期存在的参与度低、代表性不足等问题；另一方面AI带来了隐私侵犯、偏见、操纵、虚假信息和影响选举等新风险。需要超越"AI对民主是好是坏"的简化和问题。

Method: 跨学科合作，汇集全球59位作者的34个章节，从五个维度系统分析：Part 1 AI赋能集体智慧；Part 2 利用大语言模型和社交媒体推动 deliberative 民主；Part 3 AI构建韧性自治理系统；Part 4 民主在AI时代的转型挑战；Part 5 重新构想民主与AI的互动关系。

Result: 提出了三个核心问题：(1) 如何利用AI升级民主制度及其原则；(2) 如何与AI互动及以何种条件进行；(3) 构建民主韧性需要哪些新的价值观和设计原则。全书从集体智慧、deliberative 民主、韧性自治理、转型挑战和未来展望五个层面提供了系统性分析。

Conclusion: 超越AI对民主二元对立的简单判断，转向如何主动设计、引导AI服务于民主制度的升级与韧性建设，重新构想民主与AI的互动关系。

Abstract: Interfacing Artificial Intelligence (AI) with democracy is one of the most profound challenges of our times. On the one hand, AI comes with opportunities to overcome long-standing challenges in democracy, such as low participation in deliberative and voting processes with poor representation of people. On the other hand, new risks arise from AI algorithms that are privacy-intrusive, biased, manipulative, spread misinformation and influence election results. Moving beyond the over-simplistic question of whether AI is good or bad for democracy, the Handbook on Democracy in the Era of Artificial Intelligence asks instead: how to upgrade democracies and the principles they are built on, using AI? How to engage with AI and on what terms? Which new values and design principles are required to build democratic resilience? In 34 chapters by 59 authors across the world from different disciplines, we explore how AI can empower collective intelligence for democracy (Part 1) and what is the future of deliberative democracy using large language models and social media (Part 2). We also illustrate the role of AI for building resilient self-governance systems (Part 3) and the challenges of transforming democracy in the age of AI (Part 4). We conclude with broader perspectives (Part 5) that re-imagine the interplay of democracy and AI.

</details>


### [431] [Fault Lines: Navigating Ethics and Responsible AI Where National Policy Meets Local Practice in Public Sector Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13039)
*Sitong Lyu,Shabnam Taghiyeva,Mohit Kukadia,Denis Newman-Griffis*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过英国特殊教育需求与残疾（SEND）领域的案例研究，采用17场半结构化访谈的主题分析，揭示了中央政策与地方实践之间在负责任AI实施中的五大挑战：影子AI使用与数据隐私风险、市场-政府不对称、劳动力准备不足、缺乏标准化定义与测量、以及人类问责缺口。研究发现，高风险决策场景加剧了责任、公平与人类监督的张力，表明原则性监管方法的局限性，并提出负责任的公共部门AI需要国家政策调整与地方机构能力、价值观和治理机制的结构性改革。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英国政府采取支持AI的立场以应对财政压力并改善公共服务，但如何将这一愿景转化为负责任的AI实践路径不明确。国家政策与地方执行之间存在知识和实践断层，亟需研究负责任AI在国家-地方接口处的解释与实施方式。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈（17场）的主题分析方法，访谈对象包括政策制定者、从业者和第三部门专业人士，以SEND领域为高风险的案例研究。

Result: 识别出地方当局面临的五大相互关联挑战：影子AI使用与数据隐私风险、市场-政府不对称、劳动力准备不足、缺乏标准化定义与测量、以及人类问责缺口。参与者提出了从加强数据保护框架到重新平衡市场-政府关系、提升劳动力能力等可操作步骤。

Conclusion: 负责任的公共部门AI需要同时进行国家政策调整和地方层面机构能力、价值观与治理机制的结构性改革。原则性监管方法在高风险决策场景中存在局限性。

Abstract: The UK government has adopted a pro-AI stance to help transform public service delivery in the face of severe financial pressures, but the path to translate this vision into responsible AI practice remains ill-defined. While UK policy is often set at the national level, local authorities are responsible for most public service delivery, and the rapid advance of AI-first narratives in the public sector is exposing fault lines in knowledge and practice at this national-local interface. This paper examines how responsible AI is interpreted and implemented at the interface between the UK's central government and local authorities, taking the high-stakes area of Special Educational Needs and Disabilities (SEND) as a case study. We present a thematic analysis of 17 semi-structured interviews with policymakers, practitioners, and third-sector professionals to identify barriers and enabling conditions for responsible AI where national policy meets local practice. We identify five interconnected challenges facing local authorities: shadow usage of AI and data privacy risks, market-government asymmetry in AI provision, insufficient workforce readiness, a lack of standardised definitions and measurements, and gaps in human accountability. For each, participants proposed actionable steps, from strengthening data protection frameworks and rebalancing the market-government relationship to enhancing workforce capacity. Our examination of SEND brings these challenges into sharper focus, showing how high-stakes decisions affecting vulnerable children and families intensify tensions around accountability, fairness, and human oversight, exposing the limits of a principle-based regulatory approach. We argue that responsible public sector AI requires both national policy adjustments and structural reforms to institutional capacity, values, and governance mechanisms at the local level.

</details>


### [432] ["Is This Not Enough?": Asymmetries in Institutional Accountability and Collective Sensemaking in the Case of Canada's Algorithmic Visa Triage System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13071)
*Dipto Das,Matthew Tamura,Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed,Shion Guha*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过分析加拿大移民局（IRCC）临时居民签证（TRV）分流系统的算法影响评估（AIA）以及Reddit上申请者的讨论，揭示了加拿大签证系统中算法问责制在制度层面和申请者体验层面的差异，识别出三种结构性不对称：认识论不对称、管辖权不对称和时间-关系不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在跨国移民背景下，公共部门算法治理系统中制度设计的问责机制与申请者实际感知之间的结构性鸿沟，尤其是现有制度披露框架未能捕捉的不对称体验。

Method: 采用混合方法：使用适用于公共部门的算法决策框架（ADMAPS）分析IRCC的AIA，同时对Reddit上的申请者讨论进行定性分析。

Result: 发现了三种不对称：1）认识论不对称——决策逻辑的可及性差异；2）管辖权不对称——地缘政治定位导致的暴露程度差异；3）时间-关系不对称——等待和不确定性体验的差异。

Conclusion: 算法治理系统在跨国移民中会产生结构性不对称，这些不对称无法被机构披露框架所捕捉；扩展ADMAPS框架可以解释问责制的不均匀转化。

Abstract: This paper examines how algorithmic accountability in Canada's visa system is articulated institutionally and experienced by applicants across borders. We analyzed Immigration, Refugees and Citizenship Canada (IRCC)'s Algorithmic Impact Assessment (AIA) for the temporary resident visa (TRV) triage system using the algorithmic decision-making adapted for the public sector (ADMAPS) framework and analyzed Reddit discussions among applicants using a mixed-methods approach. We show that while institutional artifacts emphasize transparency, procedural safeguards, and bounded impacts, applicants engage in collective sensemaking to interpret opaque decisions, often relying on peer knowledge amid uncertainty. We identify three asymmetries between how institutional accountability is structured and how people perceive the process: epistemic asymmetry in access to decision logic, jurisdictional asymmetry in exposure shaped by geopolitical positioning, and temporal--relational asymmetry in how waiting and uncertainty are experienced. We emphasize why it is important to shift attention from institutional design to the uneven distribution of experiences with public-sector algorithmic governance. Together, these contributions demonstrate how algorithmic governance systems in the context of transnational migration produce structured asymmetries not captured by institutional disclosure frameworks, and how extending ADMAPS can account for those uneven translations of accountability.

</details>
