### [433] [Structuring Transparency: Developing Domain-Specific Generative AI Declaration Frameworks in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13389)
*Nicholas Micallef,Olga Petrovska*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文针对高等教育中生成式AI（GenAI）使用声明过于笼统的问题，提出了一种任务特定的声明框架，将写作和编程评估中的GenAI使用细分为不同认知与发展阶段（如结构规划vs文本生成、代码改进vs代码生成），以促进学术诚信、AI素养和专业实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高等教育中现有的GenAI使用声明（如“我使用了GenAI”）过于泛化和二元化，无法捕捉不同学术任务中AI工具的细微应用，不利于维护学术诚信和培养学生AI素养。

Method: 基于已有的GenAI使用分类法，为计算机科学系设计了两种任务特定的声明结构——一种面向写作活动，一种面向编程评估，将AI使用按认知和发展阶段（如结构规划、文本内容生成、代码改进、代码生成）进行分类。

Result: 提出了一个包含两种任务特定声明结构的设计工件和相应立场，认为需要超越二元声明，转向领域特定的透明披露。

Conclusion: 该任务特定、领域特定的声明框架有助于鼓励学生反思自身学习过程，明确可接受帮助与学术不端的界限，并为计算机科学及其他学科更诚实的评估奠定基础，帮助学生适应未来职业中记录GenAI工作流程的要求。

Abstract: As Generative AI (GenAI) disrupts higher education, institutions increasingly require students to declare AI use. However, generic, binary declarations (e.g., "I used GenAI") fail to capture the nuanced application of these tools in different academic tasks. Establishing transparency is key to protecting academic integrity, promoting AI literacy, and shifting the focus from policing to professional practice. In response, this paper contributes a design artefact and an accompanying position: a framework of two task-specific declaration structures, one for writing-focused activities and one for coding assessments, developed for a Computer Science department on the basis of an existing taxonomy of GenAI usage, together with an argument that task-specific disclosure is needed to move beyond binary declarations. By categorising AI usage across specific cognitive and developmental stages, such as structural planning vs. Textual Content Generation, or code improvement vs. code generation, the framework encourages students to reflect on their own learning process and clarifies the boundary between acceptable assistance and academic misconduct. We propose this domain-specific approach as a foundation for fostering more honest assessment in Computer Science and other disciplines, aiming to better prepare students for professional environments where documenting GenAI workflows might be an essential job requirement.

</details>


### [434] [Exploring Systems-Thinking Approaches to Loss of Control Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13474)
*Aurelio Carlucci,Sean P. Fillingham,James Walpole,Jakub Kryś*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了内部部署的智能体AI系统（用于编码和研究）所引发的社会技术控制问题，指出其风险超越了模型行为本身。作者将“内部部署失控”定义为无法可靠地约束、审计、撤销或阻止AI对代码、基础设施、评估或部署流程的修改，从而及时防止严重的组织或社会危害。通过运用STECA、STPA和FRAM三种系统安全方法分析一个基于公开材料重建的前沿实验室编码智能体场景，研究发现：已发表框架可能使治理责任和反馈循环在外部无法验证；监测与干预的延迟会使原本有效的控制措施失效；以及常规操作变化会逐渐削弱安全防护的校准性和独立性。作者认为，前沿AI风险管理应将模型层面的评估与系统级危险分析和持续运营保障相结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI风险管理主要关注模型行为层面的评估，但忽略了内部部署智能体系统作为一个社会技术系统所面临的更广泛控制问题。作者希望探究已建立的系统安全方法能否识别出模型级评估可能遗漏的风险。

Method: 从公开材料重建一个通用的前沿实验室编码智能体场景，并应用STECA（社会技术系统安全分析）、STPA（系统理论过程分析）和FRAM（功能共振分析方法）三种系统安全方法进行分析。

Result: 三种方法得出了互补的发现：（1）现有框架可能使治理责任和反馈循环无法在外部验证；（2）监测和干预的延迟会使原本有效的控制措施失效；（3）常规操作的可变性会逐渐削弱安全防护的校准性和独立性。

Conclusion: 前沿AI风险管理应将模型层面的评估与系统级危险分析和持续运营保障相结合，以跟踪控制措施是否随时间保持有效。

Abstract: Internal deployment of agentic AI systems for coding and research creates a sociotechnical control problem that extends beyond model behaviour. We treat internal-deployment Loss of Control as the inability to reliably constrain, audit, reverse, or halt AI-mediated changes to code, infrastructure, evaluation, or deployment processes in time to prevent serious organisational or societal harms. We ask whether established systems-safety methods can identify risks that model-level evaluations may miss. Using a generic frontier-lab coding-agent scenario reconstructed from public materials, we apply STECA, STPA, and FRAM. The analyses surface complementary findings: published frameworks can leave governance responsibilities and feedback loops externally unverifiable; delays in monitoring and intervention can make otherwise valid control actions ineffective; and routine operational variability can gradually erode the calibration and independence of safeguards. We argue that frontier-AI risk management should pair model-focused evaluations with systems-level hazard analysis and operational assurance that tracks whether controls remain effective over time.

</details>


### [435] [Digital Twin-Based Simulation for Predictive Decision-Making in Waterway Logistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13492)
*Matthijs Jansen op de Haar,Daniel Frutos Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究了数字孪生技术在内河航道网络货运路径规划中的应用，通过预测性决策应对不确定水位条件。相比传统反应式方法，预测模型在70%-100%预测精度下显著降低运营成本和模态转换，平均减少28.3%燃油成本。研究基于六次专家访谈识别三种应急情景，并在时间切片仿真环境中验证了DT方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法未能充分考虑气候变化导致的水位波动加剧，往往依赖反应式应对措施，导致更高的运营成本和排放（如被迫转向公路运输），亟需更主动的预测性决策方法。

Method: 通过六次领域专家访谈识别三种常见的应急情景及对应操作响应，将这些情景纳入时间切片仿真环境，比较数字孪生驱动的预测性决策与反应式方法的性能差异。

Result: 预测模型在70%-100%预测精度范围内显著降低运营成本和模态转换；平均减少28.3%燃油相关成本（通过减少船舶总航行距离实现）；极端条件下仍有效。

Conclusion: 数字孪生赋能的预测性方法在内河航道货运路径规划中优于传统反应式方法，具有实际应用价值，经领域专家评估确认了其实践相关性和适用性。

Abstract: This paper investigates the potential of a Digital Twin (DT) for freight routing across inland waterway networks under uncertain water-level conditions. Existing approaches insufficiently account for increasing climate-induced volatility in water levels, which often result in higher operational costs and emissions due to the need for more expensive transport alternatives, such as road transport. These existing methods often rely on reactive countermeasures to remain resilient.
  To address these limitations, six interviews with experts in domains related to inland shipping were conducted to identify three common contingency scenarios and appropriate operational responses. These scenarios were subsequently incorporated into a time-sliced simulation environment in which predictive decision-making, enabled by a DT environment, was compared against reactive approaches. The results demonstrate that predictive modeling substantially reduces operational costs and modal shifts at prediction accuracies between 70% and 100%, despite extreme conditions. In addition, the predictive model achieves an average 28.3% reduction in fuel-related costs by reducing the total distance ships travel. The simulation outcomes were evaluated together with domain experts to assess the practical relevance and applicability of the proposed DT-enabled approach.

</details>


### [436] [The Tone of Awareness: Topic, Sentiment, and Toxicity Maps During Mental Health Month on TikTok](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13581)
*Henrique Ferraz de Arruda,Andreia Sofia Teixeira,Pranay Gundala Reddy,Anindya Mondal,Kleber Andrade Oliveira,Filipi Nascimento Silva*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过分析2023和2024年心理健康意识月期间TikTok上28,341个视频和80,130条评论，利用BERTopic提取主题、XLM-T分析情感、Detoxify检测毒性，发现心理健康话题的基调在不同主题和年份间存在差异：视频情感偏负面，评论情感更混合或正面；毒性整体中位数低但在特定主题的评论中存在长尾异常值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管TikTok的使用引发了对心理健康的担忧，但关于创作者如何构建相关内容以及受众如何接收这些内容的机制尚不清楚。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统性地分析TikTok上心理健康话语的主题、情感和毒性特征。

Method: 通过TikTok Research API收集2023和2024年心理健康意识月（5月）的28,341个视频和80,130条评论；使用BERTopic和log-odds关键词从视频文本中提取主题；分别对视频转录和评论采用XLM-T进行情感分析、Detoxify进行毒性检测，区分内容生产和受众接受两个层面。

Result: 发现稳定的跨年重复主题（临床状况、情感披露、自我关怀、宣传活动等），但参与度高度集中于少数主题。视频情感在情绪化主题上多为负面，而评论倾向于更混合或积极的极性（尤其是自杀预防主题）。毒性中位数整体较低，但评论中呈现更长尾的异常值，集中在"Duet""Suicide Prevention""Psychisch"等特定主题。

Conclusion: 本文提供了TikTok心理健康意识月活动中话语的主题级分解，揭示了内容生产与受众接受之间的情感和毒性差异，为理解社交媒体心理健康传播的复杂动态提供了数据驱动的见解。

Abstract: Despite raising concerns about the mental health effects associated with the usage of TikTok, little is known about how related content is framed by creators and received by audiences. We collect the content of 28,341 TikTok videos and 80,130 comments from Mental Health Awareness Month (May) in 2023 and 2024 via the TikTok Research API, and study how the tone of awareness varies across topics and years. We characterize "tone" as the emotional and interpersonal framing of mental health discourse, operationalized through sentiment and toxicity measures. We extract topics from video text using BERTopic and log-odds keywords, then quantify topic-conditioned sentiment (XLM-T) and toxicity (Detoxify) separately for video transcriptions and comments. Sentiment captures the affective valence of content, while toxicity reflects the presence of harmful or abusive language. We find a stable set of recurring themes across years, spanning clinical conditions, emotional disclosure, self-care, and campaign-oriented content, with engagement highly skewed toward a small subset of topics. All sentiment and toxicity analyses are computed separately for video content and comments, allowing us to distinguish between content production and audience reception. Sentiment in videos is often negative for emotionally charged topics, while comments tend to shift toward more mixed or positive polarity, especially for suicide prevention. Toxicity is low in median overall, but exhibits longer-tailed outliers in comments than in videos that are more pronounced in comments and concentrated in specific topics (e.g., "Duet", "Suicide Prevention", and "Psychisch"). Overall, our results provide a topic-level decomposition of mental health discourse on TikTok during awareness-month campaigns.

</details>


### [437] [C-QUERI: Congressional Questions, Exchanges, and Responses in Institutions Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21548)
*Manjari Rudra,Daniel Magleby,Sujoy Sikdar*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文构建了美国国会听证会问答对数据集（第108-117届国会），通过分析发现提问者的党派归属可仅从问题特征中预测，揭示了跨党派提问策略的系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治采访和听证会中的提问常服务于推进党派叙事和塑造公众认知等战略目的，但缺乏大规模数据集来系统研究这种话语策略。

Method: 开发了从非结构化听证会转录文本中提取问答对的流水线，构建了涵盖第108-117届国会的委员会听证会数据集。

Result: 发现提问者的党派 affiliation 可从其问题本身进行预测，表明不同党派在提问策略上存在系统性差异。

Conclusion: 该数据集和方法不仅推进了国会政治研究，还为分析采访类场景中的问答行为提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Questions in political interviews and hearings serve strategic purposes beyond information gathering including advancing partisan narratives and shaping public perceptions. However, these strategic aspects remain understudied due to the lack of large-scale datasets for studying such discourse. Congressional hearings provide an especially rich and tractable site for studying political questioning: Interactions are structured by formal rules, witnesses are obliged to respond, and members with different political affiliations are guaranteed opportunities to ask questions, enabling comparisons of behaviors across the political spectrum.
  We develop a pipeline to extract question-answer pairs from unstructured hearing transcripts and construct a novel dataset of committee hearings from the 108th--117th Congress. Our analysis reveals systematic differences in questioning strategies across parties, by showing the party affiliation of questioners can be predicted from their questions alone. Our dataset and methods not only advance the study of congressional politics, but also provide a general framework for analyzing question-answering across interview-like settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [438] [Graph Reduction in Multirelational Networks: A Spreading-Oriented Reduction Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12581)
*Mateusz Stolarski,Michał Czuba,Piotr Bielak,Piotr Bródka*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出SORB基准框架，系统评估图缩减对影响力最大化任务的影响，发现稀疏化在单层网络中有效，但在多层网络中会导致排名严重退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实网络存在不完整、噪声和动态演化特性，直接分析计算代价高。虽然影响力最大化已被广泛研究，但图缩减作为预处理步骤对其准确性的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建开源标准化框架SORB，在多种真实网络（含单层和多层结构）上集成图缩减（稀疏化与粗化）评估流程，系统比较不同缩减策略对IM任务性能的影响。

Result: 稀疏化在单层网络上能保持种子集质量；而扁平化多层网络无论采用何种缩减策略，均表现出系统性的排名退化。

Conclusion: 图缩减对IM的影响强烈依赖于网络类型（单层 vs. 多关系）和下游任务指标（Gain@k vs. AUC_cutoff），需采用缩减感知的多任务评估范式研究复杂网络中的传播过程。

Abstract: Real-world networks are inherently incomplete, noisy, and dynamically evolving, making it difficult to capture all actors and their relationships. Their scale often renders direct analysis computationally demanding. While influence maximisation (IM) has been widely studied, the role of graph reduction as a preprocessing step, and its impact on IM accuracy, remains underexplored. In this work, we introduce the Spreading-Oriented Reduction Benchmark (SORB), an open-source, standardised framework for systematically evaluating IM models across diverse task settings. SORB provides an extensible pipeline operating on a representative collection of real-world networks, including single- and multilayer structures, and accounts for graph reduction directly into the evaluation process. This design shifts the focus from analysing IM algorithms in isolation to quantifying how graph reduction alters predictive performance. Using SORB, we study the effects of sparsification and coarsening across multiple IM scenarios. Our results show that the impact of reduction is strongly dependent on both the network type (single-layer vs. multirelational) and the downstream task ($Gain@k$ vs. $\mathrm{AUC}_{\mathrm{cutoff}}$): sparsification preserves seed set quality on single-layer networks, whereas flattened multilayer networks exhibit systematic ranking degradation regardless of reduction strategy. These findings highlight the importance of reduction-aware, multi-task evaluation when studying spreading processes in complex networks.

</details>


### [439] [Orchestrating the Twin Transition in Multinational Corporations: Technology Roadmapping for Green and Digital Global Business Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12787)
*Han-Teng Liao,Karen Ang*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文整合技术路线图与ITU创新生态系统工具，通过文献计量分析揭示GBS从流程自动化向"可持续智能"的演进，提出GBS作为连接宏观压力与利基创新的"运营气闸"的框架，并识别出波兰、葡萄牙、马来西亚等"中等强国"枢纽在分裂地缘政治中为全球价值链提供第三条路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨国企业面临数字化效率与环境责任的双重压力，亟需一个能够协调绿色转型与数字化转型（双重转型）的理论框架，而已有的全球商业服务（GBS）作为"活实验室"的潜力尚未得到系统性挖掘。

Method: 采用文献计量分析方法识别研究集群，将技术路线图（TRM）与国际电信联盟（ITU）以ICT为核心的创新生态系统工具包进行整合，并将聚类结果映射到利益相关者画布上。

Result: 发现GBS经历了从基础流程自动化到"可持续智能"的演化转变；识别出GBS单元作为"运营气闸"的中介角色；定位了波兰、葡萄牙、马来西亚等"中等强国"枢纽在避开中等收入陷阱、为全球价值链提供"第三条道路"方面的作用。

Conclusion: 提供了一个数据驱动的设计框架，帮助领导者和创业支持网络协调人才与供应链流动，深化了对工业5.0的理解，明确了GBS在驾驭动荡、多极化数字经济中的核心机制地位。

Abstract: Global Business Services (GBS) have emerged as a "living laboratory" for the Twin Transition of Green and Digital Transformation, as multinational corporations (MNCs) face increasing pressure to harmonize digital efficiency with environmental stewardship. Aiming to derive a socio-technical framework, this paper synthesizes Technology Roadmapping (TRM) with the International Telecommunication Union (ITU) ICT-centric innovation ecosystem toolkit. A bibliometric analysis of research clusters reveals an evolutionary shift from basic process automation toward "Sustainable Intelligence," identifying the GBS unit as a central "operational airlock" that mediates between landscape pressures -- such as the EU's dual mandate and Carbon Border Adjustment Mechanisms -- and niche innovations in AI-native workflows. The study further maps these clusters onto a stakeholder engagement canvas, highlighting how resilient "Middle Power" hubs in Poland, Portugal, and Malaysia are bypassing the middle-income trap to provide a "third way" for global value chains amidst a bifurcated geopolitical cloud. The results offer a data-driven design approach for leaders and entrepreneurial support networks to orchestrate talent and supply chain flows, thereby enriching the conceptual understanding of Industry 5.0 and the role of GBS as a primary mechanism for navigating a volatile, multipolar digital economy.

</details>


### [440] [To Share or Not to Share: Orchestrating Trustworthy Data in Global Value Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12788)
*Han-Teng Liao,Chang-Yi Kao*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于IDSA框架的RegTech参考架构，通过可信环境遥测技术解决欧盟CBAM下半导体-石化产业链的监管透明度与数据主权矛盾，并引入前瞻性路线图方法将上游物理脆弱性转化为循环负反馈回路。以台北-槟城科技走廊为例，展示了主权数据交换驱动的数字产品护照（DPP）如何推动全球商业服务（GBS）能力需求，并探讨了Agentic AI与金融科技绿色融资的集成方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟碳边境调节机制（CBAM）即将实施，全球半导体价值链面临监管透明度与数据主权之间的结构性张力，亟需一种既能满足强制性碳合规要求又能维护企业数据主权的技术架构。

Method: 采用国际数据空间（IDSA）框架设计RegTech参考架构，区分CBAM强制性要求与SBTi自愿性框架，并纳入SSbD框架的叠加复杂性；提出前瞻性路线图方法，将上游物理脆弱性映射为循环负反馈回路；以台北-槟城科技走廊为案例，通过主权数据交换实现数字产品护照（DPP）。

Result: 构建了可扩展的RegTech架构蓝图，实现了从线性技术栈到循环反馈系统的范式转变；验证了主权数据交换在DPP驱动GBS能力需求中的可行性；提出了Agentic AI自主合规与FinTech绿色融资的集成方案。

Conclusion: 该架构为全球产业集群提供了实现主权化、可持续化和透明化价值链的可扩展蓝图，有效调和了CBAM监管要求与数据主权保护之间的矛盾。

Abstract: As the EU Carbon Border Adjustment Mechanism (CBAM) approaches, the global semiconductor value chain faces growing structural tensions between regulatory transparency and data sovereignty. This article proposes a RegTech reference architecture using the International Data Spaces (IDSA) framework to orchestrate trustworthy environmental telemetry across the semiconductor-petrochemical nexus. The framework distinguishes the mandatory CBAM requirements from voluntary Science Based Targets initiative (SBTi) frameworks, while addressing the additive complexities of the Safe-and-Sustainable-by-Design (SSbD) framework. Moving beyond standard linear technology stacks, we introduce a prospective roadmapping methodology that transforms upstream physical vulnerabilities into circular, negative feedback loops. Focusing on the Taipei and Penang technology corridor, the article details how sovereign data exchange enables Digital Product Passports (DPPs) to drive Global Business Services (GBSs) capability demands. Finally, we discuss the integration of Agentic AI for autonomous compliance and FinTech green financing, providing a scalable blueprint for global industrial clusters to achieve sovereign, sustainable, and transparent value chains.

</details>


### [441] [MIDSim: Simulating Multi-Channel Information Diffusion in Social Media with LLM-Powered Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13140)
*Lexi Liu,Qi Cao,Yuanhao Liu,Huawei Shen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于大语言模型的多智能体系统，用于模拟社交媒体中多通道信息扩散，同时考虑社交链路和算法推荐渠道，并在三个真实平台数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息扩散研究主要关注社交链路传播，忽略了推荐系统等平台算法提供的其他扩散通道，也无法捕捉用户行为的复杂性。

Method: 构建LLM驱动的多智能体系统，用大语言模型实例化个性化用户代理，扩散过程联合建模社交曝光流和算法曝光流。

Result: 在微博、小红书、Twitter三个真实扩散事件数据集上，框架能够真实模拟宏观扩散现象并生成多样化的评论内容，显著优于基线模型。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效弥补了现有扩散模型忽视算法渠道和用户行为复杂性的不足，在多渠道信息扩散模拟中表现卓越。

Abstract: Information diffusion in social media shapes public opinion and collective behavior, making its modeling and simulation an important research problem. Existing studies have investigated information diffusion through epidemic-based, cascade-based, and point process models. However, they predominantly focus on diffusion through social links, overlooking other diffusion channels enabled by platform algorithms (e.g., recommender systems) and failing to capture user behavioral complexity. To address these limitations, we propose an LLM-powered multi-agent system for simulating multi-channel information diffusion, where large language models instantiate personalized user agents and the diffusion process jointly models social and algorithmic exposure streams. We further construct three real-world diffusion dataset spanning Sina Weibo, RedNote, and Twitter, containing diffusion records, user profiles, historical posts, and social relationships. Experimental results on real diffusion events show that our proposed framework realistically simulate macro diffusion phenomenon and generate diverse comment content, significantly outperforming baselines.

</details>


### [442] [Ollivier-Ricci curvature in cycle overlap mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03317)
*Zexian Zhou,Bo Jiao*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最优传输原理的Ollivier-Ricci曲率近似计算方法CCOM，通过利用包含目标边的3-5阶环结构来最小化运输距离，并采用贪心剪枝算法实现。该方法在保持低时间消耗的同时，显著提高了大规模无标度网络上曲率计算的准确性，并在社区检测任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Ollivier-Ricci曲率计算在大规模无标度图上存在计算复杂度高或误差大的问题，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出最优传输原理，利用包含目标边(x,y)的3、4、5阶环来最小化运输距离；设计CCOM方法，采用贪心和剪枝算法近似实现最优传输原理。

Result: 理论和实验均验证了CCOM在真实网络上能以低时间消耗显著提升曲率计算准确性；在基于曲率的社区检测任务中，CCOM在大型无标度图上表现优于基线近似方法。

Conclusion: CCOM是一种高效且准确的Ollivier-Ricci曲率近似计算方法，适用于大规模无标度图，在社区检测等下游任务中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Ollivier-Ricci curvature of an edge (x,y) is defined by comparing the distance taken to transport from neighbors of x to neighbors of y. It is a structural measure that has been studied in many fields such as community detection and deep neural networks. However, high computational complexity or error limits its application in large scale-free graphs. This paper proposes an optimal transport principle to minimize the distance by 3,4,5-cycles that include the edge (x,y), and designs a curvature calculation approach named Curvature in Cycle Overlap Mode (CCOM). In this approach, a greedy and pruning algorithm is proposed to approximate the optimal transport principle. We theoretically and experimentally verified that our approach CCOM can significantly improve the accuracy of the curvature on real-world networks with low time consumption. In addition, we compared CCOM with baseline approximation approaches in community detection tasks using the same curvature-based framework, and experimentally confirmed the effectiveness of CCOM on large scale-free graphs.
